Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(new)
Vol.6

Chapter 2894
Going too far
As the human girl saw the Liyuan Clan act like this, her face dimmed.
She feared that she wouldn't be able to leave today.
She was already extremely grateful that Luo Zheng was willing to help her, but 200,000 soul pills was just too much.
"Forget it." The little girl clenched her teeth and said in a barely audible voice, "I'll go to the Liyuan Clan and do hard labor …"
Although she knew that hard labor didn't bode well for her, she had no other choice.
At this time, Luo Zheng said, "200,000 soul pills, I'll pay!"
These Liyuan Clan members didn't know what to say. In order to prevent them from changing their minds, he immediately nodded to Ling Shuang.
Ling Shuang didn't say anything, but when Luo Zheng asked for help, she immediately took out her spatial ring and paid 100,000 soul pills to the Liyuan Clan members.
The human girl's eyes widened. She was shocked, but her face was also filled with gratitude.
The asking price of two thousand Soul Cores soared to two hundred thousand. In the end, Luo Zheng paid the price.
These Liyuan Clan members didn't think that Luo Zheng would really take out 200,000 soul pills. If they continued to make things difficult for them, it would be too embarrassing. Thus, the leader of the Liyuan Clan members waved his hand and said, "Let's go!"
The human girl walked in front of Luo Zheng and said, "I'll wait for you at the 12th Layer of Heaven. These soul pills … I, I'll definitely return them to you!"
In truth, for a Yang Soul that could step into the 12th or 13th Layer of Heaven, 200,000 soul pills wasn't too much.
However, this little girl's clan had long since declined. It would be difficult for her to gather 200,000 soul pills, so she was a bit hesitant.
"Let's go," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
No one knew each other on the other side of the river.
Even if she went to the 12th Layer of Heaven, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to find her. Luo Zheng didn't care whether or not she would return these soul pills.
The human girl nodded and walked towards the star ring.
Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang waited in line for a while before it was finally their turn.
According to their previous agreement, Luo Zheng also paid 100,000 soul pills.
When it was Ling Shuang's turn, the Liyuan Clan member pointed at her and said, "You, one million soul pills."
Ling Shuang raised her eyebrows and asked, "Why?"
"That's because you paid for his 200,000 soul pills," said the Liyuan clansman with a smile. "If you can't afford it, please head over there!"
He gestured and pointed to the west.
"Buzz …"
Luo Zheng's Sun Soul emitted an irrepressible anger, and a powerful soul aura immediately spread out.
Even the other outsiders in the queue felt that the Liyuan Tribe was going too far.
"The Liyuan Clan is such a bully!"
"Who told them to meddle in other people's business?"
"To pass through the Eleventh Heaven, even a dragon has to coil up, and a tiger has to crouch down …"
The other races discussed in low voices. They also knew that this human couple was going to be in trouble.
"What? Not convinced? "
The members of the Liyuan Tribe looked at Luo Zheng indifferently, not taking him seriously at all.
No one dared to make a move against a member of the Li Yuan Clan in the Eleventh Heaven!
Ling Shuang could feel Luo Zheng's angry aura. She said, "I don't have any Million Soul pills. Can I use a soul crystal instead?"
Upon hearing the mention of soul crystals, the eyes of the Liyuan Tribe members lit up.
No matter which super force it was, soul crystals were always in high demand. Even if one had Soul pills and God crystals, it was very difficult to exchange for a soul crystal.
"That depends on how many soul crystals you're willing to hand over, hehe …" The Liyuan Tribe member laughed.
"One finger." Ling Shuang bluntly replied.
"Deal." The Liyuan Tribe member was also very straightforward.
There weren't many opportunities to obtain soul crystals. One finger of soul crystals was much more valuable than Million Soul pills. Soul pills could be slowly accumulated, but soul crystals simply couldn't be bought anywhere.
Ling Shuang took out a soul crystal from her spatial ring and handed it over to the Liyuan Tribe member.
As the other outsiders saw this soul crystal, their eyes lit up. This was a soul crystal, and she was using it as a toll to pass through the Eleventh Heaven? Which human family did this woman come from? How could she be so generous?
As they sighed, they secretly cursed the Liyuan Tribe members for being black-hearted. From the looks of it, the Liyuan Tribe would only become more and more black-hearted in the future!
"Let's go." Ling Shuang pulled on Luo Zheng, but she didn't move him.
As the Liyuan Tribe member saw that Luo Zheng wasn't willing to leave, he chuckled and said, "Human boy, if you don't want to leave, then you can stay here forever."
"Don't be angry, let's go!" Ling Shuang anxiously said.
Staying here would only lead to more trouble, so Ling Shuang was naturally a bit anxious.
In the end, Luo Zheng moved his feet and followed Ling Shuang towards the star ring. The two Yang souls entered the star ring, and with a slight twist, they turned into beams of light that shot towards the Twelfth Heaven.
The little human girl was still waiting at the Twelfth Heaven, staring at the star ring.
She owed Luo Zheng such a great favor, so she naturally had to find a way to repay it. It was impossible for her not to know the name of her benefactor.
Weng …
As the two beams of light descended, Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang's Yang souls arrived at the Twelfth Heaven.
"Mm, benefactor …"
The little human girl remembered Luo Zheng's aura and walked towards him.
However, Luo Zheng didn't give her a chance to speak. He only said to Ling Shuang, "Wait for me here …" Then, he left the other side.
Ling Shuang only had time to warn him to be careful before Luo Zheng's figure disappeared.
She shook her head slightly. If the Liyuan Tribe member had only asked for two hundred thousand Soul Pills, then she probably would have tolerated this. However, when she wanted to pass, they actually asked for a million Soul Pills. This was truly too much. If it weren't for the fact that she still had soul crystals, wouldn't she have been forced to stay at the Twelfth Heaven?
When the little human girl saw how anxious Luo Zheng was, she didn't know what to do. She stood in place for a while, then asked, "This … big sister, where did benefactor go?"
Ling Shuang replied, "He left temporarily."
She could imagine that before long, the Twelfth Heaven would probably be turned upside down by Luo Zheng.
"Oh." The little human girl still stood in place. "Then I'll wait for him here."
"Why are you waiting for him?" Ling Shuang asked.
"I want to know benefactor's name so that I can return the Soul Pills to him in the future," the little girl said.
Ling Shuang looked at her, then smiled and said, "You can go now. You don't need to return the Soul Pills."
Two thousand Soul Pills were naturally nothing. As for the extra million Soul Pills, it was completely because of the Liyuan Tribe. It had nothing to do with this little human girl.
Ling Shuang's personality was very magnanimous. She naturally wouldn't blame the little girl for the problem.
"No! I must return these Soul Pills! "The little girl was unexpectedly persistent.
Ling Shuang finally became impatient. She said word by word, "I paid for these Soul Pills. I said that you don't need to return them. You can go now!"

Chapter 2895
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
A Hundredfold Revenge
When Luo Zheng's body had just returned to the temple of the Heavenly Palace, he saw the disciples of the Heavenly Palace guarding at the gate of the temple solemnly.
Apparently, a strong enemy had invaded the Heavenly Palace.
"What's wrong?"
Luo Zheng stepped forward and asked a disciple of the Heavenly Palace.
Although these disciples of the Heavenly Palace didn't know the identity of Luo Zheng's body, they knew he was on the Heavenly Palace's side.
"You, you weren't here just now … but something big happened!" The disciple of the Heavenly Palace said.
"What big thing? Did the You Xiong Clan come to make trouble again? "Luo Zheng asked.
The You Xiong Clan had suffered a great loss. They should have learned their lesson. It was impossible for them to make trouble for the Heavenly Palace at this time. Could it be that some other clans had invaded the Heavenly Palace?
The disciple of the Heavenly Palace shook his head and said, "Someone did come to make trouble, but it was not the You Xiong Clan … I don't know where this fellow came from. He could devour the souls of others directly!"
"Devour souls directly?" Luo Zheng was also surprised.
"Yes!" The disciple of the Heavenly Palace nodded and said, "That guy came to ask for the Soul Crystal directly. We didn't give it to him. Then one of us let him devour his soul directly. After that, we retreated to the temple."
The temple was full of powerful restrictive spells. Except for Luo Zheng's body, which could come and go freely, even the most powerful souls would be burned into ashes.
"Then that guy left?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"No," the disciple of the Heavenly Palace shook his head, "he ran to the You Xiong Clan. I guess he also went to ask for the Soul Crystal …"
How could it be so easy to get the Soul Crystal?
Many elite disciples of the You Xiong Clan were busy repairing the temple. Without the protection of the restrictive spells, they suffered a great loss. Seven or eight of their clansmen were devoured at once. The rest of the clansmen of the You Xiong Clan fled to the other side when they saw the situation was not good.
That guy went to the ruins of the You Xiong Clan's temple to pick up the treasures.
However, the temple had already been searched by Luo Zheng. Other important things had been taken away by the You Xiong Clan. Of course, that guy didn't get anything.
According to that guy's character, he wouldn't give up so easily. Then he rushed toward the Divine Farmer Clan's Divine Temple.
The Divine Farmer Clan had taken precautions a long time ago, and they lured that person into the temple and almost killed that person. But that person was quite alert, and he managed to escape from the temple at the critical moment, and he managed to avoid such a great calamity.
After listening to the Heavenly Palace disciple, Luo Zheng asked, "Where is that person now?"
"It seems that he has already left the Nirvana Realm. Although his devouring technique is terrifying, he is alone and his Soul Cultivation is not very strong. After the You Xiong Clan realized what had happened, they joined forces with the Divine Farmer Clan and sent several Indigo Souls to chase after him. However, he managed to escape," the disciple of the Heaven Palace said.
"The ability to devour souls is really full of wonders in this world …" Luo Zheng muttered in his heart.
If he wasn't afraid of the soul's backlash, this ability would be extremely heaven-defying.
This person's Yang Soul cultivation might not be very strong now, but as long as he swallowed it bit by bit, his cultivation speed would probably not be much slower than absorbing Soul Crystals. It was unknown how this person came to possess such an ability.
But the good news was that this guy wasn't from the You Xiong Clan or the Divine Farmer Clan, and now he had provoked these two clans …
After learning about this, Luo Zheng left the temple and went straight to the Star Ring. Ling Shuang was still waiting for him at the 12th Layer of Heaven.
During the process of ascension, Luo Zheng didn't stop. In less than an hour, he had ascended to the 10th Layer of Heaven and stepped into the 11th Layer of Heaven.
On the square of the 11th Layer of Heaven, several Liyuan Clan members were standing.
They were responsible for driving the ascended Yang Souls to the other side to line up.
With a faint flash of light, Luo Zheng's body appeared in the center of the square.
"This way …" The Liyuan Clan member shouted at Luo Zheng and was about to open his mouth to drive Luo Zheng over. But when he opened his eyes wide, he was stunned and his face was full of shock. "This, this … body?"
Apparently, the Liyuan Clan member had never seen such a situation before.
Luo Zheng glanced indifferently at the Liyuan Clan member, then walked forward confidently.
Whether it was the other Liyuan Clan members or the Outsiders in line, when they saw Luo Zheng's body swagger in front of them, their eyes were somewhat dazed.
"What's going on?"
"This guy doesn't seem to be a Yang Soul …"
"What do you mean not a Yang Soul? That's just a body! How did he get to the other shore? "
"Am I seeing a ghost?!"
In the mortal world, when one's soul left one's body, it was called seeing a ghost, but when these souls saw a body, their faces looked like they had seen a ghost.
Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't line up here. He walked past the line and arrived in front of the Liyuan Clan members.
"This … friend, what business do you have with us?" the Liyuan Clan member in the lead asked.
These Liyuan Clan members had always been arrogant and domineering in the 12th Layer of Heaven, so when faced with Luo Zheng's body, their attitude was already extremely polite.
"The soul pills you took just now will be returned a hundredfold. One million soul pills and one hundred million soul pills. Otherwise, today's matter will be difficult to resolve," Luo Zheng said in a low voice.
When the Outsiders in line heard this, they were all somewhat puzzled. They didn't know what had happened, but the meaning in Luo Zheng's words was very clear. He was here to extort the Liyuan Clan!
In the 12th Layer of Heaven, only the Liyuan Clan would extort other clans.
No matter which superpower or powerful clan, the Liyuan Clan would take advantage of them. Today, there was actually someone who dared to extort the Liyuan Clan. This was also new.
The Liyuan Clan members instantly understood that the person in front of them was the one who had just paid the one million soul pills!
The Liyuan Clan leader was silent for a moment, then said, "Friend, just now, we Liyuan Clan had eyes but failed to recognize Mount Tai. We will return the soul crystal and the two hundred thousand soul pills you gave us! Lang Du, take out the soul crystal. "
These few people from the Liyuan Tribe did not have a high status in the Liyuan Tribe. Blackmailing Luo Zheng and the others earlier was already an act of insubordination. If the elders of the Liyuan Tribe knew that they had offended a person who had entered the Nirvana Realm physically, they would probably be punished.
Therefore, they chose to keep the peace and wanted to return the soul crystal and soul pills.
When the Outsiders in line saw the Liyuan Clan's reaction, they were secretly pleased.
The Liyuan Clan, who had always been incomparably unyielding, also had a time when they would admit defeat?
However, Luo Zheng stretched out a finger and said, "One hundred million soul pills, or one hundred soul crystals."
When these words came out, the expressions of all the Liyuan Clan members changed slightly.
A trace of fear also appeared in the hearts of the Outsiders. This kid really dared to ask for it! From the looks of it, something big was going to happen today.
"Kid, we Liyuan Clan have more or less returned the full amount. We'll treat the one hundred million soul pills as a joke. Lang Du, give him the soul crystal," said the Liyuan Clan leader.
When the Liyuan Clan member called Lang Du handed over the soul crystal, Luo Zheng grabbed Lang Du's hand and crushed the soul crystal!
The pure soul crystal splattered everywhere, some of it entering the Outsiders' Yang Soul bodies. These Outsiders naturally enjoyed it.
Luo Zheng's body didn't have a spatial ring, so he couldn't store the soul crystal. He also didn't plan on taking this soul crystal.
When the Liyuan Clan members saw Luo Zheng waste the soul crystal like this, they all felt their hearts ache.

Chapter 2896
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Abyss Spirit Clan
As soon as Luo Zheng said this, the surroundings immediately fell silent.
Most of the members of the Mysterious Clan thought Luo Zheng was crazy.
In the eyes of the Liyuan Clan, Luo Zheng had come to ask for 100 million Soul Elixirs. Now he was threatening to raze the Liyuan Clan to the ground.
There was a human among the members of the Mysterious Clan. He was from the Divine Farmer Clan.
He had also come from the Fourth Heaven to the Thirteenth Heaven. However, he hadn't been in the Nirvana Realm for long and hadn't seen Luo Zheng demolish the temple.
But in the Divine Farmer Clan, he had heard about what had happened in the Fourth Heaven. Naturally, he had heard about Luo Zheng's "body."
"I didn't expect this guy to come all the way to the Eleventh Heaven. I'm afraid the Liyuan Clan has offended him. Is he planning to make trouble for the Liyuan Clan?"
The Liyuan Clan was an extremely powerful Mysterious Clan. Even the You Xiong Clan didn't dare to provoke them.
There would be a good show today.
When the leader of the Liyuan Clan heard Luo Zheng's arrogant words, he raised his head and laughed. "You? You think you can raze the Liyuan Clan to the ground? Ha, ha, ha, ha, ha … What a joke! "
The Liyuan Clan had wanted to settle the matter with compensation. After all, Luo Zheng's body had reached the Nirvana Realm. It was indeed a special case. They didn't want to cause too much trouble.
If this matter got out of hand and the Liyuan Clan asked for a sky-high toll, it would affect the reputation of their clan.
Now that they couldn't settle the matter peacefully, the attitude of the Liyuan Clan changed.
"It's really amazing for your body to reach the Nirvana Realm. If it were other clans, they wouldn't be able to do anything to you. But the Liyuan Clan is not afraid of you."
"If you know what's good for you, go to the Twelfth Heaven."
"The Liyuan Clan has the same roots as the Abyss Spirit of the Glorious Sect Heaven. You have nothing to fear with your body."
There were also creatures in the Nirvana Realm.
The creatures of the Nirvana Realm from the First to the Fourth Celestial Layer weren't that strong. However, the higher the level, the more powerful the creatures were. Some of them even occupied a territory in the Nirvana Realm. These territories were absolutely forbidden to be trespassed as they were known as forbidden areas!
For example, the Birth Clan of the Eighth Heaven, the Rainflower Clan of the Ninth Heaven, and the Dan Clan and Xiao Clan of the Tenth Heaven.
However, the whereabouts of these races in the Paramita Realm were very secretive. They basically did not communicate with the Yang souls, and the Yang souls that wandered in the Paramita Realm did not dare to barge into their territories. They were rather mysterious.
The only exception was the Abyss Spirit Clan in the 11th Heaven!
This clan was inextricably linked to the Liyuan Clan. It was said that the Liyuan Clan was not considered a superpower during the Middle Ages, but after the Clan Leader of the Liyuan Clan stepped into the Eleventh Heaven and met the Abyss Spirit Clan, the relationship between the two clans was confirmed.
The Abyss Spirit Race recognized the Liyuan Race as one of their own. Naturally, they supported the Liyuan Race without restraint, and the entire Eleventh Heaven fell into the hands of the Liyuan Race.
It could be said that the Abyss Spirit Tribe had contributed greatly to the Liyuan Clan's development.
"Is that so …"
Luo Zheng grabbed the arm of the Liyuan clansman. The enormous sun soul was as light as a feather in Luo Zheng's hand. He tossed it out, smashing it into the wide square.
Seeing Luo Zheng directly make a move, some of the Liyuan clansmen immediately began to counterattack.
When the foreign clansmen in the queue saw this scene, they all scattered like birds and beasts.
Some people enjoyed the show, but there were also some who had faces full of worry. They had been dragged into this matter for no reason, and they could not even leave the Paramita Realm in this damned place. They could neither go up nor down, so they did not know what to do.
The Liyuan clansmen were extremely large in size, and all of them were born with divine strength. Moreover, their clan had been taught by the Abyss Spirit Clan, a Paramita Realm race. They had all cultivated a secret soul technique that could allow their sun soul's strength to explosively increase for a short period of time.
In an instant, six or seven Liyuan clansmen charged towards Luo Zheng, their terrifying soul pressure pressing down on Luo Zheng's body.
Soul spears appeared in their hands, and they stabbed towards Luo Zheng.
However, the Liyuan clansmen still underestimated Luo Zheng's physical body.
Although the soul would become stronger the more one cultivated, there was still a fundamental difference between the soul and the physical body. Moreover, Luo Zheng's physical body was far stronger than that of ordinary people.
Boom, boom, boom, boom, boom ….
The soul spears smashed into Luo Zheng's body like wooden arrows. As soon as they touched Luo Zheng's body, they immediately dissipated.
After a single round of attacks, Luo Zheng was still standing in place, completely unscathed.
The Liyuan clansmen stared with wide eyes. When the leader of the Liyuan clansmen saw Luo Zheng's appearance, he inwardly cursed to himself.
"My turn?"
Facing his clansmen, Luo Zheng still held back.
When he had destroyed the Youxiong clan's Divine Temple, Luo Zheng hadn't killed a single human.
However, Luo Zheng didn't feel any sympathy towards the Liyuan clansmen. In Luo Zheng's heart, these intelligent creatures were no different from wild beasts!
Swish!
Luo Zheng exerted strength in his legs, and his entire body shot out like an arrow as he leapt into the air.
When he passed by a large Liyuan clansman, he gently pressed his finger against the top of his head.
Whoosh ….
This Liyuan clansman's Yang soul instantly dissipated.
However, Luo Zheng's attacks didn't stop there. He turned around and swept towards the next person. In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng had attacked four times, and the Yang souls of four Liyuan clansmen had already dissipated in Luo Zheng's hands.
"So, so strong …."
"We can't fight against him!"
"Notify the Yuanling clan and have them step in!"
The remaining Liyuan clansmen all fled, but this area had been sealed. Neither the Liyuan clansmen nor the other clansmen were able to leave the other side.
Luo Zheng didn't take advantage of his victory to pursue them. After observing for a moment, he said to the other clansmen, "You can find your own Stargate and leave this place."
Without the Liyuan clansmen obstructing them, they could naturally leave the Eleventh Layer of Heaven through the Stargate without having to pay 10,000 soul pills.
The other clansmen all revealed grateful expressions. No matter what, Luo Zheng had helped them save 10,000 soul pills. More importantly, he had taught the overbearing Liyuan clansmen a good lesson!
The Liyuan clansmen were tyrannical. Not only in the Eleventh Layer of Heaven, but they were also a tyrannical clan in the Mother World. Many smaller clansmen submitted to them.
"Friend from the human clan, thank you, but you have to be careful of those Yuanling clansmen!"
"Those Yuanling clansmen are creatures of the other side, and they also exist in the form of flesh …."
"They are very strong!"
Some clansmen who knew a little bit of inside information informed Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng nodded and strode forward on his own.
The Liyuan clansmen hadn't just built a temple on the other side, but a huge city! Just the city walls alone were hundreds of meters tall, and the buildings inside were easily thousands of meters tall.
Soon, Luo Zheng had already arrived at the bottom of the wall. He stretched out his hand and gently pressed against the wall.
As he abruptly exerted his strength, a loud rumble sounded. The hundreds of meters tall and 100 meters wide wall directly collapsed, revealing a huge gap.

Chapter 2897
Once upon a time.
"What happened?"
"The city wall collapsed?"
"Who can destroy this wall? Could it be a Demon Beast? "
The Abyss Spirit Clan and the Liyuan Clan were not the only living beings in the 12 Heavens of the Paramita Realm.
There was an extremely powerful Nirvana Realm creature here, and it was called the Ghost Beast.
It was impossible for a Yang Soul at the eleventh heaven to destroy the city walls. The only thing that could cause such great damage was those elusive Ghost Beasts.
But when they saw Luo Zheng's figure, they were all stunned.
"What's that?"
"It seems to be a human."
"He's not in soul form. What a joke!"
When the Liyuan clansmen saw Luo Zheng's true appearance, they were filled with shock and astonishment.
This scene went against the common sense in their hearts!
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes as he slowly walked forward. He could sense the emotions of the Liyuan Tribe members.
When he walked towards a huge building, his footsteps did not change at all, but his toes directly stepped into the pillar of the building, and then his body touched the surface of the pillar.
This thick pillar couldn't stop Luo Zheng's footsteps at all. As he moved forward, it shattered with a loud bang.
Many of the Li Yuan clansmen hadn't realized the danger they were in, and they all came forward to stop them.
"I don't care which clan you're from! You're courting death! "
"No one has ever dared to offend our Liyuan Clan, let alone a mere human!"
"Are you from the You Xiong Clan?"
Luo Zheng couldn't be bothered to pay attention to these clansmen of the Liyuan Clan.
Some people who were courting death dared to approach. Luo Zheng raised his hand and lightly tapped them, shattering their Yang souls.
After killing a few Liyuan clansmen, the others didn't dare to approach.
However, the members of this race were truly valiant. Even though they knew that Luo Zheng was extremely dangerous, they didn't leave the shore. The majority of them even gathered behind Luo Zheng. Although some of them didn't dare to come forward, they still cursed and swore.
"Rumble …"
Luo Zheng passed through a huge building, which finally collapsed.
He still didn't change his direction and continued to walk towards the center of the city. If one looked down from above, they would see that Luo Zheng had drawn a straight line, and wherever this line passed, it was a mess!
It could be said that nothing could stop him!
More and more Liyuan clansmen followed behind Luo Zheng. Seeing the destruction of such a large city, their hearts were bleeding.
"Human brat! Stop tearing it down! "
"What do you want? You can ask for it!"
"…"
These people from the Liyuan Tribe weren't the people in charge of this city. He'd already asked for a hundred million soul pills, but they'd only treated him like an idiot.
Sometimes, if you don't make the other party feel pain, they won't obediently submit.
Along the way, Luo Zheng turned a deaf ear to their words!
When Luo Zheng passed through a wide road, what appeared in front of him was a huge, magnificent building.
When Luo Zheng had just entered the Fourth Heaven, he'd been very emotional when he saw the temples. He hadn't expected that the human race would be able to build such a huge building in the Paramita Realm.
But compared to this huge building in front of him, it was child's play.
"This building is very beautiful."
For the first time, Luo Zheng said something.
When the Liyuan clansmen heard this, their hearts immediately trembled.
"What, what are you thinking?!" they asked.
It was fine if Luo Zheng destroyed the outer buildings, but this building was the temple of the Liyuan Tribe. How could they allow it to be destroyed?
"Tear it down," Luo Zheng spat out.
Then, he walked towards the huge building.
"Stop him!"
"Don't let him enter the temple!"
"The temple of our Liyuan Tribe cannot be desecrated!"
The Liyuan clansmen became agitated, and quite a few of them threw themselves at Luo Zheng without any regard for their own safety.
But as soon as these people got close, they were scared out of their wits by Luo Zheng.
The Liyuan clansmen fell into despair. Would the temple really be destroyed in the hands of a human brat?
At this moment, a loud horn sounded in the distance.
"Yo!"
Outside the tall city walls, dozens of huge eagles flew towards Ao Xiang. On these eagles stood giants.
These giants looked very similar to the Liyuan Tribe. The difference was that the Liyuan Tribe's Yang Soul could only see the outline, but these giants' appearances and patterns could be seen clearly.
They were the Abyss Spirit Tribe that lived in the Paramita Realm!
"Great! The Abyss Spirit Knights are here! "
"They've finally made their move!"
"Kill this damn human brat!"
When the Liyuan clansmen saw the Abyss Spirit Knights in the sky, they were all excited. This was the backing of their Liyuan Tribe.
"Hua hua hua …"
The Abyss Spirit Knights spread their wings and flew high into the sky. They lined up and swooped down towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng had destroyed so many buildings. Neither the Liyuan Tribe nor the Abyss Spirit Tribe would let him go. The Abyss Spirit Knights had the mentality of killing Luo Zheng.
"Hua!"
When the Abyss Spirit Knights were a few hundred feet away from Luo Zheng, a white bone spear appeared in their hands.
These bone spears were not weak soul spears. They were many times stronger than soul spears!
"Kill!"
All the Abyss Spirit Knights sped up, and their murderous aura filled the sky.
"How strong … are the Paramita Realm creatures?" Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes.
Although he had entered the Indigo Soul Realm, he still did not know much about the Paramita Realm.
After entering the Paramita Realm for so long, he had only seen two types of Paramita Realm creatures. One was the corpse of the Sky Weaver Spider in the Sky Sunflower Temple, and the other was the Void Beast in the temple.
He had seen two types at once. One was the Abyss Spirit Tribe, and the other was the eagles that the Abyss Spirit Tribe rode.
"Whoosh!"
The bone spear went straight for him. Luo Zheng turned over and dodged the bone spear. He twisted his hand and easily caught the bone spear in his hand.
"This power is not bad …"
"It's much stronger than a pure Sun Soul."
"But it's still far from enough to deal with me!"
While grabbing the bone spear, Luo Zheng pulled it out.
The bone spear surged in the air and made an ear-piercing sound. The end of the bone spear whipped the chest of an Abyss Spirit Knight.
"Peng!"
A mist of blood burst out from the Abyss Spirit Knight's body. The Abyss Spirit Knight was swept off the eagle's back and lay on the ground motionlessly. The eagle he rode also rolled and smashed to the ground. It could no longer fly down.
Luo Zheng turned the bone spear gently and did not stop at all. He directly smashed it into the second Abyss Spirit Knight that was charging at him.
"Bang, bang, bang, bang …"
Every time the bone spear in Luo Zheng's hand whipped out, an Abyss Spirit Knight would be smashed off the eagle's back.
The Abyss Spirit Tribe was indeed stronger than the Sun Soul Realm. However, they were only creatures of the 11th Celestial Level. In terms of pure physical strength, they were still unable to compete with Luo Zheng.
Seven or eight Abyss Spirit Knights were sent flying by Luo Zheng. The Abyss Spirit Knights behind quickly controlled the eagles to fly up.
The abyss spirits all had terrified expressions, as if they had recalled something bad.
"This guy is a Aragami?"
"Another Aragami can enter the Paramita Realm?"
"Impossible! The rules have been changed. No Aragami has been able to enter the Paramita Realm for billions of years! "

Chapter 2898
In ancient times, the masters of the mother world mainly cultivated the Aragami when they entered the Nirvana Realm. They used the Ancient God Body Refining Technique to enter the Nirvana Realm.
At that time, it was much easier to walk in the Nirvana Realm.
After all, the physical body was far stronger than the soul, and the Aragami could easily transform into thousands of feet tall. At that time, the Nirvana Realm creatures were in an extremely weak position. Some of them were even slaughtered by the Aragami.
The Abyss Spirit Clan could only hide in a corner of Yaozong Heaven.
When the Aragami came to plunder, it was a catastrophe for the Abyss Spirit Clan.
Once upon a time, the Abyss Spirit Clan was almost exterminated by the Aragami!
To the Abyss Spirit Clan, the memory at that time was not a good memory. It was their dark age.
After struggling at death's door for a long time, the Abyss Spirit Clan finally survived until the Aragami Cultivation Technique gradually declined and the rules of truth changed. Only then did the Nirvana Realm creatures become the masters.
Those who entered the Nirvana Realm were only those weak Sun Souls. How could these Sun Souls be the opponents of the Nirvana Realm creatures?
Therefore, the Nirvana Realm creatures carried out crazy revenge!
At that time, the Fourth Heaven and above was a land of death for the masters of the Nirvana Realm in the mother world. It was extremely dangerous. Anyone who entered would die because no one could resist the siege of the Nirvana Realm creatures.
This was also the reason why most races built their divine temples in the fourth heaven. Even though there were also Nirvana Realm living beings in the first to fourth heaven, the Nirvana Realm living beings here did not form a race and would not consciously attack the Yang soul.
The hatred of the Nirvana Realm creatures gradually subsided with the passage of time. As for what resolved the hatred, it was still unknown.
In short, from the Middle Ages until now, the Nirvana Realm creatures and the Sun Souls got along peacefully. Among them, there was no lack of super clans that chose to join forces with the Nirvana Realm creatures.
For example, the Abyss Spirit Clan and the Liyuan Clan had a blood relationship. That was even more natural.
After the few Liyuan Clan members who collected the "toll" escaped, they informed the higher-ups of their clan. The higher-ups of the Liyuan Clan quickly communicated with the Abyss Spirit Clan. That was why the Abyss Spirit Knights were dispatched before Luo Zheng demolished the temple.
These Abyss Spirit Knights were almost always successful. Their cultivation might not be strong, but the Sun Soul of the Nirvana Realm did not have the ability to resist. However, they were still in the dark and did not expect that they were facing a body!
This scene naturally evoked fear in their hearts …
No one from that era would want to return to that era.
Those who had heard of that era were also filled with fear towards that era.
If there were a large number of corporeal bodies that could step onto the Nirvana Realm, it would undoubtedly be the descent of darkness for them.
"Aragami?"
A playful smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
In the entire Chaos, only those at the bottom of the Chaos could cultivate the Aragami. Moreover, none of them could reach the Nirvana Realm with the Ancient God Body Refining Technique. Luo Zheng had reached the Nirvana Realm, but he had never tried to activate his Aragami body. "So this is the Aragami you are talking about?"
"Hua hua hua …"
In an instant, Luo Zheng's body grew to over ten thousand feet tall.
Even the Abyss Spirit Knights sitting on the backs of eagles had to look up to them.
The Abyss Spirit Knights looked at the expanding Luo Zheng and their faces turned pale. The eagles they were sitting on also became restless.
"You are indeed a cultivator of the Aragami!"
"I heard from my grandfather that this guy can really become so huge!"
"I'm afraid he's over ten thousand feet tall?"
The Abyss Spirit Clansmen opened their mouths wide and looked at Luo Zheng in a daze.
At this time, it was not only the Abyss Spirit Knights who were shocked. There were also many Sun Souls of the Liyuan Clan in the city.
When the Liyuan Clan saw the arrival of the Abyss Spirit Knights, their morale was greatly boosted.
The Sun Souls of the Liyuan Clan had heard a lot about the power of the Abyss Spirit Knights, but no one had seen them. This was because no one dared to cause trouble in the Eleventh Heaven over the years. Even if they did, it would be difficult to alarm the Abyss Spirit Knights!
Who would have thought that such a powerful Abyss Spirit Knight would be so weak in the hands of Luo Zheng?
They were all depressed, shocked, and puzzled. When they saw Luo Zheng's body expand to such an extent in the blink of an eye, the Sun Souls exuded an aura of extreme fear. The more timid Sun Souls directly retreated from the Nirvana Realm.
However, more Sun Souls refused to leave.
"Oh my god, was this human sent by the heavens to destroy our Liyuan Clan?"
"Our Liyuan Clan has occupied the Eleventh Heaven for so many years. Are our good days coming to an end?"
"From the looks of it … the temple can't be saved."
In a corner of this large city, the Sun Soul of the Liyuan Clan who had previously extorted Luo Zheng also saw Luo Zheng's huge figure. His face was filled with fear. At this moment, he finally understood where Luo Zheng's confidence came from, and it was too late for regrets in his heart.
"One hundred million soul pills … asking him to leave is not expensive!"
"One hundred million soul pills!"
"Give it to him!"
The Sun Soul seemed to have lost its mind, and as it chanted, it rushed into the temple.
In the square outside the city, there were many outsiders who also saw the giant figure rising in the city. Luo Zheng's body was too huge, and it was difficult for them not to notice him.
"That's … a human?"
"Look at that figure, it seems to be that person just now!"
"How did this person become so huge?"
Although these outsiders knew that Luo Zheng wanted to wreak havoc in the Liyuan Clan, they never expected that Luo Zheng would use an almost unparalleled method …
In front of Luo Zheng, the temple that was not very tall only reached his calves. It was as if he could trample the entire temple into pieces by lifting his leg. However, only Luo Zheng knew that he was just using his connections to intimidate others.
Although Luo Zheng had absorbed a large number of desolate bones, the limit of his gigantification was far more than ten thousand feet.
However, the strength of the Aragami was nothing compared to the power of the Nirvana Realm. After all, the Aragami only targeted True Gods. As for Luo Zheng, when he was a perfected True God, he only had the strength of a Divine Jun at his full strength.
The Aragami of ancient times had entered the Nirvana Realm using the Ancient God Body Tempering Technique. They cultivated the True Meaning of the Divine Body, and similarly, they could fuse with the Nirvana Realm token.
However, Luo Zheng's nature was very special. He had not cultivated the True Meaning of the Divine Body, so he did not know how his body could fuse with the Nirvana Realm token, or if he could do so.
At this moment, three more eagles flew in from outside the city.
On the back of the eagles sat three elders from the Abyss Spirit Clan. These three elders had lived for an unknown period of time. Their tall bodies had shrunk by half. Compared to the Abyss Spirit Knights, they were considered dwarfs.
The three elders floated in front of Luo Zheng. The white-haired elder from the Abyss Spirit Clan said, "Human, why did you destroy our city and our temple for no reason?"

Chapter 2899
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Compromise
Luo Zheng's voice reverberated in the air like thunder, causing the scalps of the Sun Souls to go numb.
When the three elders of the Abyss Spirit Clan heard Luo Zheng's words, they looked at each other.
They looked very calm on the surface, but their hearts were filled with worry.
If Luo Zheng had come to find trouble for no reason, they would be in big trouble this time. If there was a reason, the situation would be different. Things that could be easily solved by negotiation.
"Friend from the Human Clan, please wait for a moment. I will ask the Liyuan Clan." The leading elder cupped his hands towards Luo Zheng.
Soon after, the eagle under his feet quickly descended and charged into the temple without consulting anyone.
After the time it took to burn a stick of incense, the elder rode the eagle back to Luo Zheng. He said with a calm expression, "I apologize for what the Liyuan Clan did to you. They broke the rules. We will pay 100 million Soul Elixirs."
100 million Soul Elixirs was only a toll for 10,000 people. The Liyuan Clan could easily pay it.
The damage Luo Zheng had caused to this big city was more than 100 million Soul Elixirs. Moreover, if they did not pay 100 million Soul Elixirs, they would have to pay a higher price.
The elder was worried that Luo Zheng would go back on his word. After all, they did not have any room to bargain.
"Send it to the Human Clan's Star Ring in the 12th Heaven. 100 million Soul Elixirs will be exchanged for 100 Soul Crystals," Luo Zheng said.
The three elders of the Abyss Spirit Clan naturally accepted Luo Zheng's conditions without any objections.
Luo Zheng's figure slowly shrank to the size of a normal person.
Just as he was about to leave, the elder of the Abyss Spirit Clan asked, "Friend from the Human Clan …"
"What's the matter?" Luo Zheng turned his head and raised his eyebrows.
"As far as I know, the Ancient God Body Refining Technique has lost its effect and cut off the path to the other side. How did you enter this place?" the elder asked.
This was probably the elder's biggest doubt. He did not believe that anyone could go against the Truth. The Truth was the standard of the entire Chaos and was greater than everything. If Luo Zheng had really surpassed the Truth, he would be an existence above the Chaos Ancient Gods. It was impossible for him to only have this little cultivation.
"I … don't know," Luo Zheng said with a smile. "Even if I did know, I probably wouldn't tell you."
The elder of the Abyss Spirit Clan The elder of the Abyss Spirit Clan smiled awkwardly. He could only watch as Luo Zheng left.
There were many people from the Liyuan Clan on both sides of the road. They looked at Luo Zheng, their eyes were full of fear.
What the Liyuan Family didn't know was that they weren't the first superpower to suffer a loss in Luo Zheng's hands. You Xiong Family had suffered a lot before.
Outside the city, Luo Zheng stepped into the Human Clan's star ring and entered the Twelfth Heaven.
Ling Shuang had been waiting here for a long time. When she saw Luo Zheng walk out, she immediately heaved a sigh of relief.
This time, Luo Zheng was not fighting against the You Xiong Clan, but the Liyuan Clan. The Liyuan Clan had the support of the Abyss Spirit Clan, a Nirvana Realm clan.
"How is it?" Ling Shuang asked.
"Wait here. Someone should be coming soon," Luo Zheng replied.
"Wait for what …" Ling Shuang blinked and asked.
"A hundred spirit crystals." Luo Zheng shrugged.
Ling Shuang was slightly shocked. "You really want them to pay a hundred times? They are willing? You didn't tear down their temple again, did you? "
Ling Shuang had just experienced how overbearing the Liyuan Clan was. If they wanted to force such a superpower to submit, they would probably have to tear down their temple.
"Almost. They agreed before I could tear it down," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Although Luo Zheng did not explain the process, Ling Shuang could roughly guess that even if he did not tear down their temple, he probably did a lot of damage.
This guy was just like what Granny had said. If Luo Zheng could successfully cross the Sea of Truth, he would probably be able to do whatever he wanted in the Nirvana Realm. But now, this guy was not doing whatever he wanted. He was simply lawlessly tearing down other people's houses everywhere he went …
The two of them waited near the star ring for more than two hours. Then, a Yang Soul appeared beside the star ring and walked towards Luo Zheng.
There was only one physical body in the Nirvana Realm. Luo Zheng was naturally an extremely conspicuous existence.
"The Liyuan Clan entrusted me to deliver a hundred spirit crystals to you," the person said politely. He took out small cylinders from his Sumeru Ring. These cylinders all had spirit crystals sealed inside. There were a total of a hundred of them, which was exactly a hundred spirit crystals.
Luo Zheng took these spirit crystals and casually threw them to Ling Shuang behind him. At the same time, he asked, "The Liyuan Clan gave so many spirit crystals to you. Aren't they afraid that you will run away?"
The person smiled and said, "Of course not. I am in the Liyuan Clan. How can I run away?"
Luo Zheng shrugged noncommittally. The Liyuan Clan was a superpower. Other than their own clan, there should be a small number of outsiders serving them.
After handing over the spirit crystals, the person stepped into the star ring and returned to the eleventh heaven.
After Luo Zheng's soul returned to the Nirvana Realm, he did not stay in the twelfth heaven. Instead, he directly entered the star ring and continued to the thirteenth heaven.
…
The thirteenth heaven was called the Void Bright Heaven. It was the last heaven in the Color Realm and also the most important heaven in this realm.
Most Yang Souls would stay here to search for the most suitable Nirvana token for themselves.
The Void Bright Heaven had never disappointed the many creatures in the Mother World. Even now, there were still many secrets in the Void Bright Heaven that had not been unraveled. There were also many temples that had not been set foot in.
However, the thirteenth heaven was also the most dangerous heaven in the Color Realm. There were as many as six or seven Nirvana Realm races living in it alone. This did not include some Nirvana Realm creatures that had not formed a race. They were equally powerful and dangerous.
Once, there were a few major powers in the Mother World that wanted to imitate the Liyuan Clan and control the thirteenth heaven. In the end, they all failed. The Nirvana Realm races inside were constantly attacking each other. How could they let the Yang Souls rule the world?
"I thought the thirteenth heaven would be very barren. I didn't expect it to be so lively here." After Luo Zheng arrived at the thirteenth heaven, what he saw was poles apart from what he had imagined.
The star rings used by the various races for ascension were placed haphazardly. Other races could be seen everywhere. They got along very well with each other. There were no disputes.
Since these Yang Souls could set foot in the thirteenth heaven, they had basically cultivated the Indigo Soul or were only a step away from it.
"You should know that the thirteenth heaven gathers the elite disciples of all the races in the Mother World. Naturally, there are many Indigo Souls here," Ling Shuang replied.
Although it was her first time here, she was already used to this.
Ling Shuang led Luo Zheng past the haphazardly placed star rings. She led him all the way to the south and saw a single-story building.
"It's really here," Ling Shuang said.

Chapter 2900
Translator: Nyoi-Bo Studio Editor: Nyoi-Bo Studio
The Spreading Dark Domain
This stronghold was naturally not as heavily guarded as the Divine Temple. There were no guards at the entrance.
However, as soon as Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang stepped in, someone came forward to welcome them.
"Princess Ling Shuang, we're finally here." The person bowed to Ling Shuang and at the same time, cupped his hands to Luo Zheng. "Brother Luo Zheng."
Ling Shuang had communicated with Song Feiyu beforehand. Song Feiyu had been waiting here for a long time.
Luo Zheng recognized this person's voice. It was Song Feiyu. He responded, "Brother Feiyu!"
In the Seven Mountains Party, Song Feiyu had performed well and led Taiyi Mountain to second place.
However, in Luo Zheng's mind, Song Feiyu's talent was still inferior to Feng Ge, Mo Yijian, and the others.
As the leader of Taiyi Mountain's disciples, this person naturally had his own strengths.
Song Feiyu's Sun Soul was very unique. The Sun Soul's body had already entered the Indigo Soul Level. It shone with a purple brilliance. Behind the Sun Soul were three small souls. These souls looked exactly like Song Feiyu, but they were only the size of a palm.
Luo Zheng stared at him for a while. Song Feiyu sensed it and the three souls behind Song Feiyu gently floated over.
"Brother Luo Zheng, are you curious about me?"
"This is a split soul."
"Every soul is an individual," Li Yundong said.
These small souls floated in front of Luo Zheng and each of them said something. Their voices were all Song Feiyu's and sounded very strange.
"Senior Brother Song, your split souls are so fun." Ling Shuang laughed when she saw this scene.
Although she already knew that Song Feiyu's soul had split into three, Song Feiyu had always been active in the Thirteenth Heaven. It was also her first time seeing these three split souls.
"Why did Brother Feiyu split his soul?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Before Song Feiyu could say anything, Ling Shuang had already answered on his behalf, "Because Senior Brother Song's Nirvana Token is a four-body combination, and it's a descending type."
Song Feiyu's Nirvana Realm tokens were four clay figurines. After arriving in the mother world, each of the clay figurines could freely take shape. The three divided souls could think independently and control the other three clay figurines independently.
"I see." Luo Zheng nodded.
Some outstanding Nirvana Realm tokens were indeed worthy of his cooperation with the Art of Soul Division.
"Let's go! I'll take you guys to familiarize yourself with this area, "Song Feiyu said.
The reason he was waiting for Ling Shuang here was to act as a guide. After all, the Thirteenth Heaven was the final layer of the Color Realm. It was very different from the other regions.
After leaving this simple stronghold, Song Feiyu said as he led the way, "The 13th Heaven is completely different from the 12th Heaven below."
As he spoke, the "little Song Feiyu" behind him flew in front of Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang and turned into a round pancake.
"From the First Heaven to the Twelfth Heaven, they are all circular. The star rings are also randomly distributed in this circle. It's very scattered. Only the Eleventh Heaven is a special case because of the Liyuan Clan. You guys probably already know that," Song Feiyu said.
Ling Shuang and Luo Zheng nodded at the same time. The Liyuan Clan was probably still in chaos now.
"But the Thirteenth Heaven is long and narrow." Song Feiyu's finger shook gently, and a piece of the round pancake was bitten off, leaving only a thin strip in the middle. "The star rings of all the races in the mother world are gathered on this end, and the road to the Desire Realm is on the other end."
Song Feiyu's explanation was very vivid. Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang instantly understood.
"Why is the Thirteenth Heaven shaped like this?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Song Feiyu smiled and said, "Actually … the Thirteenth Heaven itself is also circular, but the two areas are both dark areas, shrouded in darkness. If you rashly enter, you will only fall into endless darkness. Moreover, the dark areas are still expanding step by step. If one day the two dark areas are connected to each other, I'm afraid the road to the Desire Realm will be completely closed."
When Luo Zheng heard about the dark areas, his heart suddenly moved slightly.
He had obtained the candle that caused the underwater whirlpool in the Sea of Truth. Luo Zheng could not feel the existence of the Paramita Power from the candle, but 9527 asked Luo Zheng to keep it well. At the same time, he told him that the candle was a necessary item to enter the dark areas.
According to 9527, Luo Zheng did not need the candle for the time being. After reaching the Fourth Heaven and obtaining the Sumeru Ring, Luo Zheng threw the candles in the corner.
"It's just as you think. To enter the dark areas, you need the candle," 9527 answered directly when he heard Song Feiyu's introduction.
"There is a dark area in the Thirteenth Heaven?" Luo Zheng asked.
"This is the first time I've heard of it," 9527 said. "The dark areas used to only exist in the highest few heavens, but the dark areas themselves can spread. It is estimated that they have only invaded the Thirteenth Heaven in recent years."
Song Feiyu continued, "There are generally only two purposes for creatures who enter the Thirteenth Heaven. One is to get a better Paramita token, and the other is to go to the Desire Realm. I wonder if Your Highness Ling Shuang and Brother Luo Zheng want to go to the Desire Realm, or to get a Paramita token?"
In Song Feiyu's view, these two were the pride of the Heavenly Palace. With their talent, they should have integrated a good Paramita token in the Fourth Heaven. Their purpose was most likely to go to the Desire Realm.
But Luo Zheng's answer exceeded Song Feiyu's expectations, "I need a Paramita token, one that is suitable for the Dao of Nature."
Song Feiyu had seen Luo Zheng's strength during the Seven Mountains Party. Luo Zheng could lift an enormous rock by himself. He should have integrated a strengthening-type Paramita token. Song Feiyu chuckled, "So it seems that Brother Luo Zheng has dual cultivated the True Meaning of Dao."
It was rare to see someone dual cultivating the True Meaning of Dao at this stage. Most would only choose to cultivate the second True Meaning of Dao after reaching the Chaotic Origin Realm in order to pursue greater strength.
Song Feiyu naturally did not know that Luo Zheng was not dual cultivating but triple cultivating.
"Since Brother Luo Zheng needs a Paramita token, you can either explore the Deity Temple or participate in the auction of the Rats," Song Feiyu suggested.
"The Rats are referring to the Eared Rat Clan?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Right," Song Feiyu answered.
The Eared Rats were greedy. Even though the Eared Rat Clan was powerful and had revitalized the trade in the Paramita Realm, other races still called them Rats. Naturally, there was disdain in their words.
Luo Zheng had long heard that the Eared Rat Clan monopolized the trade in the entire Paramita Realm. But after entering the Fourth Heaven, he had only toured around the Deity Temple. He did not touch other regions of the Paramita Realm and did not interact with other races.
Hearing Song Feiyu's words, Luo Zheng became interested.
Just the 100 Soul Crystals he got from the Li Yuan Clan were worth 100 Million Soul Pills. Furthermore, he had plundered even more Soul Crystals from the You Xiong Clan.
If the Eared Rat Clan could auction off some precious Paramita Tokens, it would save him the trouble of personally searching for them.
"Then we will have to trouble Senior Disciple Brother Song to bring us over to have a look." Ling Shuang directly said from the side.

Chapter 2901

Sale
Under Song Feiyu's lead, the three of them headed north for a short distance before they saw a short building.
In front of the building, there were many foreign tribes gathered, as if they were waiting for something.
Song Feiyu walked over alone to ask around, then came back to Luo Zheng and said, "There's still about two hours before the auction starts. Looks like we have to wait here."
"Luo Zheng, since the Eared Mouse Clan is hosting the auction, they should be buying Nirvana Realm tokens. You can sell your Nirvana Realm tokens to them," Ling Shuang suddenly reminded him.
As she said that, Luo Zheng remembered that he still had a large number of Nirvana Realm tokens in his hands.
These Nirvana Realm tokens were treasures of the You Xiong Clan, and they were worth a lot.
After Luo Zheng took it away, he also checked the other Nirvana Realm tokens one by one. Although every Nirvana Realm token was not bad, they were not particularly suitable for Luo Zheng.
Now that Luo Zheng had integrated two Nirvana Realm tokens, the Mighty God Totem and the Mind's Eye had their own uses, he had to be very careful when integrating the last Nirvana Realm token.
Selling off these Nirvana tokens would be the best choice.
"These rats will naturally buy Nirvana Realm tokens, but they are very picky. If ordinary Nirvana Realm tokens are not good enough for them, I'll bring Brother Luo Zheng over," Song Feiyu said.
From Song Feiyu's point of view, this was the first time Luo Zheng had stepped into the Thirteenth Heaven. During the Seven Mountains Meeting, Luo Zheng's Yang Soul had yet to reach the Indigo Soul Level, and he was at most at the Fourth Heaven. In such a short time, Luo Zheng should not be able to produce a good Nirvana Realm token.
Of course, he did not know that Luo Zheng's Nirvana Realm tokens were all snatched …
"Senior Song, let's not trouble you. Luo Zheng can go by himself," Ling Shuang stopped him.

Although Lingshuang trusted Song Feiyu, she didn't want to expose Luo Zheng's identity.
Song Feiyu felt a little strange, but he didn't say anything. He just pointed the way for Luo Zheng.
According to Song Feiyu's instructions, Luo Zheng walked around the short building and found another entrance.
As soon as he entered, he heard a shrill voice.
"Squeak … Human, are you planning to sell the Nirvana Token in your hands?" Not far away, a mouse half the height of a human paced over.
"Yes," Luo Zheng replied.
"Squeak squeak, just follow me," the Ear Mouse said.
The Ear Mouse led Luo Zheng into a low house. After entering, Luo Zheng almost had to bow and walk forward.
There were two other Ear Mice in the low house. After seeing Luo Zheng, they greeted him. One of them said, "You can present the Nirvana token in your hand. However, we have to make it clear that we, the Ear Mice, will not accept any kind of trash. If your Nirvana token is below the top grade, we will not take it out."
"Top grade? The Nirvana tokens are divided into grades? "Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
The Three-eared Mice glanced at each other when they saw Luo Zheng's expression.
This human kid didn't even know the grade of the Nirvana token and wanted to sell it to them. Was he playing with them?
The Ear Mouse that led Luo Zheng in pointed at the door and said, "If you don't even know this, please leave. We are very busy. Squeak …" The Ear Mouse's tone became unfriendly.
Luo Zheng really didn't know how the Nirvana tokens were divided into grades.
In his mind, the Nirvana tokens were divided according to the place where they were obtained. The Nirvana tokens of the Second Heaven were better than those of the First Heaven, while the Nirvana tokens of the Thirteenth Heaven were better than those of the Twelfth Heaven.
However, although this method of division was intuitive, it was actually not strict. Although the Nirvana tokens of the First Heaven were generally average, there was a very small probability of obtaining a high-quality one. For example, Luo Zheng's Mind's Eye could not be called a "Nirvana token of the First Heaven."
Seeing that the other party was about to drive him away, Luo Zheng felt helpless. He stretched out his hand and a square box appeared at his fingertips. A Nirvana token fell out from the Sumeru Ring. "Sorry, I really don't know how to distinguish the grades of the Nirvana tokens. I hope you can enlighten me."
The Nirvana token that Luo Zheng took out was an animal skull with two sharp horns and an extraordinary aura.
The Three Ear Mice's eyes lit up slightly when they saw the skull.
"A salted cow skull of the Fifth Heaven?"
"It's a top grade one. Squeak."
"We can accept it. Squeak."
The Three Ear Mice said in unison.
Luo Zheng took the opportunity to ask the Three Ear Mice how the grades of the Nirvana tokens should be divided.
"You don't even know the grades of the Nirvana tokens. How did you cultivate to the Indigo Soul Grade? What a strange human. Squeak … Nirvana tokens are generally divided into six grades: low-low-grade, low-grade, middle-grade, high-grade, high-high-grade, and super-grade." One of the Three Ear Mice helplessly taught Luo Zheng a lesson.
Naturally, the higher the grade of the Nirvana tokens in the same layer of heaven, the more expensive they were. However, if they were all high-grade, the price of a high-grade Nirvana token in the Thirteenth Heaven would naturally exceed that of a high-grade one in the Twelfth Heaven.
"A salted cow skull of the Fifth Heaven is worth 100,000 soul pills. I wonder if you can accept it." The Three Ear Mouse who inspected the skull offered a price.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.
"Here are 100,000 soul pills. You can leave now." The Three Ear Mouse took out 300,000 soul pills from the Sumeru Ring and handed them to Luo Zheng.
After selling one Nirvana token, Luo Zheng did not want to leave.
He took out another one from the Sumeru Ring.
"I think it's a Zither Flower Seed from the Sixth Heaven. It's a high-grade, 120,000 soul pills …"
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded again.
Just when the Three Ear Mouse thought that Luo Zheng could finally leave, Luo Zheng took out another Nirvana token.
The Three Ear Mouse was speechless. They only thought that Luo Zheng was a casual customer who did not have anything good in his hands. However, they were still quite depressed when he took them out one by one. One of the Three Ear Mice said, "How many Nirvana tokens do you want to sell? Can you take them all out at once?
