"Or is this a scam? You don't have the Paramita Key! "
"What are you waiting for?"
The Ear Mouse was naturally waiting for Luo Zheng.
If Luo Zheng wasn't here, how could they open the door so easily?
Although they had learned about Luo Zheng's physical body through various channels, seeing was believing. Just now, Luo Zheng had gently pushed out a palm and directly shattered Ji Yan's Sun Soul. This kind of power made the Ear Mouse overjoyed.
Ling Shuang stood together with her worries, waiting for Luo Zheng's news.
She looked around and saw Luo Zheng carrying a Sun Soul in the distance. Her eyebrows immediately furrowed and she muttered, "Feng Ge doesn't have legs. Can't he walk on his own …"

Chapter 2914
All the members of the Mysterious Clan were watching Luo Zheng quietly. Most of them had fear in their eyes.
The Ear Mice were very relaxed. It seemed that the Sun Soul named Luo Zheng was right. Luo Zheng was indeed the spokesperson of this body.
"Squeak … you are finally here!"
The Three-ear Mice took the lead to greet him. Although it was the first time they had seen this body, they still expressed their full respect.
Luo Zheng certainly knew that the Ear Mice had been waiting for him, but he didn't want to talk about opening the door now. "Let's talk about opening the door later."
He bypassed the Ear Mice and walked toward the disciples of the Heavenly Palace. At the same time, he asked, "Which clans attacked the disciples of the Heavenly Palace just now?"
As soon as Luo Zheng said this, the members of the You Xiong Clan, the Shennong Clan, the Golden Crow Clan, and other clans were shocked.
"The You Xiong Clan, the Golden Crow Clan, the Shennong Clan, and the Minyue Clan," Chou Xinghe replied carefully.
Luo Zheng glanced around and saw the bird-shaped Sun Souls of the Jinwu Clan. He said, "Don't even think about exploring the Temple of Heaven. You can choose to get out of the Nirvana World or be killed by me."
Hearing Luo Zheng's choice, the expressions of these clans became more and more gloomy.
Even if Luo Zheng's body was extremely powerful, it only made them a little afraid. After all, they could leave the Nirvana World at any time.
Therefore, even many members of the You Xiong Clan stood in the distance and watched. They didn't give up the idea of exploring the Temple of Heaven. After all, they had already paid the Soul Elixir.
If the Ear Mice were on the side of the body, the situation would be different.
Moreover, once they entered the Temple of Heaven, they couldn't leave the Nirvana World. At that time, they would be at the mercy of this body.
"My friend, every injustice has its perpetrator and every debt has its debtor. Just now, the You Xiong Clan kept asking for help. We had no choice but to attack. Moreover, we didn't kill any disciples of the Heavenly Palace. It's a bit overbearing for you to not let us enter the temple," a member of the Divine Farmer Clan said.
"But the Divine Farmer Clan still chose to attack?" Luo Zheng laughed coldly, "Since you chose to follow You Xiong Family, you have to pay the price. Back then, your Shennong Family joined hands with You Xiong Family to exterminate the Nine Li Clan. It's not that we don't want to take revenge, it's just that the time hasn't come yet."
The annihilation of a super force was a big deal even in the huge Mother World. All the non-human creatures present knew what happened back then. If Shennong hadn't suddenly attacked and caused Jiuli to be attacked from both sides, Jiuli wouldn't have been destroyed.
The other creatures of different races felt a little strange. Why did Luo Zheng mention the Nine Li Tribe at this time?
Luo Zheng's tone contained a trace of anger. Was he from the Jiuli Tribe?
Feng Ge, who was hiding behind Luo Zheng, also felt Luo Zheng's aura. He guessed that this person's identity was more complicated than he had imagined.
The six people standing in the corner were even more stunned.
"Brother! Why did he mention Jiuli! Could he be of the same race as us? "The human girl lowered her voice and asked.
"It sounds like it," another person said.
The strongest person among them shook his head, "Not necessarily. Maybe he was just making a comparison. The branches of the Nine Li Tribe are either dead or scattered. How could we give birth to such a person …"
Now, the Nine Li Tribe had lost all its resources. It was difficult for the younger generation to compete with the other big clans. The decline of the Nine Li Tribe was inevitable.
The human girl curled her lips. She knew her brother was very suspicious. But she had made up her mind that if she had a chance, she would ask him in person.
The Divine Farmer Clan didn't refute. Not far away, a member of You Xiong Family laughed and said, "Your Excellency, I really admire you for reaching the Nirvana Realm with your physical body. But you said we would pay the price. I don't know whether you will pay the price by yourself or by the Heaven Palace!"
The You Xiong Family knew it was hopeless to enter the All-Heaven Divine Temple. They weren't afraid of offending Luo Zheng.
Although the Heaven Palace was one of the three orthodoxies of the human race, it couldn't compete with the You Xiong Family in terms of overall size, let alone the Divine Farmer Clan.
"It's not me alone, nor is it the Heavenly Palace. It's the righteous path of the world. Those crooked paths can be awe-inspiring for a while, but they can't be awe-inspiring forever. In the end, they will be annihilated by the long river of history and become insignificant grains of sand," Luo Zheng's voice was powerful and resonant.
He couldn't compete with the You Xiong Family alone, and neither could the Heaven Palace. However, many superpowers in the Mother World had chosen the Path of the End. Those superpowers were potential allies of the Heaven Palace.
"Well said!"
A half-human, half-snake woman of the Nuwa Tribe applauded.
"The Path of the End is the right way!"
"There are always some idiots who want to use some specious reasons to realize their ambitions!"
"I always think Lie Shan is a bad guy!"
Most of these Alien Clans were living beings who believed in the Path of the End.
In fact, the debate about the Great Path was not for these young people. But it didn't mean they didn't know about it.
Luo Zheng said what was on their minds. Of course, they would agree and agree.
Those from the You Xiong Family were not convinced. They still wanted to argue, but Luo Zheng had already lost interest.
People who walked different paths could not make plans together. If they really argued about these reasons, it would take days and nights to finish. He waved his hand at the Ear Mouse and said, "You can clear the area now. I don't want to see these people again."
The Ear Mice were even less interested in these arguments. Since Luo Zheng was here, it was time to open the door. Arguing about these things was a waste of time.
"Squeak! Planer! Come out and clear the area! Those who haven't handed in the Soul Core Token, including the You Xiong Family, Shennong Family, Jinwu Family, and Minyue Family, are not allowed to enter. Everyone else, please gather in front of the Sky Temple! "the Ear Mouse shouted.
Not far away, a dozen Ear Mice each carried a spray bottle. These bottles kept spraying out light blue liquid. After the liquid was sprayed out, it covered the ground in a layer.
This liquid was called "Soul Avoiding Water". It had the same effect as the "Soul Breaking Sand" of the Heavenly Palace and the You Xiong Family's Soul Entangling Silk. It prevented the Yang Soul from leaving the other side.
After being covered in the Soul Avoiding Water, the Ear Mice set up a restriction against the Yang Soul. The entire front of the Sky Temple became a temporary desperate situation. No Yang Soul dared to barge in.
"Since you don't let our Minyue Family enter the Sky Temple, please return the Soul Core Token we handed in earlier," a Minyue Family member said.
The Soul Core Token had already fallen into the hands of the Ear Mouse. It would be extremely difficult to make them spit it out.
But in front of so many foreign races, it was impossible to renege on the debt!
The Ear Mouse rolled his eyes and squeaked, "Change what I just said. The You Xiong Family, Shennong Family, Jinwu Family, and Minyue Family have already handed in the Soul Core Token. They can enter at will, but we are not responsible for their lives."
Luo Zheng was still standing in front of the Sky Temple!

Chapter 2915
Ear Mouse's True Purpose
"Pfft …"
Feng Ge laughed on Luo Zheng's back. She knew that the Ear Mouse clan was very smart, but this was too shameless.
At this time, Ling Shuang suddenly said, "You should come down. How can you keep lying on someone's back?"
Ling Shuang had been unhappy for a long time, but she didn't want to lose her temper just now.
Feng Ge was scolded by Ling Shuang for no reason. She was very unhappy and said coldly, "Who are you? What does it have to do with you that I am willing to be carried by him? "
Feng Ge couldn't see Ling Shuang's face, so she didn't recognize who she was. She only thought that she was a disciple of the Heavenly Palace. How many disciples of the Heavenly Palace could enter Feng Ge's eyes?
"You!" Ling Shuang raised her eyebrows. She was about to call out "Luo Zheng", but she thought that she couldn't reveal her identity, so she held back. She reached out and pinched Luo Zheng's arm. "Hey! Put her down! "
Luo Zheng's mind was not on the two women just now. He didn't expect that the two women behind him would have such a conflict.
He was still in a daze, but Chou Xue reacted quickly. He chuckled and said, "We're all family. Prince Feng Ge, this is Prince Ling Shuang. Just now, Prince Ling Shuang even lent a helping hand …"
"So it was you." Feng Ge was also slightly surprised.
"So what if it was me?" Ling Shuang's momentum was not weaker than Feng Ge's.
Feng Ge's stubbornness flared up. She didn't back down. She leaned her head on Luo Zheng's shoulder and deliberately said, "My soul energy has been sucked dry. I don't have any strength left. I can't come down!"
When Luo Zheng carried her here, she had been replenishing her soul pills. Even though she had lost a considerable amount of soul strength, she was now almost recovered.
Feng Ge's words were exactly what Ling Shuang wanted.
With a smile on her face, Ling Shuang said, "The temple is about to open. Since your soulforce is insufficient, you should withdraw from the other side and have a good rest. Your Highness Feng Ge must take care of your Saint Body. This Temple of Heaven is dangerous …"
Who would have thought that just as she finished speaking, Ling Shuang would jump down from Luo Zheng's back with a 'shua' sound. Even though she was in her Yang Soul form, one could still see that her flexibility was astonishing. She coldly replied, "I don't have the fortune to make Her Highness Ling Shuang care about me. It's just a mere replenishment of my Soul Power!"
As he spoke, Feng Ge took out a Soul Pill and continuously absorbed it into her Yang Soul's body. Her soul's aura also quickly filled up.
"Your Highness Feng Ge, the journey to the Temple of Heaven is fraught with dangers. Your Ten Thousand Gold Body is indeed not suitable to enter," Luo Zheng also advised.
Whether it was Chou Xue or Ling Shuang, neither was in Luo Zheng's plan. However, the matter of the Temple of Heaven would naturally attract the disciples of the Heavenly Palace. Moreover, the conflict caused by Feng Ge was also unexpected by Luo Zheng.
"I don't have a Ten Thousand Gold Body. Compared to someone, I'm not so delicate," Feng Ge's words were full of thorns.
Ling Shuang raised her eyebrows and wanted to retort, but Luo Zheng reached out and pinched her arm. Ling Shuang endured it. The purpose of her and Luo Zheng coming here was not to quarrel with Feng Ge.
The Eared Mouse Clan used the "Soul Repelling Water" to seal the area in front of the temple and set up a barrier above it. Those large clans who had paid their Soul Pills were lined up in front of the temple.
The Liyuan Clan, who had been particularly arrogant at the beginning, had remained silent since Luo Zheng's appearance.
Most of these Liyuan Clan members had rushed over from the Eleventh Layer of Heaven. They had seen Luo Zheng's huge figure with their own eyes. They were full of fear towards Luo Zheng's body, but they were afraid to avoid it. How could they dare to provoke it?
When the various foreign clans were ready, one of the Eared Mice climbed up the steps in front of the Temple of Heaven and stretched out a thin claw. Thin and long lines appeared on this claw.
As it gently pressed its claw in front of the door, the gate of the Temple of Heaven flashed with a metallic luster and slowly opened.
The foreign clans all seemed incomparably anxious. They all wanted to take the lead and enter first, but this area had been set up with the Eared Mice's barrier, so they could only act according to the Eared Mice's orders.
"Okay, you can go in," said the Eared Mouse who opened the gate of the temple.
As soon as he finished speaking, the Liyuan Clan's enormous Yang souls rushed into the divine temple without hesitation.
The other foreign clans also rushed in, each one fighting to be first …
In contrast, the Eared Mice didn't seem to be in a hurry, even though they were the ones who had opened the gate.
More than two thousand Sun Souls had just gathered in front of the temple, but in a short while, they had all entered the temple.
"You guys go in too," Ling Shuang said to Chou Xue.
What she meant was for Chou Xue to quickly take Feng Ge away. She really felt that Feng Ge was an eyesore.
Ling Shuang had been with Luo Zheng ever since she entered the Sea of True Intent. She didn't want anyone else to be there.
However, Chou Xue, Feng Ge, and the other disciples of the Heavenly Palace clearly felt that following Luo Zheng was the best choice. Would they be fools not to hug this thigh?
Luo Zheng didn't move, so they didn't move either.
"I have an agreement with the Eared Mice. I can only bring Ling Shuang into the temple," Luo Zheng explained.
Among the living beings who entered the temple, only Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang didn't pay the Soul Pills.
Feng Ge looked deeply at Luo Zheng. She had a straightforward personality. Since he didn't intend to explore the temple with her, she also stepped into the temple.
Chou Xue cupped his hands towards Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang and said, "Since that's the case, we'll go in first. I still have to thank the two of you for saving me!"
"It's a small matter. Don't mention it," Luo Zheng replied.
After the disciples of the Heavenly Palace had also entered, Luo Zheng also walked up the steps and to the entrance of the Temple of Heaven.
At this time, the Eared Mice surrounded him. One of the Eared Mice asked respectfully, "How should I address this human friend?"
"You can just call me Tianxing," Luo Zheng replied.
The Eared Mice also understood that Luo Zheng wouldn't tell them his real name, so they didn't care about it. One of the Eared Mice asked, "Since Your Excellency Tianxing has entered the Nirvana Realm with your physical body, you must have something extraordinary. I wonder if it's possible for you to destroy the Temple of Heaven?"
"Destroy the Temple of Heaven …"
Luo Zheng could easily tear down the Temple of Heavenly Sunflower of the First Layer of Heaven. The temples built by the You Xiong Clan of the Fourth Layer of Heaven and the buildings built by the Eleventh Layer of Heaven were even worse. They were almost like paper in front of him.
However, Luo Zheng didn't have much confidence in destroying the Temple of Heaven of the Thirteenth Layer of Heaven.
This Temple of Heaven had a faint metallic luster. It seemed that some special metal was mixed in during the construction.
He walked to the door and pressed against the wall, slowly releasing his power.
"Sizzle …"
The wall was obviously stronger than Luo Zheng had expected. Even though Luo Zheng had used 50% of his power, he only left a fingerprint on the surface of the wall.
"The thickness of the wall of this temple is five feet wide. It will be quite difficult to destroy it," Luo Zheng said seriously.
Who knew that when the Eared Mice saw this, their faces would be filled with ecstasy.
"Your Excellency Tianxing can actually make the wall cave in!" the Eared Mouse praised.
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng didn't understand.
"There is a wall blocking the path between the Temple of Heaven and the Hidden Realm. That wall naturally can't be compared to the outer wall of the Temple of Heaven. However, with Your Excellency Tianxing's divine power, there shouldn't be a problem!" the Eared Mouse explained.
The Eared Mice weren't in a hurry because they knew that no Yang soul could destroy that wall.

Chapter 2916
Compared to the other large clans, the Ear Mouse had an incomparable advantage in this regard.
After all, the Ear Mouse controlled most of the transactions in the Paramita Temple. Whether it was the Paramita Key or the Paramita Token, they all knew where it went.
In the past ten years, the Paramita Key of the Temple of Heaven had been very rare, and only three keys had been sold in total. When a strange phenomenon appeared at the back of the Temple of Heaven, the Ear Mouse immediately found the creature who held the Paramita Key, and offered to buy it at an extremely high price.
But when they explored the end of the Temple of Heaven, a wall caused the Ear Mice to fall into despair.
The Sun Soul could not pass through it at all.
Then, the Ear Mice quickly came up with another strategy and began to spread the news. Thus, the Temple of Heaven quickly became known to the major forces of the Mother World.
At this time, the Ear Mice's goal was to earn more Soul Pills. In any case, the wall could not be passed, so they definitely could not enter the Hidden Domain.
But a few days ago, the news from the Fourth and Eleventh Layers of Heaven made the Ear Mice change their strategy again!
They saw hope in breaking through the wall, and this hope was the "Celestial" in front of them.
The Temple of Heaven was built very widely. The entire temple was a hundred times larger than the Sky Sunflower Temple.
After Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang followed the Ear Mice inside, they saw a wide hall. In front of the hall, there was a statue.
The statue was sixty feet tall, with the head of an eagle and the body of a bear. It had an extraordinary aura.
"This is the clan that built the Temple of Heaven?" Luo Zheng asked.
One of the Ear Mice nodded, "The Temple of Heaven is a powerful clan. They can be considered a dominator force."
"Dominator force?" Luo Zheng blinked.
"Yes, a powerful clan that once dominated the entire Primal Chaos. We call them dominator forces," the Ear Mouse laughed, "In comparison, the current super forces of the Mother World are simply too weak …"
Although the Mother World was relatively peaceful now, it was still a situation of power struggle. There had never been a dominator force.
Li Yuan, Li Shan, You Xiong, Shennong, Tian Gong, and so on.
There were a lot of super forces.
There was a large gap between these super forces, but it was not absolute. At the very least, no super force could control the entire Mother World, let alone the entire Primal Chaos.
Hearing the Ear Mouse said this, Luo Zheng thought of the Sky Sunflower Temple.
He then asked, "Where are the Fearful Eye Demons in the Sunflower Temple?"
"It can also be defined as a dominator-level force," Ear Mouse continued Luo Zheng's words and replied, "However, there is still a big gap between the Eye Demon Clan and the Mi Tian Clan. Clans that can leave behind temples in the Nirvana Realm were once considered dominator-level forces."
"Is that so?" Luo Zheng asked 9527 in his mind.
"Sort of, but the Dominator Rank is defined by these rats themselves. Above these forces, there are existences like me," 9527 replied.
Only living beings who could control the truth were the true masters of the chaos.
Luo Zheng thought of what he said outside the temple. A trace of doubt emerged in his heart. He asked Ear Mouse, "Does the Mi Tian Clan also believe in the Dao of the End?"
"Yes." Ear Rat nodded.
"It seems that even the powerful Mi Tian Clan has failed," Luo Zheng said with a look of regret.
Whether it was the Fearful Eye Demon Clan or the Mi Tian Clan, they had once dominated the chaos. They could mobilize more resources and were probably more powerful, but in the end, they still could not escape that needle …
"Squeak, you all live too painfully, and you only blindly repeat the path of your predecessors. Before the End comes, everything still depends on us Ear Mice," Ear Mouse suddenly said.
"Depend on you Ear Mice?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
Ling Shuang also blinked her eyes.
The Ear Mouse Clan should be the most unambitious super force in the Mother World. Other than blindly earning Divine Crystals and Soul Pills, this clan seemed to have no other needs.
No matter how powerful the Nirvana Token, how rare the Nirvana Key, or how many Soul Pills, they could all be exchanged for Divine Crystals …
The Divine Crystals, other than being used as currency, could only be used to replenish the energy of the Nirvana Token. Therefore, this clan's obsession with Divine Crystals had always been incomprehensible to other foreign clans.
"Squeak squeak squeak …"
"Squeak …"
A few Ear Mice suddenly squeaked a few times and stopped this topic.
That Ear Mouse also squeaked and immediately changed the topic.
Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang naturally understood. No matter what, a super force was definitely not as simple as one imagined. Similarly, the Ear Mice should not be underestimated.
Bypassing this statue, they reached the end of the hall. Under the wall, there were 19 semi-circular arches in total.
Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang entered for the first time, so naturally, they did not know how to pass through.
"The nineteen arches all lead to the same path in the end, but the difficulties are all different. The thirteenth and seventeenth are all dead ends. The easiest is the seventh, and the hardest is the nineteenth. Almost all the Yang souls who enter die, but there are quite a number of Yang souls who have unearthed powerful Nirvana Realm tokens." The information provided by the Ear Mouse was worth more than a hundred thousand Soul Elixirs. After all, the more they understood about the temple, the more hope they had of surviving.
It continued to ask Luo Zheng, "May I know which arches Lord Tianhang will choose?"
The Ear Mouse gave the right to choose to Luo Zheng.
"I choose the 19th," Luo Zheng replied.
There were not many things in the Nirvana Realm that could threaten Luo Zheng. Moreover, Luo Zheng's initial goal was to find the Nirvana Tokens. Naturally, he would not let go of this opportunity.
The Ear Mice looked at each other. They did not know much about the 19th. They only knew that this passage was very dangerous. However, this time, Luo Zheng was leading the way. Moreover, by choosing this path, the Ear Mice would also have the opportunity to find some high-grade Nirvana Tokens.
"Then let's go." The Ear Mice also trusted Luo Zheng's strength and did not hesitate too much.
Luo Zheng took the lead and walked at the very front. Ling Shuang and the Ear Mice followed behind.
Behind the arched door was a long and narrow semi-circular passageway. The top of the passageway was uneven and covered with veins that resembled human blood vessels. The veins emitted a faint red light that flickered, sometimes bright, sometimes dim, implying the rhythm of breathing.
After Luo Zheng entered the passage, he did not feel any discomfort, but behind him, Ling Shuang already felt strange.
"Hmm …"
Ling Shuang could not help but hug her arms tightly and lean against Luo Zheng's back.
"What's wrong?" Luo Zheng turned around and asked with concern.
He did not need to worry about his own safety, but he had to protect Ling Shuang.
"I feel that my Soul Power is flowing away …" Ling Shuang said.
Luo Zheng looked at the Ear Mice. The Ear Mouse said, "After entering the Temple of Heaven, the Soul Power will continue to flow away. This is the rule of this temple. It's just that before entering the passage, the speed of the loss was very slow. Now, the speed of the loss has increased.
Ling Shuang nodded and took out a handful of Soul Elixirs and put them into her Sun Soul to replenish her Soul Power.

Chapter 2917
The Face on the Wall
"What's going on with this tunnel …"
Out of curiosity, Luo Zheng reached out and pinched the uneven wall.
He felt something soft in his hand, as if it was some kind of animal's internal organs.
"We're not too sure either. Squeak, rumor has it that the nineteen passages in the Divine Temple of Heaven are living creatures, and we're all passing through their stomachs," one of the mice said.
There were too many things gathered on the other side of the world. Among them, there were many things that had never been recorded. It was almost impossible to fully understand everything.
The Ear Mice and Ling Shuang had to absorb soul pellets to replenish their lost soul power every time they moved forward.
As they continued to move forward, the blood-red luster of the patterns above the passageway became brighter and brighter. Sometimes, the entire passageway was illuminated in blood-red, and sometimes, it would dim and become pitch-black. The atmosphere gradually became strange.
Even though Luo Zheng was leading the way, the Ear Mice and Ling Shuang still felt the pressure and were secretly on guard.
"Ahhhh …"
At this moment, they suddenly heard a series of screams coming from the front.
"What happened?!"
"It should be the creature that entered the passage before us!"
"What happened to them?"
Quite a lot of non-humankind creatures had entered the All-Heaven Divine Temple.
Some of the foreign races who were aware of the situation knew that the nineteenth passageway was extremely dangerous, so they naturally avoided it. However, there were bound to be some among the two thousand foreign races who didn't know about it.
"I'll go take a look!"
Luo Zheng took a big stride forward along the passageway.
After about two thousand feet, he saw faces with closed eyes floating on both sides of the wall!
These faces were of different sizes and shapes. They seemed to belong to all kinds of races. Among them, Luo Zheng even saw a few human faces. The only thing they had in common was that they all had an expression of extreme pain and were letting out low growls of "Wu Wu".
"The screams just now didn't come from these faces …"
As he took a few more steps forward and stepped into the corner of the tunnel, he saw seven or eight Yang souls being grabbed by hands and pulled towards the wall with all their might.
There were also many "faces" on the walls in this area. The eyes on these faces were open.
"A member of the Liyuan Clan?"
The Liyuan Tribe was so large that they could practically fill up the entire tunnel. Luo Zheng was naturally able to tell at a glance.
At this moment, the sun souls of the Liyuan clansmen had all been pulled into the walls by those hands. These sun souls had all used their trump cards, and their soul force was constantly surging through the passageway. However, no matter what they did, they were unable to break free from those ghastly white hands!
In the blink of an eye, the Liyuan Clan member was dragged into the wall.
The last Liyuan clansman discovered Luo Zheng. He stretched out his remaining hand and pointed at Luo Zheng as he cried out, "Save, save me …"
Although Luo Zheng didn't have a good impression of the people of the Liyuan Tribe, when he saw the Liyuan Tribe member's outstretched hand, he still instinctively rushed forward and grabbed his arm.
Under Luo Zheng's fierce tug, he failed to pull the Liyuan clansman out of the wall.
The thick arm of the Liyuan clansman was actually torn off by Luo Zheng!
Since the Yang Soul Body had left the main body, it could exist as a separate soul, just like the soul swords that Chou had sacrificed himself for.
However, if the consciousness of Chou's main body disappeared, the soul swords would also dissipate.
The Liyuan clansman's arm remained in Luo Zheng's hand for a breath of time, before it transformed into a wisp of purple smoke and disappeared!
As Luo Zheng frowned, the faces on the walls on both sides of him opened their eyes. At the same time, one hand after another reached out!
These hands only had three fingers, but each finger was a foot long and completely snow-white. It was unknown what kind of creature they were.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
In the instant they drilled out of the walls, they all rushed towards Luo Zheng to grab him.
Ka ka ka …
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng's body was grabbed by seven or eight hands, and he was dragged towards the side of the wall.
"Not bad … but if you want to drag me in, I'm afraid you're dreaming," Luo Zheng said coldly.
Luo Zheng's body suddenly twisted, and his right hand spun like a wheel. The hands that were grabbing onto his body all broke apart, turning into pieces of broken bones that protruded out of the wall.
Perhaps the things in the wall also knew that Luo Zheng was not to be trifled with, for the broken bones all retreated back into the wall.
"I want to see what's inside the wall!"
Luo Zheng grabbed a piece of retreating bone and tried to pull it out of the wall. Unfortunately, the bone was very brittle, and it broke with a single tug.
Wu wu wu …
The faces floating on the wall began to growl again.
As they let out a hair-raising sound, they actually began to move along the wall behind Luo Zheng. Ling Shuang and the Ear Mice were in that direction!
"You're courting death!"
Luo Zheng dared to choose the 19th passage, which was the most difficult passage, because he had considerable confidence in himself. However, Ling Shuang and the Ear Mice were still very weak. He naturally would not allow these big and small faces on the wall to get close to them!
Dong!
Luo Zheng chased after them, channeled his strength, and smashed a fist into the faces on the wall.
The power of this punch was extremely terrifying, and the entire passage shook violently.
However, the faces were not hurt at all. They just dived into the wall, swam for a distance, and then reappeared. Some of the faces' hollow eyes stared at Luo Zheng, with a hint of mockery.
Dong! Dong! Dong!
Luo Zheng followed these faces closely, and smashed the wall several times in a row.
The walls of the passage were very flexible, and completely dissolved Luo Zheng's strength. Naturally, the "faces" were not hurt at all.
"Tianhang! What's going on? "
Ling Shuang heard the loud noise and knew that Luo Zheng had made his move. However, there was a patch of red in front of them, and their divine senses were also blocked, so she and the Ear Mice could not see clearly.
"Watch out for both sides of the wall!"
Luo Zheng shouted as he ran.
"Squeak, what's going on?"
"It seems that some of the faces are flying along the wall?"
"Those are faces! And the faces of our Ear Mice … Squeak squeak squeak! "
As the saying goes, "as timid as mice", the Ear Mice were not very brave.
The reason why they dared to enter the Temple of Heaven was that they relied on information that far exceeded that of the other alien races. This time, they even had Luo Zheng's protection, which was why they dared to enter the 19th passage.
Seeing the faces moving at high speed, they panicked.
When the faces moved along the wall to Ling Shuang's side, a white three-fingered hand broke through the wall and grabbed at them.
One of the Ear Mice was too close to the wall, and before it could react, it was dragged into the wall. It only had time to let out a miserable squeak.
"Get away from the wall!"
"Don't get caught by those hands!"
"Spiritual Shield!"
A small round shield flashed in the claws of one of the Ear Mice. The small round shield shone brightly, and spread out around the Ear Mice, protecting Ling Shuang as well.
The Spiritual Shield was an extremely powerful soul magic treasure, only slightly weaker than Ling Shuang's Soul Oracle Necklace.

Chapter 2918
The strength of these hands was nothing to Luo Zheng, but his Yang soul could not resist it.
Even if Ling Shuang's Yang soul entered her indigo, she could not struggle out of it!
Ling Shuang saw one of the Ear Mice being dragged into the wall and stopped breathing. She knew what would happen.
In a moment of desperation, the soul necklace on Ling Shuang's chest glowed, and the small soul sword flew out and slashed at the arm.
"Crack!"
The small soul sword, which was almost always successful, only left a shallow cut on the hand!
Since this attack did not cut off the arm, there was no more chance. Ling Shuang was directly dragged toward the wall.
Just as her body was about to sink into the wall, Luo Zheng's figure had already rushed over. He grabbed the arm and squeezed hard. "Pa!" The bones suddenly shattered!
"Wuwuwu …"
One of the faces on the wall cried out in pain and was drawn into the wall.
However, more faces appeared on both sides of the wall, and more hands reached out from the wall.
Luo Zheng hugged Ling Shuang and pulled her to the center of the passage. At the same time, he said, "Stay by my side!"
When the Ear Mice saw that the spiritual shield could not protect them, they were all scared pale.
"Squeak, squeak, squeak, squeak …"
They screamed desperately and nestled around Luo Zheng, wishing that they could drill into Luo Zheng's body.
"Wuwuwu …"
Countless hands broke out of the wall, almost occupying the entire passage.
Luo Zheng ignored them, but as long as they dared to reach for Ling Shuang and the Ear Mice, Luo Zheng would use his hand to chop them off one by one.
"Crack, crack, crack …"
More and more white bones broke on the ground, almost reaching Luo Zheng's knees.
"Your Excellency Tianhang, squeak, squeak, squeak … Over here!"
"Behind you, help!"
"Cut it off quickly!"
The Ear Mice were all panicking. They were completely different from how they were outside the Temple of Heaven.
Ling Shuang, on the other hand, was very calm.
She was protected in Luo Zheng's arms, but she did not panic at all.
Luo Zheng had promised to protect her, and he would definitely do it. After walking together with Luo Zheng in Paramita, she already had blind trust in him.
The whole process lasted for about the time it took for three incense sticks to burn. The broken hand bones around Luo Zheng almost reached Luo Zheng's waist.
No more hands reached out from the wall, leaving only faces floating on the wall, looking at Luo Zheng with empty eyes.
"Squeak squeak, it's finally gone!"
"The nineteen paths are really dangerous!"
"Lord Tianhang is too powerful. Squeak squeak, he's our best friend!"
Luo Zheng couldn't help but roll his eyes when he heard the Ear Mice's flattery. He and the Ear Mice were far from being friends.

One of the Ear Mice observed for a while before saying, "These faces should belong to the legendary Enchanters, right?"
"What's an Enchanter?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"I know," Ling Shuang answered. "Those who can't return from the Paramita are collectively called Enchanters."
The soul could die in the Paramita, and the body would become a pure shell.
However, some souls didn't die in the Paramita, but stayed there forever for some reason, unable to wake up for eternity. Such people were called Enchanters.
There were a few such Enchanters in the Heavenly Palace, and Ling Shuang had heard of them.
"Their Yang soul is enslaved in the wall forever. How pitiful." Luo Zheng stared at the faces and sighed.
The Ear Mice weren't in the mood to pity those things. They stretched out their hands, and a small blue triangle appeared in their hands. They began to collect the broken bones on the ground.
The strength of these arms was amazing, and they could be used as a good Paramita token to be traded. Naturally, the Ear Mice wouldn't let them go.
After collecting all the broken bones, the Ear Mice bowed to a mouse face on the wall, and then left with Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang.
Just as they moved, the faces on the wall moved with them.
After they lost their arms, they could no longer launch attacks and could only stare helplessly from the wall. Even so, they still followed closely behind Luo Zheng on both sides and constantly stared at him while emitting wails that were filled with unwillingness and resentment.
After Luo Zheng led the group forward for more than two thousand feet, the passage became wider, and the whole passage became an upward path. The "blood vessels" densely covering the top of the passage gradually became thicker!
"What is that!" Ling Shuang looked up and said.
Luo Zheng stared, and his eyes flashed slightly. "This is … a heart? What kind of creature's heart is this? "
At the top of the tunnel hung three hearts that were half the size of a human. These hearts were still beating, and all the thick blood vessels were gathered on these hearts.
Every time the heart beat, the red light dimmed a little, and with another beat, the blood-red light shone again!
"Squeak … Oh my god!"
"This is …"
"Squeak … Squeak … Squeak …"
At first, the Ear Mice were talking in the human language, but after a few squeaks, they began to talk in the Ear Mice's language!
Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang quietly looked at each other, and their faces showed dissatisfaction.
The Ear Mice had handled countless Nirvana Realm tokens, and naturally, they had an incomparable advantage in identifying them.
These guys must have recognized this thing, and obviously, they didn't want Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang to know, so they talked in the human language.
Luo Zheng had seen the shrewdness and cunning of the Ear Mice.
Who knew what they were plotting, but since they didn't want Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang to hear, they naturally had a purpose.
Luo Zheng's face sank, and he said, "Since you don't want us to know, you can go on your own. We will leave the Temple of Heaven now."
Without Luo Zheng, it would be a problem for them to leave the passage alive, not to mention they still wanted to explore the Dark Domain!
These three hearts were indeed no small matter. As the Ear Mice clan, they wanted to get the most benefits first, and they were indeed discussing whether to tell Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang the truth.
The Ear Mice were talking non-stop, and suddenly became anxious when they heard Luo Zheng's words.
"Squeak! That's not what we meant! "

The Ear Mice explained to Luo Zheng, and they were obviously a little excited.
Although the Ear Mice were a little confused, Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang still understood.

Chapter 2919
Terrifying ability.
The Paramita Realm had always been a mystical place.
The strongest thing about these Paramita Tokens was not the Paramita Energy they provided. Instead, it was the various abilities they provided.
The strengthening-type Nirvana Token usually provided the most powerful Nirvana Power, just like how the Strength God Totem's Nirvana Power far surpassed the other Nirvana Tokens of the Thirteen Levels of Heaven. However, the Strength God Totem was not popular among the Nirvana Tokens.
On one hand, it was restricted by the fleshy body, preventing Paramita Realm experts from the Color Realm from unleashing its full strength. On the other hand, pure strength was easily restrained.
But Luo Zheng's body was unique, and the Mighty God Totem was naturally compatible with it. Hence, he chose the Mighty God Totem without hesitation.
The two most popular types were the mystical type and the descending type of Nirvana Token.
These two types usually had unfathomable abilities.
"The Heart of the Accomplice is a mysterious Nirvana token that was once possessed by a member of the Sirius Clan," the Ear Rat explained in a methodical manner.
The Celestial Wolf Race was strong individually, but they were few in number. They could not fight against those super organizations.
The Celestial Wolf Race once resided in the Star Wolf State and rarely participated in the conflicts outside.
But several tens of thousands of years ago, the super organization War Hounds Race invaded the Star Wolf State.
The Celestial Wolf Race naturally resisted with their lives. But the War Hounds Race was a super organization and was much stronger than the Celestial Wolf Race.
Especially the Patriarch of the War Hounds Race, 'King Xun'. He was ranked among the apex experts in the Origin World. Almost no one from the Celestial Wolf Race could resist him. They were forced to retreat. Even the Patriarch of the Celestial Wolf Race was killed by King Xun.
But at the final juncture, a member of the Celestial Wolf Race stood out and blocked the path of 'King Xun'. He even killed 'King Xun' and forced the War Hounds Race to retreat from the Star Wolf State. And this member was only at the Early Stage Chaotic Origin Realm.
"Early Stage Chaotic Origin Realm?" Luo Zheng was stunned for a moment before saying: "What is the strength of that King Xun?"
Ear Mouse pondered for a moment before saying: "He is not as strong as the Sky Palace's East Emperor, but the gap should not be too far.
"What!" Luo Zheng's pupils dilated slightly.
The Chaotic Origin Realm was a huge hurdle. After crossing it, one's strength would increase tremendously. Luo Zheng would definitely cross this hurdle too.
But he had never thought that he could fight against an existence like Eastern Emperor with his Chaotic Origin Realm strength. That was not a battle on the same level.
"Impossible. The reason why King Xun died was because the Heavenly Wolf Clan gathered the power of their entire clan and lured King Xun into an ancient trap. That was how they managed to kill him," Ling Shuang refuted.
Ear Mouse shook his head and said confidently, "Most of the outside world thinks so, but we, Ear Mouse, know the inside story. There is no mistake."
The humankind rarely paid attention to the conflicts between the non-humankind. Therefore, the information Ling Shuang got from the Heaven Palace was quite different.
'How could an Early Stage Chaotic Origin Expert defeat a powerful existence like King Xun? This is unbelievable! "Luo Zheng was still skeptical of the Ear Mice's words.
The Ear Mice stared at the three hearts at the ceiling of the cavern and said, "Our news is that that Early Stage Chaotic Origin Celestial Wolf Clansman relied on this Heart of Accomplice."
"Even if this heart is stronger, how much Paramita Energy does it possess? Even if it has a Divine Jun Energy of 2,000 to 3,000, it is impossible for it to injure an expert like King Xun. "Ling Shuang refuted.
"Killing King Xun did not rely on the Divine Jun Energy. Instead, it was the ability of the Heart of Accomplice." Ear Mouse explained, "This heart can turn a target into his accomplice!"
The Celestial Wolf Clan's patriarch had been killed, so the War Hounds Clan naturally went on a killing spree. King Xun was unstoppable in the Celestial Wolf World, causing the experts of the Celestial Wolf Clan to fall one after another.
After King Xun charged into the tribe of the Heavenly Wolf Clan, a rhythmic heartbeat sound could be heard from the tribe.
This heartbeat sound could be heard clearly by all living beings from the two great clans. As for the mice hiding in the Star Wolf Continent, they naturally heard it as well.
Just as everyone was puzzled, a Heavenly Wolf Clansman stood out and charged towards King Xun.
This Heavenly Wolf Clansman was merely at the Early Stage Chaotic Origin Realm. Why would King Xun place him in his eyes?
With a casual grab, King Xun tore the Heavenly Wolf Clansman to pieces.
However, after killing the Heavenly Wolf Clansman, a Paramita Token floated out of the clansman's body. This Paramita Token was the Heart of Accomplice.
The heart floated above King Xun's head.
King Xun did not know what the heart was. He casually punched the heart and immediately sent it flying millions of kilometers away.
However, even as the heart gradually flew away, the heartbeat continued reverberating around King Xun. The other living beings could hear it clearly.
Not long after, the heart gradually returned and continued floating above King Xun's head.
King Xun attacked again in an attempt to shatter the Heart of Accomplice. However, the heart could not be destroyed at all. No matter how King Xun used all sorts of means, even attempting to banish it into the void, it continued to appear beside King Xun. Its heartbeat became faster and faster!
When the heartbeat reached a limit, it suddenly stopped. As for King Xun, he plummeted from the sky as his body stiffened and he died.
Regardless of whether it was his soul or his body, they both silently disappeared …
An existence like King Xun had a fleshy body that had reached an unfathomable stage. His soul had also reached an unfathomable realm. Such a death was simply too bizarre.
"King Xun killed himself. That Early Stage Chaotic Origin Realm Heavenly Wolf Clansman defined King Xun as an accomplice. Since the Heavenly Wolf Clansman died, King Xun had to die as an accomplice." That Ear Mouse said.
"It sounds similar to the Life Contract in the Life Divine Dao." Luo Zheng said solemnly.
After executing the Life Contract, the lives of two parties would be connected. When one party died, the life of the other party would wither too.
"Heh, that Life Contract is just a small trick. It is impossible for it to be compared with the Heart of Accomplice!" Ear Mouse sneered.
Luo Zheng nodded in agreement.
The energy level of the Deity Dao was still too low. Techniques like the Life Contract could be easily destroyed. It was hard for it to work on ordinary Paramita Realm experts, much less an expert on the level of King Xun.
This Heart of Accomplice could actually drag an apex expert from the Origin World down with it. That was truly frightening!
"Our Ear Mouse Clan had once investigated for a very long time. We had investigated the past of that Heavenly Wolf Clansman in the dark, yet we were unable to find the origin of this Heart of Accomplice. In the end, this Paramita Keepsake was defined as a unique item in the entire Origin World. But now, this definition has to be changed." Ear Mouse said.
Since three Hearts of Accomplice had appeared before them, it was naturally not a unique item.
"If it is really as you say, this Paramita Keepsake is too frightening!" Ling Shuang's voice turned solemn.

Chapter 2920
Regardless of whether the heart of the accomplice was unique or not, such an ability was extremely terrifying.
Three hearts of the accomplice could be exchanged for the lives of any three peerless powerhouses.
It was a great weapon for any superpower!
Luo Zheng, Ling Shuang, and even the Ear Mice were tempted.

Ling Shuang and the Ear Mice already knew how powerful the nineteenth passage was. Any slight movement would raise their alertness.

As the blood vortexes were spinning, eight huge bloody hands reached out from it and slapped down on Luo Zheng.

Although his body was strong enough to move unhindered in the Nirvana Realm, Luo Zheng knew that he was not invincible.
His body could not travel to the Nirvana Realm after the Ancient God Physique Refinery Technique was sealed. Before that, many Aragami had entered the Nirvana Realm with their body. But where did they go?
The Aragami were the same as the True Gods. Once they completed their cultivation, their lifespan would be endless.
At that time, the number of Aragami was about the same as the number of True Gods now. They could be found all over the mother world. But now, none of them could be found? Not only did the Nirvana Realm not exist, but the mother world was also not found. It did not make sense.
According to Luo Zheng's speculation, the Nirvana Realm must have some means to subdue the Aragami.
The first bloody hand slapped down on Luo Zheng at an extremely fast speed. Luo Zheng blocked the attack with both of his hands.
"Bang!"
"Bang!"

"Wu wu wu …"
The "faces" on the wall made a collective sound. It sounded like they were crying and mocking at the same time.
"I'm fine." Luo Zheng left the wall and walked towards the bloody hands.
An ordinary Nirvana Realm cultivator would definitely not be able to withstand 300 Godly Jun of force on their physical body. However, when it hit Luo Zheng, it only made his blood boil.
It was a pity that he couldn't use the stone sealing solution. Otherwise, he could take it even if it was doubled, let alone 300 Godly Jun of force!
Hearing Luo Zheng's answer, the Ear Mice were slightly relieved. They were also shocked. If that slap had landed on their bodies, their Yang Soul would have been turned into ashes instantly. The difference between the physical body and the Yang Soul was too great.
"Wu …"
When the huge face above the passageway saw Luo Zheng walking towards it again, there was a hint of warning in its wail.
Since he had already come this far, what could scare Luo Zheng away?
After taking a few steps, he suddenly exerted force on his feet and rushed towards one of the bloody hands like an arrow.
"Whoosh!"
The palm of that bloody hand was about the same size as Luo Zheng. When it saw Luo Zheng rushing towards it, the bloody hand suddenly flipped over and slapped towards Luo Zheng.
"Totem of the Mighty God!"
In the independent courtyard of the Heart Flow Sword Sect, the Totem of the Mighty God contained in Luo Zheng's inner world exploded with a powerful force. The objects placed in the room were instantly crushed by this force!
Luo Zheng didn't need to expend too much energy to deal with the Yang Soul in the Nirvana Realm.
However, when dealing with the Abyss Spirit Knights, Luo Zheng had no choice but to activate the power of the Nirvana Realm token.
These bloody hands in front of him were no small matter. Luo Zheng naturally couldn't underestimate them!
"Whoosh!"
Since he couldn't use the stone sealing solution, Luo Zheng didn't dare to activate the Totem of the Mighty God at full strength. At this moment, the power that exploded out was 500 Godly Jun!
The power of 500 Godly Jun in the Nirvana Realm was already extremely terrifying.
The Ear Mice felt the pressure of this power and had no choice but to continue retreating. Their Yang Souls were exposed to this pressure and were extremely uncomfortable.
"Boom!"
The bloody hand collided with Luo Zheng's body, and blood splattered everywhere.
A large hole appeared in the center of the bloody hand. Luo Zheng forcibly squeezed through the center of the bloody hand!
"Wu …"
The bloody hand seemed to be able to feel pain.
After being directly smashed by Luo Zheng, it kept trembling. The face in the passage let out a sound that sounded like crying and laughing. The other seven bloody hands slapped at Luo Zheng from different directions.
"But this is just a little bit of skill …"
The corner of Luo Zheng's mouth curved up.
He couldn't leave the Nirvana Realm in the temple. Therefore, before Luo Zheng entered the temple, he had made careful preparations and poured a lot of Divine Crystals into the Nirvana Realm token.
The power of the Mighty God Totem of the Nirvana Realm was very abundant. He didn't have any worries and wantonly extracted the power of the Nirvana Realm!
"Boom!"
"Boom!"
"Boom …"
He moved forward and left. Every time a palm slapped at him, Luo Zheng would suddenly exert force. He would either use his elbow or his fists. He would either penetrate the palm or break the wrist!
The screams of the face at the top of the passage didn't stop.
"Wu wu wu …"
After a while, the eight bloody hands were broken by Luo Zheng one by one.
Six of the bloody hands retreated into the blood vortex, and the two that were cut off by Luo Zheng were gently jumping on the ground. The two huge hands gradually lost their vitality.
"Done!"
Luo Zheng turned his head and said to Ling Shuang.
"Squeak squeak, take these two bloody hands," one of the Ear Mice said to himself. He was about to take out his Sumeru Ring to take them.
"Wait," Luo Zheng suddenly said, "I cut off these two bloody hands. Naturally, I will take them."
These Ear Mice took the broken bones for themselves. Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang didn't say anything. The two of them didn't come here to collect junk. They didn't have the same desire for soul pills and Divine Crystals as the Ear Mice. Naturally, they turned a blind eye and let them take it.

Chapter 2921
The Pattern
The Ear Mice had no way of resisting these blood hands, so it was only natural that Luo Zheng would take them away.

The Ear Mice were also very tactful and didn't dare to say anything.

After all, he chose to cooperate with the Ear Mice only to help them break through the walls. As for what they could obtain from the Temple of Heaven, it was all up to their own ability.
Without Luo Zheng, they wouldn't have been able to pass through the nineteen passages, let alone see the three hearts.
The Liyuan clan's overall strength was stronger than the Ear Mice, but they would also be trapped in the passage walls, never to be reincarnated.

Although the hearts of accomplices were strong, they had to sacrifice their own lives. In other words, a life for a life. This kind of ability could not be used unless it was absolutely necessary.
If he were to bear this Nirvana Realm Token, it would take up one of Luo Zheng's True Meaning of Dao. After all, he could only bear three Nirvana Realm Tokens at the moment.
As for the two bloody hands, although the Ear Mice did not help to evaluate them, Luo Zheng reckoned that they should be a top-grade Nirvana Token, which did not quite meet Luo Zheng's requirements.
Before leaving, Luo Zheng glanced at the giant face above the passage again.
The giant face continued to whine, trying to convey some kind of message. Even though the face was huge, it was just like the other faces. After losing those hands, it was powerless.

In the Eleven Paths.
On both sides of the passage, skeletons half the height of a human sat upright.
The legs and backs of these skeletons had already fused into the walls, as if they were embedded in them.
The eyes of each skeleton flickered with a faint light, silently watching the Yang souls in the passage.
"Your Highness Feng Ge, please slow down," Chou Xinghe reminded from the side.
Even though in front of the Temple of Heaven, Feng Ge had suffered a big blow from the You Xiong Clan, it did not affect her state of mind at all. Once she decided on something, she would definitely press forward.
"It's okay," Feng Ge replied coldly.
There was still a faint blue light flickering under her feet. That was the You Xiong Clan's Soul-entangling Silk.
No one in the Temple of Heaven could leave the Nirvana Realm. Whether or not they were bound by the Soul-entangling Silk naturally did not mean much. She was also not in a hurry to purify the Soul-entangling Silk. After a period of time, the Soul-entangling Silk would naturally lose its effect.
"Kekeke …"
As the disciples of the Heavenly Palace continued to go deeper into the passage, the lower jaw of a small skeleton embedded in the wall suddenly opened and closed. From the skeleton's mouth, a thin hundred-legged worm suddenly drilled out!
"Careful!"
Chou Xinghe stood behind Feng Ge, on full alert.
Seeing this strange phenomenon appear, Chou Xinghe immediately rushed towards Feng Ge.
But Feng Ge's figure suddenly flashed. His right hand swept through the air and a golden light whipped towards the hundred-legged worm. With a crisp "pa", the hundred-legged worm had already been reduced to ashes by the whip created by the Golden Crow Divine Fire.
"You're too cowardly," Feng Ge shook her head.
In the past, she had regarded Chou Xinghe as her only worthy opponent in the Heavenly Palace.
Chou Xinghe shrugged and smiled helplessly.
If it weren't for you being so reckless, he wouldn't be so nervous at all. Who knows what is hidden in the Temple of Heaven. If you're not careful, it's possible that everyone will be wiped out!
"Da!"
At this moment, a faint sound came from behind Chou Xinghe.
The leg of a Heavenly Palace disciple was bitten by a hundred-legged worm as thick as a thumb.
The Heavenly Palace disciple's Sun Soul, which had been flickering with purple light, began to turn pitch-black. He didn't even have any reaction before his entire Sun Soul turned into a cloud of black smoke and drifted along the top of the passage.
"Careful!"
"Don't get bitten by the hundred-legged worm! It's very poisonous! "
"Look, those little skeletons have opened their mouths!"
The little skeletons on both sides of the passage opened their jaws and hundred-legged worms crawled out, rushing over from the walls and the ground.
Seeing the Heavenly Palace disciple's fate after being bitten by the hundred-legged worm, everyone's hair stood on end!
"All of you, follow me," Feng Ge said from the front.
As she rushed forward, her hands constantly waved. The Golden Whip of Divine Fire extended from her hands and lashed out continuously. Chou Xinghe and the other Heavenly Palace disciples also rushed forward with her!
… …
In the seventh passage.
Under the protection of her brothers, the little human girl proceeded forward cautiously.
There were too few of them and they were also weaker, so they naturally chose the seventh passage, which was the easiest.
In fact, of the nineteen passages, the vast majority of the foreign races chose this passage. Those foreign races had already cleared the obstacles in front of them and were walking at the back, so they naturally weren't in any danger.
Every hundred feet or so, they would send someone to squat in a corner and use their soul force to draw patterns on the wall.
"How many is this?" One of them asked.

Chapter 2922
"That clue should be correct, do your best" one of the human Yang souls said.

The little girl's big brother nodded, "En, the Ear Mice want to use that body to destroy the wall, which will inevitably lead to an unimaginable disaster"
"Then won't they all die? What about that body? "the little girl asked again.
"It will also die" the little girl's big brother said indifferently.
Hearing her big brother's words, the little girl felt a bit depressed.
Hearing the body mention the Nine Li tribe, the little girl felt a sense of closeness. She felt that this person was somehow related to the Nine Li tribe.
It would be a pity to die like this in the Mi Tian Temple.
"Should we remind him?" the little girl asked.
Her big brother sneered, "Let's not talk about whether he will believe us or not, how will we explain the origin of this clue? Tell him that we are descendants of the Nine Li tribe and expose our identities? "
Hearing her big brother say that, she immediately shut up.
Ever since the Nine Li tribe was destroyed, they had spent most of their time in hiding.
In the past few years, they had finally calmed down a bit, but they had always been living in the shadows.
It was difficult to expose their identities on the other side of the river, so they could naturally cultivate in peace. However, the identity of the Nine Li tribe was extremely sensitive, so they could not be exposed under any circumstances.
The group of people continued to move forward. As they passed through the passageway, they drew a series of strange diagrams.

This underground passage was not long, only about a thousand feet long. At the end of the passage was a basement, and there was a row of strangely shaped large stones in the basement.
These stones were very smooth, but there were oval depressions in the center. These depressions seemed to have something embedded in them, but now they were empty.

Buzz, buzz!

After six or seven consecutive saber strikes, Luo Zheng could only roll on the ground.

If it weren't for Luo Zheng holding the fort, even Huo Ze and the others would probably have retreated. They were not afraid of risks, but it would be too stupid to take a risk of certain death.

Earlier, Luo Zheng had suggested returning the crystals. After all, 9527 had said that the creatures in the Dark Domain that could make candles were not ordinary. However, no one was willing to do so, so Luo Zheng did not force them.
Now that they could not leave, their mentality was naturally different.
When they wanted to save their lives, everything they had was unnecessary, let alone these crystals of unknown use.
Therefore, they took turns to return to the hut and took out the crystal stones from the sumeru ring and returned them to the display shelf. After all the crystal stones were returned, everyone left the wooden door again and stepped onto the fork in the road.
Huo Ze walked at the front and was the first to turn into the fork.
Luo Zheng and the others breathed heavily at this moment. They stared at Huo Ze's movements. If he disappeared in front of the wooden door again, they would be in big trouble. They would be trapped here forever!
"I'm coming …" Huo Ze waved his hands, and there was a hint of joy in his voice.
Everyone's hearts were immediately put at ease.
"Let's go! We shouldn't stay here for too long, "Luo Zheng said. What he worried most was that the owner of the cabin would come back.
Since there was such an inhibition here, it meant that the owner of the wooden house was not hospitable. The people of the Jiuli Tribe and the disciples of the Heavenly Palace were also well aware of this. They all went down the branch road and prepared to return to the wooden bridge.
Just as Huo Ze was the first to step onto the wooden bridge, he suddenly felt that something was wrong. His entire body suddenly bounced backwards and even knocked down two Jiuli tribesmen onto the ground!
"Hua!"
A huge red snake head suddenly appeared and bit the top of the wooden bridge.
Fortunately, Huo Ze reacted quickly. Otherwise, he would have been swallowed by now!
The Red Python was accidentally thrown off the wooden bridge by Luo Zheng. Although the bottom of the wooden bridge was very deep, the Red Python would not fall to its death.
It climbed all the way up along the mountain wall below and had been hiding in the darkness all this time.
It had been lurking for so long just to wait for Luo Zheng and the others to come out. It did not expect Huo Ze's perception to be so sharp that he could avoid its fatal attack.
The others were also startled when they saw the Red Python suddenly rush out.
This thing was really haunting them. It had been guarding here all this time!
The people of the Jiuli Tribe and the disciples of the Heavenly Palace all desperately retreated and went straight to the fork road.
The Red Python also crossed the wooden bridge and stretched out its long snake head to bite the last two Jiuli tribesmen!
Just now, in order to avoid the Red Python, Huo Ze directly knocked down the last two Jiuli tribesmen. The two Jiuli tribesmen naturally retreated slower. When they were about to fall into the snake's mouth, a black shadow suddenly fell from the sky!
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng jumped up from the back and stimulated the power of the Rage God at the same time.
His body, which contained the power of hundreds of Godly Jun, was still enough to make the Red Python suffer. The snake's mouth, which was originally open, was forcibly closed by Luo Zheng's feet.
"Go! I can't hold on for long! "Luo Zheng said to the two Jiuli tribesmen.
Luo Zheng had fought with the Red Python on the wooden bridge. Although Luo Zheng threw it off the wooden bridge when it was unprepared, the strength of the Red Python was actually higher than Luo Zheng's.
"Swoosh!"
How could the Red Python let Luo Zheng step on its head?
Its tail was raised high. After winding around the wooden bridge, it suddenly turned into a thick whip and directly lashed at Luo Zheng.
When the disciples of the Heavenly Palace and the Jiuli tribesmen retreated, they felt the power contained in the snake's tail. Their faces were extremely ugly.
The power contained in the tail probably reached the power of thousands of Godly Jun. Not to mention in the Nirvana Realm, even if they were in the Mother World, they would probably be smashed into pieces by the power of thousands of Godly Jun!
How could Luo Zheng dare to face the Red Python's tail?
He somersaulted backward and narrowly avoided the whip of the snake's tail. However, the Red Python's reaction was also fast. After the snake's tail circled a small half circle in the air, it continued to lash at Luo Zheng again!
"Swoosh!"
Luo Zheng's huge body was still in the air and he couldn't move to dodge the attack.
Seeing that he was about to be hit by the snake's tail, Luo Zheng quickly released the power of the Rage God. His huge body quickly shrunk and he just happened to avoid the sweep of the snake's tail!
"Puff!"
Luo Zheng, who had landed on the ground, also retreated quickly into the fork in the road.
The Red Python had been waiting under the wooden bridge for so long, but it didn't swallow anyone. It was very angry and simply left the wooden bridge and went straight to Luo Zheng and the others.
Luo Zheng and the others had nowhere to hide. They climbed all the way up and ran toward the wooden house again.
But at this moment, a strange scene appeared.
When the Red Python crawled all the way to the fork in the road, it stopped abruptly. Its huge snake head only dared to move left and right at the fork, and it didn't dare to climb up one more step!
"Woo …"
In the sound of the Red Python, there was even a trace of fear hidden in it. Its whole body began to retreat gently.
"This Red Python doesn't dare to come up!"
"It's actually scared! Haha! "
"…"
Seeing this scene, several disciples of the Heavenly Palace stood on the fork in the road above and laughed at it.
They were chased by this thing all the way and almost lost their lives several times. Of course, they were happy to see this scene.

Chapter 2936
Twin Blue Star
The wooden bridge was the only way out of this damned place. The giant serpent had been blocking the crossroads, so they really couldn't do anything.
When the disciples of the Heavenly Palace heard this, they suddenly stopped talking …
"What should we do now?" Lan Qing asked in a low voice.

Who knew when the python would return to the wooden bridge? The journey back was so long that the probability of returning safely was too small.
Ling Shuang stood beside Luo Zheng. Her face was not as gloomy as other people. As long as Luo Zheng was safe and with her, no matter how many monsters there were in this world, there seemed to be nothing to be afraid of.
"Should I lead it away?" Luo Zheng asked.
Ling Shuang shook her head immediately, "You are not the opponent of this giant serpent. Besides, even if you can lead it away, how far can you lead it away? You can't go into the darkness, right? If the giant serpent comes back, we will all die. "
"I can lead it into the dark realm," Luo Zheng said.
"I know," Ling Shuang nodded. The candles that were fished out from the bottom of the Sea of Truth were still in Ling Shuang's hands. She probably already knew how to use these candles, "But on the one hand, you can't outrun the giant serpent, and on the other hand, there are still many dangers in the dark realm. The result should be the same …"
Of course, she didn't want Luo Zheng to sacrifice himself for no reason …
Faced with the giant serpent that refused to leave, everybody was thinking quickly. Some of them even tried to chant the Disillusionment Spell, but it was impossible to leave this place easily with the Disillusionment Spell since they were already here!
"Woo …"
The red giant serpent was still lying on the ground. When it opened its mouth, the snake's tongue was sticking out. The sound it made was particularly strange. It didn't sound like a snake, but it also didn't sound like a chicken.

"…"
"…"
In the end, those disciples of the Heavenly Palace actually wanted to use words to drive the giant serpent away.
Of course, such a method had no effect. The red python was still lying on the ground, motionless and looking lazy.
This was its natural instinct to lure its prey. After all, the Dark Realm also believed in a set of cruel rules. It had to rack its brains to catch prey. However, it did not realize that these humans were much smarter than it. How would they dare to rush down from the fork in the road?
Time slowly passed …
Huo Ze walked back and forth on the fork in the road, while Chou Xinghe followed the wooden house to search for some clues, but in the end, he found nothing. He could only move within the range of light, which was too limited.
One hour later …
Luo Zheng suddenly felt a violent vibration under his feet.
Boom!
The vibrations spread out in waves, constantly reverberating in the dark domain.
Luo Zheng and the Sun Souls could not sense the energy fluctuations from the dark apparition. Therefore, when they felt the vibration, it should have been some time ago …
Not only Luo Zheng, but even the red python that was crawling on the ground and guarding everyone suddenly raised its head. Its pair of snake eyes were full of vigilance.
Out of its instinctive reaction, it chose to hide at the first moment. Its body slithered on the ground for a while, and then swam toward the darkness under the wooden bridge …
"What on earth is causing the vibration? It actually scared the red python away! "
"It can't be a more powerful existence approaching, right?"
"There is nothing more terrifying than the giant serpent in the dark domain, definitely not!"
Just as they were discussing, a loud boom sounded again. This time, the accompanying vibration was even more violent.
At the same time, there seemed to be a behemoth waving past in the darkness. When the behemoth swept across the wooden bridge, everyone vaguely recognized a shadow. It was actually a giant hairy hand!
"Oh my god!"
"What kind of hand is that!"
"It's over …"
A red python was already so terrifying. The body of the giant hand was probably thousands of times larger than the red python. Everyone's heart kept sinking.
When the giant red hand swept to the bottom of the wooden bridge, it was completely hidden in the darkness.
About two breaths of time later, Luo Zheng and others felt two more smaller vibrations under their feet. Then they saw the giant hand emerge from the darkness. This time, it was holding a worm with two fingers!
No, it was the red python!
But in contrast, it really looked like a long worm.
The giant hand brought it into the darkness, and two blue lights flickered in the darkness. With the help of these two blue lights, everyone was able to clearly see the giant face. It was the face of a giant ape.
"It's the Gemini Blue Star of the dark domain!"
"So that's how it is. The Gemini Blue Star is actually … actually … the eyes of a giant ape?"
"Such a terrifying existence has been staying in the 13th Layer of Heaven all this time?"
Generally speaking, when people mentioned the dark domain, they regarded it as the existence of absolute darkness. Once it was sucked in, it was impossible to return.
However, the dark domain was not always like this. For a long time, there would always be some strange lights shining.
For example, once the candles in the three wooden houses were lit, it immediately attracted the attention of countless creatures.
In addition, the most famous one should be the Gemini Blue Star of the dark domain!
The location of the Gemini Blue Star was not fixed. Sometimes it would be close to the entrance of the 13th Layer of Heaven, sometimes in the middle, and sometimes at the end. However, the Gemini Blue Star only appeared in the dark domain in the south of the 13th Layer of Heaven. It had never been seen in the dark domain in the north.
The appearance of the Gemini Blue Star was often accompanied by violent vibrations, which was the legendary Nirvana Earthquake.
Because earthquakes and the Gemini Blue Star often appeared at the same time, almost all the races regarded the Gemini Blue Star as the precursor of an earthquake. Every observation was so accurate, which made the wise people who did not know the truth very confused.
Now, this confusion was solved …
At this distance, they could finally see it clearly.
What kind of star was this? It was the eyes of a giant ape!
The giant ape's body was too large. Of course, every time it appeared, it would be accompanied by strong vibrations.
However, everyone was confused because they had solved this ten-thousand-year-old mystery. Luo Zheng was able to resist the red python once or twice, but in the face of this giant ape, he was afraid that he would not be able to resist.
The previously arrogant red python had been sent into the mouth of the giant ape, where it was constantly chewed.
Because of the darkness, they could not hear the chewing sound, but the red python was chewed into pieces in the blink of an eye, which filled everyone's hearts with fear.
Some people began to regret.

The Three-eyed Boy
After the giant ape swallowed the red python, its huge eyes closed.

This giant hand did not directly slap down as they had imagined. Instead, it clenched its fist and slowly lowered it, as if it was holding something in its palm.
As it gently opened its palm, a child of seven or eight years old appeared in its palm!
The child's appearance, height, and size were not much different from that of a normal human. The only difference was the eyeball on its forehead!
The third eye …
Luo Zheng looked at the child's third eye and pondered in his heart.
This child was not completely unharmed. His shoulder and the left side of his face had been scratched to a pulp. However, he did not seem to care about his injuries at all.
The Three-eyed Boy shook his head and said, "I just happened to be fighting with those black monkeys. I didn't expect to catch fish at this time … It's disappointing."
"Fishing?"

A wooden bridge had been built, and there were three wooden houses. Along the way, there was light shining on them …
Some things were extremely clear when they were speculated in reverse, but when they experienced it, they were completely confused. After all, they knew nothing about the hidden domain. Who would know what these things were?

A single sentence from the three-eyed boy had sentenced all the Yang Souls present to death. The expressions of Ling Shuang, Feng Ge, Huo Ze, and Chou Xun immediately changed drastically.

The three-eyed boy's head tilted, and a contemptuous smile appeared on his face.
He gently reached out with one hand and grabbed Luo Zheng's right hand. He suddenly raised his hand and directly smashed Luo Zheng into the ground!
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng, who was almost invincible in the Paramita Realm, could not even retaliate in front of the three-eyed boy!

This streak of green light directly pierced through those people's chests, stringing them together.
Everyone who was pierced by the green light felt that their souls were no longer under control. They all looked at the three-eyed boy in horror.
However, Feng Ge was the only Yang soul that was missing.
"Your soul fire is still somewhat useful, so your life can be spared just like his," the three-eyed boy said coldly. "As for the rest of you, you can all go and die …"
Just as the three-eyed boy was about to destroy that string of Yang souls, Luo Zheng's roar could be heard. "Stop!"
The three-eyed boy turned his head and glanced at Luo Zheng. A trace of puzzlement and mockery appeared on his young face. "What right do you have to ask me to stop?"
After he finished speaking, the third eye on his forehead flickered with green light. The Yang soul of a Jiuli tribesman at the side immediately began to shrink and was directly sucked into the green light …
"Ah Qi!"
"Brother Qi!"
Huo Ze, Lan Qing, and the others saw their tribesmen fall and were naturally extremely sad. However, they did not have any means to resist. They were pierced by the green light and allowed to be slaughtered by the three-eyed boy.
"Since Feng Ge and I are fish, then we will definitely be useful to you! No matter what you want to do, she and I will serve you. However, you can't kill these people! "Luo Zheng said hurriedly.
"Even if I kill them, you still have to serve me." The three-eyed boy shrugged his shoulders. Another person's Yang soul quickly shrank into a ball and was sucked into the green light. It was a disciple of the Heavenly Palace.
"If you kill again, Feng Ge and I would rather die than do as you wish," Luo Zheng said resolutely as he looked at Ling Shuang, who was not too far away.
Ling Shuang also stared blankly at Luo Zheng. When she heard these words, she suddenly felt a trace of emotion in her heart.
If the three-eyed boy sucked away another Yang soul, she would be next. However, she did not feel much fear in her heart. Instead, she softly said, "Luo Zheng …"
Feng Ge did not want to sacrifice his own life for these people. However, when he heard Tian Xing's tone, he could not refute. At this moment, he suddenly heard Ling Shuang's soft murmur and was slightly stunned.
"Luo Zheng?"
Feng Ge stared suspiciously at Luo Zheng's physical body.
The three-eyed boy did not care about the life or death of these Yang souls. However, he had gone through so much trouble to set this up. He had also waited for a long time. Who knew when the next batch of living beings would break into the Temple of Heaven? Furthermore, this physical body was even harder to come by.

Chapter 2938
Hint
Although the Three-eyed Boy's hesitation was only for a moment, Luo Zheng still clearly caught it.

Ling Shuang stared blankly at Luo Zheng.

Ling Shuang had never expected him to compromise like this when he encountered an existence he couldn't fight against.
The Three-eyed Boy thought for a moment and sneered, "It doesn't matter if you tell anyone, as long as the two of you are willing to stay here!"
With that, the Three-eyed Boy's third eye on his forehead suddenly closed. The green light that had pierced all the Yang souls into a string also disappeared. Everyone felt their Yang souls loosen up. The feeling of being controlled disappeared without a trace.
"Scram!" The Three-eyed Boy pointed at the wooden bridge.
With him and the Giant Ape here, no one from the Dark Realm would dare to approach the wooden bridge. It should be very safe to return to the Temple of Heaven through the wooden bridge.
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly said to Ling Shuang, "Ling Shuang, you and Feng Ge are sisters. I'm afraid it'll be difficult for you to meet again …"
When Feng Ge heard this, he was baffled. Who was her sister? She had no idea what Luo Zheng was planning.

Feng Ge wasn't an idiot. Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang both had hidden meanings behind their words. She sensed that Ling Shuang had activated her spatial ring. Perhaps she wanted to give her something, so she also activated her spatial ring.

Ling Shuang's series of actions were very skillful, and she managed to fool the Three-eyed Boy.
The Three-eyed Boy couldn't be bothered with those Sun Souls. He turned to Luo Zheng and Feng Ge and said, "Come with me!"

He glanced at the first wooden house and said, "You've already been to this wooden house. Do you know what the things inside are used for?"
"It seems to be some kind of ore," Luo Zheng replied.
