He asked, "You're so powerful. It's easy for you to make these candles. Why go through so much trouble to lure people here from the 13th Heaven?"
If the Sun Souls he lured here didn't meet his expectations, wouldn't they all be killed by him?
"The dark domain isn't as simple as you think. I still have to deal with those black monkeys. Besides, we can't stay under the light for too long …" At this point, there was a hint of yearning in the Three-eyed Boy's eyes.
He was indeed very powerful. All the creatures in the dark domain were powerful.
However, they had also paid a huge price, which was to be trapped in the darkness forever.
Many places on the other side were filled with light, but they could only look up in the darkness …
Occasionally, they could get a candle and bathe themselves in the light. However, this process couldn't last long. Even though the light of the candle was so gentle, it was still fatal to them if they stayed for too long.
Following this fork in the road, the Three-eyed Boy led Luo Zheng and Feng Ge to the tallest wooden house. This was also the largest wooden house.
"Whoosh!"
The Three-eyed Boy blew gently and extinguished the candle in his hand.
The light of this wooden house wasn't connected to the other two. If they didn't rely on the candlelight, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge naturally wouldn't be able to escape.
"Ka!"
With a wave of the Three-eyed Boy's hand, the door of the wooden house opened automatically. At the same time, he said, "Figure out how to make the candles yourselves. You need to see the finished product before I return. Otherwise, you'll have to bear the consequences."
The Three-eyed Boy felt that he was already merciful enough. In the past, he wouldn't have let those Sun Souls leave. If these two still weren't willing to work properly, he would be letting himself down. He had many ways to make these two suffer a fate worse than death.
Then, he turned around and leapt into the darkness. When he fell into the candlelight again, he had already landed firmly in the hands of the huge ape.
"Hum! Hum! "
Two more dots of blue light appeared in the darkness.
"Thump … Thump … Thump …"
Regardless of whether it was the dark domain, the wooden bridge, or the Temple of Heaven, they all trembled with the footsteps of the huge ape.
"He's gone!" Ling Shuang said excitedly as she leaned on the bridge. "I … I want to go back!"
When Chou Xinghe heard Ling Shuang's words, he shook his head and said, "Your Highness Ling Shuang, stop dreaming. It's already a great fortune for us to be able to keep our lives. If we turn back now, I'm afraid even His Excellency Tianhang will scold you for being stupid!"
Those Sun Souls wouldn't be able to detect any special restrictions set up by those Sun Souls in the dark domain. Going back would really be seeking their own deaths.
"But …" Ling Shuang was still somewhat unwilling.
"His Excellency Tianhang is blessed by the heavens. Perhaps he'll be able to get through this crisis," Huo Ze comforted from the side.
In the eyes of the Jiuli tribesmen, although they didn't gain much from this trip to the Temple of Heaven, they did gain something else. The expert who had been traveling in the Nirvana Realm with his physical body was actually from the royal family of the Jiuli tribe!
Huo Ze and the others were also very concerned about Luo Zheng's life and death. Once they returned from the Nirvana Realm, they would definitely report this matter to the elders of the tribe.

Chapter 2939

Decoction
After the Three-eyed Boy left, Feng Ge suddenly changed her attitude and turned to look at Luo Zheng.
Feng Ge had another conjecture about the body in front of her!
When Lingshuang called out the name "Luo Zheng" under the threat of death, Feng Ge was naturally confused.
Luo Zheng had entered the Nirvana Realm with his body, used an alias, and changed his voice. In a situation where one could not recognize his face, it was basically impossible to recognize him.
However, after Lingshuang's reminder, when Feng Ge looked at this "Lord Tianhang" again, she thought of the image of Luo Zheng in her mind. The figure and silhouette of the two were eighty to ninety percent similar …
Although her suspicions were greatly increased, Feng Ge did not expose him in front of everyone.
She stood at the door of the wooden house and asked, "What do we do now?"
Luo Zheng shrugged and pointed inside the door. "What else can we do? We can only do as that guy said …"
"But Lingshuang just now …" Feng Ge raised her hand as she spoke.
Luo Zheng saw that Feng Ge was about to activate the Sumeru Ring and his expression suddenly changed.

"What did Lingshuang say to you?" Luo Zheng asked in return.

Behind these utensils, there was a white cotton thread …
Feng Ge looked at these things in the wooden house and felt a headache coming on.
Was she really going to be the one to make the candles?
It was common to see luminous pearls as big as the houses in Taishi Palace. The craftsmen in the palace would carve luminous pearls into various shapes and decorate the corners of the palace.
As for candles, they were only things that mortals would use. They were probably very rare in the Taishi Palace.
"Are you really going to make candles for him?" Feng Ge asked.
"If you don't want to die, do as he says," Luo Zheng replied.
Feng Ge blinked and said, "But I saw that the candle he lit can illuminate the dark domain. If we make a candle, can't we escape with it?"
The third wooden house was separated from the fork by darkness. Without the help of candles, they could not leave the third wooden house.
Luo Zheng laughed at Feng Ge's naive assumption. He pointed to the door of the cabin and said, "Why bother? There is still a candle there. Can you try to pull it out?"
Feng Ge really did as Luo Zheng said.
She jumped up and grabbed the candle at the door of the wooden house, trying to pull it out.
But the moment she reached out her hand, the candle turned into a shadow ….
Feng Ge could feel the light and heat emitted by the candle, but she could not touch it.
"Even a fool knows to escape with the candle. That three-eyed boy is not that stupid." Luo Zheng shook his head. "I'm afraid the candle we make won't be able to be lit …"
Luo Zheng placed his hopes on the few candles he pulled up from the bottom of the sea. But how to light the candles and where to run after lighting them were all problems. But before solving these problems, he had to start making candles for the three-eyed boy.
Although Luo Zheng did not know the process of making candles, it was not difficult for him to do it.
Many mortals in the Inner World had tried all kinds of methods to obtain flames during the Wilderness Expansion Era.
The first candles were made from animal fat and live beeswax …

The huge ape only left for half an hour. As the ground continued to shake, the huge ape quickly turned back.
"Whoosh!"
The huge ape stuck a large tree directly in the range of the candlelight.
The tree was about ten feet tall. Its appearance was very ordinary, but its color was very strange.
The branches, leaves, and fruits on the left were all pure black. The branches, leaves, and other things on the right were snow-white. One tree actually had two completely different colors and shapes.

Chapter 2944

Rescue
Luo Zheng's face was full of astonishment as he looked at the strange tree that was black and white in color.
He had never seen such a strange plant before …
Although the giant ape's words were vague and unclear, Luo Zheng understood them clearly. Furthermore, these words were completely consistent with Luo Zheng's conjecture.
Sure enough, there was such a thing in the Hidden Realm that could make them fearless of the candlelight. Furthermore, it appeared that these things weren't very rare. At the very least, the red python and the monkey monster with low intelligence had the ability to come into contact with them!
And now, there were two kinds of fruits on this strange tree!
Luo Zheng pointed at the white fruit and asked the giant ape, "What about the white fruit?"
The giant ape pointed at the white fruit with its huge finger and said, "Eat it … and you won't be afraid of darkness …"
The white fruit was naturally useless to the beings of the Hidden Realm. They were originally a race that was born in the darkness. However, it was extremely useful to beings outside of the Hidden Realm!
Feng Ge and Luo Zheng looked at each other. It was as if a beam of light had shone into their minds.
Eating the white fruit would allow them to integrate with the energy of the Hidden Realm and allow them to move freely within the Hidden Realm?
"You … eat it …" the giant ape commanded.
The giant ape had also been enlightened by Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng wasn't a being of the Hidden Realm. The Dao of the Life Divine Dao that he released would burn the three-eyed boy. What if he allowed him to integrate into the Hidden Realm?
Thus, the giant ape found this tree and uprooted it before tossing it in front of Luo Zheng.
"Eat it then …"
Luo Zheng had eaten many strange things during this period of time. He didn't mind eating one more white fruit.
He plucked one of the white fruits and stuffed it into his mouth.
As soon as he swallowed it, Luo Zheng felt a gentle energy spread throughout his body.
This energy wasn't powerful, but it was everywhere. It even seeped into his dantian …
In the time it took to drink a cup of tea, Luo Zheng felt some changes in his body.
Previously, Luo Zheng saw absolute darkness in the Dark Domain. Except for the candles and the eyes of the Giant Ape, everything else was pitch-black. But now, he felt that the darkness in the Dark Domain seemed to have faded a little.
The boundary between the candlelight and the darkness became smooth. Some vague shadows appeared in the depths of the darkness. There were mountains, trees, and paths. This was the true appearance of the Dark Domain!
"How is it?" Feng Ge asked.
"I …"
Luo Zheng walked to the edge of the darkness and gently reached his hand into it.
Luo Zheng was still a little nervous even though he was only putting his fingertips in.
The moment his finger entered the Dark Domain, the gentle energy came into play and helped Luo Zheng resist the devouring darkness. Luo Zheng was unharmed!
"Yes, it's a success." Luo Zheng smiled.
Right now, saving people was more important …
The white fruit seemed to have changed his physique, preventing him from clashing with the Dark Domain.
In that case, he should be able to use the Life Divine Dao to save people, right?
"Weng …"
A green light revolved around Luo Zheng. Under the illumination of this green light, everything seemed full of life.
"Rain of Life …"
The green light condensed into a green cloud above the Three-eyed Boy. The Dao Accumulation turned into raindrops that fell on the Three-eyed Boy.
This time, the Dao Accumulation of Luo Zheng's Divine Dao did not repel the Three-eyed Boy. The Dao Accumulation began healing the Three-eyed Boy's wounds.
But the Divine Dao energy was too weak to the Paramita Realm.
It was still fine for Paramita Realm experts who had yet to enter the Chaotic Origin Realm. Their fleshy bodies had not been strengthened significantly. Their essence was not much different from True Gods. It was just that the energy they could use was several dozen or even several hundred times stronger than True Gods.
But once they entered the Chaotic Origin Realm, their fleshy bodies would be strengthened.
This Three-eyed Boy was a lifeform of the Dark Domain. He should not have experienced the 'Chaotic Origin Reconstruction'. But the energy he casually unleashed was something Luo Zheng could not resist against. His fleshy body was at least as strong as a Chaotic Origin Realm expert. It might even be stronger than that!
Using the Dao Accumulation of the Life Divine Dao to heal a Chaotic Origin Realm expert was like using mortal medicine to save a heavily injured warrior. Naturally, it was effective, but the process was extremely slow.
Two hours …
Four hours …
Six hours …
It was also because Luo Zheng's inner world was extremely proficient. Even if he continuously absorbed the Dao Accumulation, he would not overdraft it.
When it reached the fourth hour, some of the tiny wounds on the Three-eyed Boy's body showed signs of healing …
But something happened at the fifth hour. The Three-eyed Boy's body started emitting strands of black smoke. His fleshy body was repelling the candlelight.
'It is the time limit of that black fruit …'
Luo Zheng did not panic. He extended his hand and plucked a black fruit from the tree before stuffing it into the Three-eyed Boy's mouth.
Not long later, that black smoke disappeared. The Three-eyed Boy no longer repelled the candlelight.
The sixth hour …
The seventh hour …
The green raindrops from the Life Divine Dao landed on the Three-eyed Boy's body, corroding many black scars.
"Wu …"
The huge ape who had been observing silently let out a sound, reminding Luo Zheng that it was time for Luo Zheng to eat that white fruit.
Actually, Luo Zheng could vaguely sense that the effect of that white fruit was fading. It was just that he wanted to confirm how long this white fruit could last.
The longest time a white fruit could last was 14 hours!
After swallowing another white fruit, Luo Zheng continued releasing the Life Divine Dao. The green Life Rain had almost completely soaked the Three-eyed Boy.
One day …
Two days …
Three days …
The Three-eyed Boy's body was gradually recovering. His fleshy body that had been torn apart by the rain had mostly recovered. But he was still unconscious.
The huge ape was like a Protector God guarding the wooden house.
Quite a few people were watching from the main entrance of the Overwhelming Heaven Divine Temple.
They could still see the three wooden houses and the Gemini Blue Star. But they did not know what had happened.
Ling Shuang's Yang Soul appeared on a hillside. She looked into the distance with a complicated expression and silently prayed in her heart.
"I know that you are blessed by providence. Even if you encounter a great calamity, you will definitely be able to turn it into a blessing. So …"
Halfway through her sentence, her Yang Soul gradually dissipated and left the other side.
Ling Shuang's consciousness returned to the Origin World. Center God Province, Great One Heavenly Palace, Great One Mountain, Heart Flow Sword Sect. On the second floor of an independent courtyard.
She sat across from Luo Zheng and stared at Luo Zheng's handsome face. Luo Zheng's brows were furrowed. What was bothering him?
Ling Shuang stretched out her hand and massaged his furrowed brows. Then, she said with certainty, "So … you will definitely wake up!"
Outside the door on the second floor, Ning Yudie sat there with a worried expression. She had not said a word for many days.
Xi Youqin, on the other hand, kept yelling for Ling Shuang to get out of the house.
Ling Shuang's guards glared at Xi Youqin. Insulting Her Highness Ling Shuang was a crime of great disrespect. If not for Her Highness Ling Shuang's prohibition, they would have taken this woman away long ago!

Chapter 2945
Translator: Transn Editor: Transn
Suspicion and Jealousy
Seven days later.
The Three-eyed Boy's injuries were almost completely healed under the constant nourishment of the Dao of Life.
After these few days of interaction, the giant ape had gained some trust in Luo Zheng and Feng Ge. It would leave the Three-eyed Boy alone for them to take care of.
After the giant ape left, it collected some exotic plants and flowers in the hidden domain.
Although it did not know anything about alchemy, it naturally understood the use of these plants and flowers after living in the hidden domain for so many years.
The giant ape brought these plants to Luo Zheng. After Luo Zheng roughly understood what it meant, he simply processed these plants and flowers and fed them to the Three-eyed Boy.
It was unknown whether it was the effect of these plants or the Dao of Life. Ten days later, the Three-eyed Boy's three eyes finally opened.
He got up from the ground and looked around. He said in surprise, "I'm fine?"
The Three-eyed Boy thought he was dead for sure.
"Yes …"
The giant ape responded. At the same time, it shook its huge head and smiled with relief.
The Three-eyed Boy stared at the giant ape and asked, "You brought me back and healed me?"
"Huh?"
The giant ape was simple and honest. It immediately shook its head.
The Three-eyed Boy was indeed brought back by it. After the Three-eyed Boy broke into the Luminous Crystal Mining Area, he was surrounded by those black monkeys. It was not the first time the Three-eyed Boy had dealt with those black monkeys. At first, he did not think much of it.
However, the black monkeys came out in full force this time. It was only when the Three-eyed Boy was surrounded that he realized that something was wrong.
He almost forced his way out in a single breath.
Although he finally rushed out of the Luminous Crystal Mining Area, those black monkeys chased after him and tore at his flesh along the way. In the end, he fell to the ground and lay in a pool of blood. The giant ape reached out at the critical moment, picked up the Three-eyed Boy, and fled.
However, the giant ape believed that the person who saved the Three-eyed Boy was Luo Zheng and not it.
It pointed at Luo Zheng with its thick finger and said, "He … Help … You …"
The Three-eyed Boy had been with the Giant Ape for a long time, so he knew every word it said. When he heard that Luo Zheng had saved him, his face was full of doubt.
Because of his physique, it was impossible for him to do so.
Then, he glanced around and saw that there were many big trees around the wooden house. When he saw the black and white tree, his eyes flashed.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were not from the same world as him. No matter what life-saving methods they had, they were like arsenic to the Three-eyed Boy. They were highly toxic existences.
But with this tree, the situation was different …
The three-eyed boy stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "You ate the Fruit of Darkness?"
Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment and then nodded, "Yes."
The three-eyed boy smiled coldly and continued to ask, "Since you've eaten the Fruit of Darkness, you should've had plenty of opportunities to escape! Why did you stay here to save me? What are your intentions? "
After eating the Fruit of Darkness, Luo Zheng could completely adapt to the environment of the Tenebrous Domain and leave the area illuminated by the candlelight.
Once they crossed the wooden bridge and escaped the Tenebrous Domain, even the Giant Ape could do nothing to Luo Zheng and Feng Ge.
However, they didn't escape and instead stayed here to save him. In the heart of the Three-eyed Boy, who was good at suspicion, there must be a big problem.
"Speak!"
The Three-eyed Boy shouted and the eye on his forehead opened. With a flash of green light, a green ray of light shot toward Luo Zheng.
The green ray of light could not only penetrate the soul, but it could also penetrate Luo Zheng's body in an instant.
"Puff!"
After the green ray of light pierced through Luo Zheng's chest, he was pulled into the air by the ray of light.
"Ugh, ugh …"
The Giant Ape let out two strange cries. It didn't expect the Three-eyed Boy to attack Luo Zheng after waking up.
"How can you do this! In any case, he saved your life! "Feng Ge stood out and berated the Three-eyed Boy.
Luo Zheng had spent a lot of energy to save this brat these days. Although he only stimulated the Divine Dao's Dao Connotation, he still wasted a lot of time and effort without rest. The Three-eyed Boy actually repaid his kindness with enmity after waking up!
The Three-eyed Boy ignored Feng Ge and stared at Luo Zheng with his three eyes. He was determined to get an answer from Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's expression didn't change much. He could still endure the pain of his body being penetrated. He heard Luo Zheng ask indifferently, "Have you always been alone in the Tenebrous Domain?"
The Three-eyed Boy didn't know why Luo Zheng asked such a question. With a cold expression, he said, "So what if I have?"
"Don't you have friends … and family?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Friends? Family? "
These two words were so unfamiliar to the Three-eyed Boy.
Speaking of friends, the only friend he had in the Tenebrous Domain was probably this Giant Ape. As for friends and family of the same race, he really didn't have any.
Some people said that the struggle between the mother world and the Nirvana Realm was very cruel. However, it was child's play compared to the Tenebrous Domain.
In the Dark Domain, there was no distinction between races. From the moment they were born, they were trapped in endless slaughter. Among the countless possibilities of death, only the lucky few who survived could take a step forward and qualify to be recruited by the organization.
The Three-eyed Boy had climbed up step by step like this. If he had taken a single wrong step, he would have fallen into an irrecoverable death trap.
The long and cruel experience had caused him to lose the ability to trust.
In the Three-eyed Boy's mind, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge must be filled with unwillingness to be locked up here by him.
When he was on the verge of death, not only did these two not escape, but they even saved him. There must be some other conspiracy!
"I explored the Tenebrous Domain not only to obtain the Nirvana token, but also because I was curious about the Tenebrous Domain." Luo Zheng continued, "At first, we did plan to escape. In fact, we were already prepared. However, I feel that you're not as bad as you look. You're a friend worth making, so I naturally chose to save you."
A friend worth making?
When the Three-eyed Boy heard Luo Zheng's words, there was an uncomfortable expression on his face. However, something in his heart was touched.
If this guy really thinks this way, then he must be sick in the head, the Three-eyed Boy thought to himself.
However, for some reason, the Three-eyed Boy didn't find such a person annoying.
His stiff and cold expression softened a little. The third eye on his forehead blinked gently. The green light that pierced through Luo Zheng disappeared. Luo Zheng fell from mid-air with a plop.
The Three-eyed Boy picked the white fruits from the black and white tree one by one and handed them to Luo Zheng. He said, "As long as you're willing to continue making candles for me, I can give you more freedom."

Black Monkey
Before the Three-eyed Boy woke up, Luo Zheng had secretly left him several Fruits of Darkness.

No matter what, making candles was the most important thing.
After the Three-eyed Boy placed the Burning Crystals in the uppermost wooden house, he turned around and stared at Luo Zheng and Feng Ge as he asked, "You said that you want to obtain a Paramita token?"
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge looked at each other.
The things in the Dark Domain were much more powerful than they had imagined.
If they could obtain a Paramita token from the Dark Domain, they would naturally be more than happy to do so.
Even Feng Ge, who thought highly of himself, realized that this was a great opportunity.
Feng Ge's own Paramita token, the King of Stone Hills, was also very precious. The King of Stone Hills even had a chance to grow. However, she was willing to exchange it for an item from the Dark Domain.
"If it's possible to obtain such a Paramita token, of course I'd be happy," Luo Zheng replied.
"The energy of the Dark Domain repels you. The same goes for the Paramita token. I'm afraid it'll be very difficult for you to fuse them," the Three-eyed Boy said again.

The Paramita tokens in the Dark Domain should not be able to fuse with creatures outside the Dark Domain.

Luo Zheng swallowed a Fruit of Darkness and walked into the Dark Domain.

The Dark Domain didn't give Luo Zheng too much pressure …
After swallowing the Fruit of Darkness, the energy in the Dark Domain wouldn't reject Luo Zheng.
The originally dark world had a hint of color in Luo Zheng's eyes.
The fork in the road that led to the wooden house was a hillside. On the side of the hillside was a cliff. The cliff was unfathomably high. The giant ape probably walked under the cliff every time it came here.
Luo Zheng walked down the hillside for a while. The road ahead was completely out of sight. Bushes and shrubs blocked the way.
"So the location of the wooden house is isolated. The Three-eyed Boy probably chose this place for safety reasons," Luo Zheng murmured.
It was his first time exploring the Dark Domain, so he didn't plan to go deep into it. Besides, Feng Ge was still boiling the liquid wax by himself.
But just as Luo Zheng turned around, there was a sudden squeak not far away!
The sound waves in the Dark Domain were also a kind of energy. So, whether it was Luo Zheng or anyone else, they couldn't catch this sound. Now that Luo Zheng had merged into the Dark Domain, he naturally heard it clearly.
Luo Zheng's gaze focused slightly. He saw that in the darkness not far away, more than ten monkey-men were constantly jumping up and down, heading straight for him!
Luo Zheng's brows furrowed when he encountered these things as soon as he came out.
Without hesitation, Luo Zheng turned around and flew up the hillside!
Even the giant red python didn't dare to enter the range of the wooden house. These monkey-men probably wouldn't dare!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The monkey-men had been active in this area for a long time. They were clearly more familiar with the terrain than Luo Zheng.
They constantly jumped and rolled, quickly closing the distance between them and Luo Zheng with great agility.
"Squeak!"
One of the monkey-men screamed and jumped on top of Luo Zheng's head. Its barbed tail was already stabbing towards Luo Zheng's head.
However, Luo Zheng wasn't one of those Yang souls.
He might not be able to face the giant red python, but these monkey-men weren't that strong!
Luo Zheng's body slightly bent. His hands shot out like lightning, twisting the monkey-man's tail to his side. With a sudden swing, the monkey-man only had time to let out a shrill squeak before its entire head slammed into another monkey-man's body.
"Boom!"
The two monkey-men were knocked dizzy. Even more monkey-men surrounded Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng once again climbed up the hillside. Whenever a monkey-man approached, he would casually grab them. Some of them were thrown to other monkey-men, while others were thrown directly into a nearby mountain stream by Luo Zheng.
Not long after, Luo Zheng stood at the top of the hillside.
He was only about thirty meters away from the wooden house. After calming down a bit, he wasn't in a hurry to run back to the wooden house.
"Squeak squeak squeak squeak squeak …"
More and more monkey-men gathered at the bottom of the hillside. There were actually several hundred of them. However, they didn't seem to have any intention of climbing up the hillside. Instead, they sat in a neat row, as if they were respectfully welcoming the birth of something.
Luo Zheng revealed a curious expression. He stayed on the hillside and observed for a while.

Chapter 2947
Broken Bridge
"Black monkeys …"

With the monkey-men, the python, and even Luo Zheng's strength, it wasn't easy to destroy this wooden bridge. However, it was too easy for the black monkeys to destroy it.

Luo Zheng didn't have the time to inquire about what was happening behind him. He was only focused on running, and his speed was actually getting faster and faster.
"Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng …"
Feng Ge called out from behind.

Feng Ge pursed her lips. She hated it when people reprimanded her without distinguishing right from wrong.
Looking at the bridge deck that was constantly collapsing behind her, Feng Ge chose to shut up …
The entire wooden bridge had no piers. It was like a thin line connecting the Temple of Sky and the hillside.
As the hillside collapsed, the entire bridge deck began to tilt downward rapidly.
After two breaths, Luo Zheng felt that the bridge deck in front of him had become an "uphill road". Just as he was wondering, the entire "uphill road" became steeper and steeper. In the blink of an eye, it had become 90 degrees!
Caught off guard, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge both fell straight toward the mountain stream.
As they were falling, Luo Zheng asked, "What's going on … Is the bridge broken?"
Feng Ge looked indifferent. "It was broken by them. I was just about to tell you."
"…"

This mountain stream was unknown how deep it was. Falling straight down from the bridge deck, Luo Zheng was also carefully observing.
After swallowing the Fruit of Darkness, he and Feng Ge had adapted to the Dark Domain. They could vaguely make out the outline of the scenery below.
After about ten breaths, Luo Zheng suddenly withdrew his legs and gently pulled Feng Ge, who was behind him, into his arms. His whole body fell heavily into the bushes.
"Boom!"
Falling from such a height, Luo Zheng's body was actually fine.
However, the Yang Soul was much weaker than the physical body. Luo Zheng fell with Feng Ge as if he was holding a piece of porcelain. Under the huge impact, if he was slightly careless, Feng Ge would probably die.
"Are you okay?" Luo Zheng asked with concern.
"I'm fine."

Chapter 2948
Overdraft
Luo Zheng looked up and said, "Let's get out of here quickly …"
Since the black monkeys had chosen to break the bridge, there was no reason for them not to chase them down the mountain stream.
"Okay, I'll listen to you." Feng Ge nodded.
Then, she felt her hand being pulled by Luo Zheng, and they began to speed forward.
At this moment, a strange thought flashed through Feng Ge's mind. She suddenly realized that her personality had changed!
In the Mother World, Feng Ge was a man of his word. Among all the peers in the Heavenly Palace, no matter who stood in front of him, they would feel half a head shorter than him. Even Ye Shang, Chou Xun, and the others felt uncomfortable when facing him.
However, after entering the Temple of Boundless Heaven, Feng Ge had suffered two or three setbacks along the way, which had dealt a great blow to his confidence.
After staying in the wooden house for a few days, he was on tenterhooks every day. Gradually, he began to blindly follow Luo Zheng!
Alright, I'll listen to you …
Such words were not something that she could say!
Luo Zheng didn't know what Feng Ge was thinking. He just wanted to get rid of those black monkeys.
While he was running along the river in the mountain stream, long pincers suddenly stretched out from the sand beside the river. After these pincers stretched out from the sand, they went straight for Luo Zheng's head.
"Since when did these shrimp soldiers and crab generals dare to find trouble with me …"
Luo Zheng frowned.
Although these prawns and crabs were also creatures of the Dark Realm, Luo Zheng still didn't put them in his eyes.
One of his hands was as fast as lightning. He grabbed one of the pincers and pulled out the main body that was hiding in the mud. Then, he smashed the main body onto the ground.
It was indeed a basin-sized crab. Its entire body was black, and there were strange white patterns on its shell.
The crab still wanted to raise its pincers to resist, but Luo Zheng stepped on its shell while running, directly crushing it into pieces.
"Plop, plop …"
Boom!
At this moment, the sound of something falling came from the mountain stream behind them. It was the sound of the black monkeys jumping down the mountain stream. Some landed on the ground while others landed in the river.
"Ga ga …"
"Gegezhi!"
The physical bodies of these black monkeys were obviously extremely strong. After jumping down from above, they were still vigorous and lively. They lived in the Dark Domain all year round, so both their vision and sense of smell were exceptionally keen. They instantly discovered Luo Zheng who was running.
Luo Zheng didn't say anything and continued to run along the mountain stream without thinking.
As for where the end of the mountain stream led to, Luo Zheng had no way to tell.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
A group of black monkeys chased after Luo Zheng and Feng Ge, making a lot of noise.
These black monkeys were famous in the Dark Zone. They hid in the rivers, on the mountain walls, and in the bushes. None of the creatures in the Dark Zone dared to show their faces. The intelligent creatures in the Dark Zone could only sigh when they saw Luo Zheng running away. They didn't know how Luo Zheng had offended those black monkeys …
"Creak!"
The black monkey at the front suddenly jumped up and grabbed a tree trunk protruding from the mountain wall. With a sudden swing, the monkey turned into a flying bat and rushed toward Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng felt the movement above him and was slightly stunned. He lifted Feng Ge up with one hand and said, "Don't be afraid!" Then, he suddenly threw Feng Ge forward.
At the same time, the power of his fists burst forth.
Under the flow of the Shen Jun Force, Luo Zheng's physical body became full and full of power.
His strength might not be comparable to the Three-eyed Boy's, but if he were to face a black monkey alone …
"Squeak!"
When the black monkey saw Luo Zheng's actions, a scornful smile appeared on its monkey-like face.
It was afraid that Luo Zheng would keep running away, so it pounced with all its strength to cut off his escape route. Unexpectedly, not only did this human not run away, but he also came to meet it.
Did he want to die?
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng collided with the black monkey in midair.
These black monkeys were not as strong as the Three-eyed Boy, but they were still not to be underestimated.
Under the collision of man and monkey, Luo Zheng felt all the bones in his body shift …
Even in the Mother World, Luo Zheng rarely encountered such a strong opponent, but it seemed to be very common in the Tenebrous Domain.
"Swish!"
Under the huge impact, the black monkey's body was bounced back and crashed into the mountain wall. Luo Zheng bounced forward and caught the slowly descending Feng Ge. Then, he fled forward at an even faster speed.
"Ga ga!"
The black monkey clung to a tree root outside the mountain wall with one hand and gritted its teeth. It flew along the tree root and tree trunk on the mountain wall and chased after Luo Zheng again.
Luo Zheng rushed along the mountain stream for dozens of miles. The black monkeys also chased for dozens of miles. From the looks of it, they would not give up until they caught him.
As Luo Zheng fled, he was faintly worried.
On the one hand, he was afraid that he had chosen the wrong direction.
These black monkeys were social creatures. If he rushed to their nest along the mountain stream, it would be troublesome. It would be suicidal.
However, this was a matter of luck. Luo Zheng's luck had always been good, so he did not have to worry about it for now.
On the other hand, it was his stamina.
As he fled, he used more of the Strength God Totem's Paramita Power in addition to the physical strength of his body!
After the power of the Paramita token was consumed, it would slowly increase, but the rate of increase was extremely limited.
If he wanted to quickly replenish the Paramita Power, he naturally needed Divine Gems.
If Luo Zheng could leave the Paramita, replenishing the Divine Gems would be a piece of cake.
However, after entering the Temple of Mi Tian, Luo Zheng would not have this opportunity.
Over the past few days, Luo Zheng had been constantly using up his Paramita power. Running at such a high speed was depleting his Paramita power even faster. Once the Paramita power of the Mighty God Totem was exhausted, he would not be able to fight against these black monkeys.
After rushing along the mountain stream for more than ten miles, the sides of the mountain stream gradually narrowed.
From a few hundred feet wide mountain stream, it slowly narrowed to five hundred feet, then three hundred feet, ten feet …
When the sides of the mountain stream became ten feet wide, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge suddenly felt a little suppressed. It was as if they would be squeezed into the middle if they tried to dodge.
"Squeak squeak squeak squeak …"
An even stranger scene appeared. The black monkeys that were chasing after them suddenly stopped in their tracks. They did not dare to enter the narrow mountain stream. They just stood outside and chattered non-stop.
"Luo Zheng, they don't dare to come in," said Feng Ge.
"It's a good thing," replied Luo Zheng.
"They must be afraid of something. If we continue on this narrow path, I'm afraid there's something bad," said Feng Ge worriedly.
"I know," Luo Zheng shrugged, then gently put Feng Ge down.
"If you know, then why are you still running inside? You're going to kill … "

Chapter 2949

Mountain Stream Impasse
For so many days, Luo Zheng had been using up his Paramita Power. Just now, he had run all the way with Feng Ge and was almost at the bottom.
In the last part of the journey, Luo Zheng had been constantly using up the Strength Totem and even the Paramita Power in his Mind's Eye. After both had been used up, he started to use up his physical strength …
In the end, even his physical strength was used up. Luo Zheng's body was like an empty bottle. With all his strength exhausted, he fell straight to the ground.
Feng Ge was only halfway through his sentence when he saw Luo Zheng in such a state. He was shocked.
"Luo Zheng, what's wrong with you …" Feng Ge asked anxiously.
Luo Zheng rarely saw such a weak and powerless state.
Although he had no strength, his brain was still functioning. Luo Zheng replied, "I'm fine. I'm just out of strength …"
"Out of strength?" Feng Ge did not expect this to happen. "How can you be out of strength?!"
In theory, after reaching the True God Realm, it was impossible for one's strength to be exhausted.
When the Paramita Power was used up, one could use the Tao Connotation of the Shinto Dao. When the Tao Connotation was used up, one could use the Vital Core to transform it. If one wanted to use Vital Core, then the Inner World would most likely collapse.
Luo Zheng's current situation was a little special. After he crazily used up the Paramita Power of the Paramita Tokens, both of them were in a state of severe overdraft. In this state, the Paramita Tokens would ask for energy from Luo Zheng, forming a unique siphon effect.
Two huge whirlpools lay across the exit of the Inner World. Luo Zheng could not use the Tao Connotation or Vital Core through the Inner World.
In his exhaustion, Luo Zheng found it difficult to speak. Naturally, he was too lazy to explain so much to Feng Ge.
The black monkeys that did not dare to come in from afar also saw the scene of Luo Zheng collapsing.
Following this increasingly narrow mountain stream was a dangerous place. Even the powerful black monkeys did not dare to barge in.
However, Luo Zheng was still at the edge of the danger zone.
They had originally planned to give up, but when they saw Luo Zheng lying on the ground, unable to move, they suddenly had another idea.

They were afraid of disturbing something in the depths of the mountain stream, so they tiptoed lightly.

The recovery of the power of the Nirvana Realm Token might take a long time, but it wouldn't take long for the power of his physical body to recover. Although that little bit of power wouldn't allow him to walk as fast as flying, it shouldn't be too much of a problem for him to move and walk.
"I want you to rest, but I'm afraid those black monkeys won't agree," Feng Ge said bitterly as he looked at the three black monkeys not far away.

Feng Ge grabbed Luo Zheng. His soul aura burst out, and he began to pull Luo Zheng into the narrow mountain stream.
Luo Zheng's body weight was nothing to Feng Ge in the mother world.
However, it was different on the other side. Feng Ge could only use the power of the Yang Soul to drag Luo Zheng. Naturally, it was very strenuous.
"Sha sha sha …"
Luo Zheng's body was dragged on the ground. From the angle of lying on the ground, he could see Feng Ge's entire body clearly.
Although he couldn't see the expression on her face, Feng Ge's Yang Soul was trembling all the time. This meant that she was also going all out!

Feng Ge didn't say anything else. He dragged Luo Zheng into the depths of the mountain stream.
The three black monkeys that chased in didn't expect Feng Ge to drag Luo Zheng away. At first, they were tiptoeing and careful. Naturally, their speed was extremely slow. At this rate, they probably wouldn't be able to catch up to Feng Ge.
"Squeak?"
"Squeak squeak!"
The three black monkeys discussed in a low voice. Their monkey faces revealed an expression that said they were going all out.
If they went all out, they would be able to catch up to Luo Zheng in two or three breaths of time!
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh!"
The three black monkeys suddenly sped up and flew over.
Feng Ge was also shocked when he saw the black monkeys rushing over.
With her Yang Soul Body, she couldn't even fight against those relatively weak monkey monsters, let alone these black monkeys that were more than ten feet tall!
However, at this moment, Feng Ge didn't have any thoughts of escaping. His cold eyes were wide open. Golden divine flames appeared on the surface of his body and turned into golden whips that lashed at the black monkeys.
This soul attack was considered an extremely powerful technique in the 13 Heavens of the Nirvana Realm. Only the best of those super forces could withstand it.
However, in the eyes of the black monkeys, this attack was ridiculously weak. Even if they let Feng Ge attack without restraint, it would be difficult to hurt them.
Before Feng Ge's golden whips could reach the black monkeys, the three black monkeys suddenly screamed. It was as if these golden whips were shackles that claimed their lives. Their faces were full of fear. They turned around and ran away in unison.
"Ah?"
This time, it was Feng Ge's turn to be stunned. She never thought that these black monkeys would run away. Could it be that the Golden Crow Divine Flame was the nemesis of these black monkeys?
Just as Feng Ge was puzzled, black, brown, and gray tentacles spread out from behind her. These tentacles almost occupied all the space in the mountain stream. There were hundreds and thousands of them!
"Hualala …"
All the tentacles wriggled like flowing black water. They quickly spread over Feng Ge and twisted towards the black monkeys.
The three black monkeys were running fast, but they weren't faster than those tentacles.
"Cluck!"
"Squeak, squeak, squeak …"
"Gagaga!"
After being entangled by the tentacles, they were dragged into the depths of the mountain stream. Only sharp screams echoed in the narrow mountain stream.
Feng Ge stood motionless on the spot. His entire body was petrified.

Chapter 2950
Within the range of sunlight.
The occasional breeze passing through the mountain stream brought with it a hint of warmth. Feng Ge felt as if she had fallen into a ten-thousand-year-old ice cellar.
She leaned against Luo Zheng, her Yang Soul trembling slightly. She gazed into the depths of the mountain stream and said, "Luo Zheng … what was that just now?"
"I don't know what it was," Luo Zheng replied.

Her usually cold voice trembled slightly. Even when they were in danger on the wooden bridge, Feng Ge had not revealed such emotions. Luo Zheng felt a trace of pity when he heard this. This time, he had really scared this child.
"I don't know either. Maybe it didn't care about us," Luo Zheng said.
In this kind of situation, there was no other choice but to resign themselves to fate. Luo Zheng's state of mind calmed down, but Feng Ge, who had encountered this kind of situation for the first time, was somewhat at a loss.
"Let's leave here," Feng Ge said again. She did not want to stay here for even a moment longer.
"Where are those black monkeys outside? Have they left? "Luo Zheng asked.
Feng Ge sized them up, then shook her head dispiritedly. "They're still guarding there …"
"Then it's settled."
As Luo Zheng spoke, he struggled to get up from the ground.
After a short rest, his strength had recovered slightly. At least he could support himself to sit up.
"You're better?" Feng Ge asked in surprise.
Seeing Luo Zheng collapse, Feng Ge was really out of her wits. Although Luo Zheng struggled to get up, she faintly felt a glimmer of hope.
She looked cold and independent, but deep down, she was extremely dependent on others. It was just that in the Heavenly Palace, it was very difficult for her to show this attitude.

Feng Ge hurriedly went up to help him up.
Luo Zheng turned his head and looked around the mountain stream. Then, he moved to the side of the mountain wall and sat down.
The situation was not as bad as Feng Ge had described. No matter what the monster was, it was good news that it did not attack them.
Those black monkeys were very stubborn and kept guarding outside.
Six to eight hours later, Luo Zheng could feel his surroundings gradually turning dark. The effects of the Fruit of Darkness were beginning to fade away.
Although Luo Zheng had tested the duration of the Fruit of Darkness and found that it could last for 14 hours, the duration of each fruit was both long and short.
To be on the safe side, he asked Feng Ge to take out two fruits, one for each of them to swallow. The originally dark world gradually regained a trace of light.
"How many Fruits of Darkness are left?" Luo Zheng asked.
Feng Ge's spatial ring shimmered with a faint light. She counted and said, "There are 14 more."
"Fourteen …"
According to the longest duration, if he and Feng Ge each took seven, it would only last for 48 hours, which was a little more than four days.
If they could not find the black and white tree in four days, they would be swallowed by the darkness. The final outcome was obvious.
Seeing that the black monkeys were very patient, waiting was not a solution.
Luo Zheng slowly stood up and said, "Let's go in and take a look."
"Go in?" Feng Ge's eyes widened.
Recalling the cold touch of those tentacles, Feng Ge could not help but shiver.
"Staying here is just waiting for death," Luo Zheng said.
Feng Ge wanted to say something, but in the end, she chose to remain silent. Luo Zheng's words made sense. She was not willing to die in the Dark Domain. Perhaps the monster was not as scary as she thought.
Luo Zheng's strength had recovered. It was not a big problem for him to walk normally.
The two walked into the depths of the mountain stream, one in front and one behind.
The two sides of the mountain stream continued to narrow. Originally, it was only about ten feet wide, but after walking for a while, the width of the mountain stream continued to narrow until it was only one or two feet wide. It was practically a stone crevice. Luo Zheng and Feng Ge could only walk sideways.
After passing through the narrow stone crevice for a few miles, the road ahead suddenly opened up. The two of them looked around and their faces were filled with surprise.
The mountain walls on both sides of the stone crack had been chiseled and carved with all kinds of murals. If one looked carefully, these murals were actually huge Sanskrit characters!
Looking at the Sanskrit characters, there was a triangular cave in the mountain wall. Black tentacles extended from the cave and hung on the ground from above.
"Those tentacles …" Feng Ge was shocked when she saw the tentacles.
"Hua hua hua …"
Feng Ge's movement attracted the tentacles' attention.
All the tentacles quickly danced and rushed toward Feng Ge and Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng grabbed Feng Ge and covered her mouth. At the same time, the two of them crawled on the ground.
The tentacles swept over their heads and kept sweeping the space around them. A few tentacles kept probing Feng Ge and Luo Zheng. In the end, they found nothing and slowly retracted back into the triangular cave.
If Feng Ge entered the Nirvana Realm with her physical body, she would have goosebumps under the tentacles' probing.
However, she seemed to have understood a little now. Her eyes were bright and she wanted to tell Luo Zheng what she had observed. However, Luo Zheng made a shushing gesture, indicating that he knew very well.
These tentacles could not see the two of them.
The black monkey was a creature of the Dark Realm. The tentacles were also creatures of the Dark Realm. They were the same kind of creatures, so the tentacles could detect their existence very keenly.
However, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge came from outside the Dark Realm. Their form was fundamentally different from the creatures of the Dark Realm.
The two of them had swallowed the Fruit of Darkness and could merge into the Dark Realm. However, the energy of the Fruit of Darkness had been absorbed by the two of them, so the energy that escaped was very little.
Perhaps the tentacles had sensed something, but there was no way to confirm it.
Luo Zheng patted Feng Ge's shoulder and encouraged her to continue moving forward. As long as she did not make any noise, they should be safe.
After walking for a while, Luo Zheng found that there was more than one triangular cave.
Every fifty to sixty feet, there would be a triangular cave, from which countless tentacles could be seen hanging down. It was unknown what kind of creature was lying in these caves. From the outside, one could not see anything except for the messy pile of tentacles.
Just as Luo Zheng was carefully observing the mountain wall, Feng Ge pointed ahead.
They had reached the end. It was a circular valley.
The walls of the valley were also engraved with huge Sanskrit characters. The mountain wall was covered with densely packed triangular caves. There were many tentacle monsters stuffed in them.
However, Luo Zheng's gaze was fixed on the center of the valley. The darkness suddenly stopped there!
A soft ray of sunlight pierced through the sky like a sharp sword. The light covered an area of about 10 feet, bathing a tombstone.

Land of Shelter
These days, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge had been living under the candlelight.
Compared to the absolute darkness of the Dark Domain, the candlelight was naturally very bright. However, it was not on the same level as the sunlight outside the Dark Domain.
After they swallowed the Fruit of Darkness, they became more accustomed to the Dark Domain and could see things in absolute darkness.
They were once accustomed to the darkness and blended into it. When they saw the real light again, it was so dazzling!
Feng Ge's eyes were wide open. She came to the same conclusion as Luo Zheng, but she did not dare to speak. She could not help but lean against Luo Zheng's shoulder and whisper, "Is that outside the Dark Domain?"
Inside the Dark Domain and the Temple, one could not leave the Nirvana Shore. Apart from these two places, one could leave.
Feng Ge had been trapped for these days. Naturally, she wanted to leave very much.
It would be best if she could get out of here …
However, according to the rules of the Nirvana Shore, she had to enter from where she left. The next time she returned to the Nirvana Shore, she would still be trapped here.
The tentacles that drilled into those triangular caves were extremely sensitive. These tentacles did not have eyes and used their perception energy to capture prey. Luo Zheng and Feng Ge also contained energy. However, they were not creatures of the Dark Domain. Naturally, the tentacles could not detect them.

"Hualala …"

Luo Zheng was much calmer. Some things were not so scary as long as they understood the rules.

With the existence of these tentacles, there shouldn't be any other Dark Realm creatures in this valley. They should be able to safely enter the pillar of light.

Wisps of black smoke emitted a pungent smell that rushed into Luo Zheng's nose. The smell was as if tens of thousands of suckling pigs had been roasted …
The tentacles were not the only thing that disappeared in the light. The black spears that the pig-faced monsters had also disappeared.
Feng Ge sat on the inner side of the pillar of light. Her body could not help but tremble. She was not used to the feeling of brushing shoulders with death.
"Hiss hiss hiss …"
The tentacles did not seem to believe in superstition. They still rushed into the pillar of light mindlessly.
Some of the thicker tentacles that rushed in were not burned by the light. They landed on the ground and Luo Zheng's body and kept bouncing. They were like barbecued meat on a metal plate. They quickly turned into a black lump and emitted a burnt smell. Finally, they were completely eliminated.
The pig-faced monsters also rushed out of the pillar of light. They stood in the darkness and kept roaring and cursing. They even stabbed their black spears into the pillar of light.

Temporary Return

"What's the matter?" Luo Zheng smiled.
"Can you come out of the Holy Tomb?" the dark green pig-faced monster asked.

This pig-faced monster didn't seem to be very smart. Under such circumstances, he would definitely die if he came out of the light pillar. Luo Zheng wasn't an idiot.
"This is our Holy Tomb. It's a pure place that can't be tainted. You've violated our taboo," the dark green pig-faced monster said again.
Luo Zheng rolled his eyes. The reason he and Feng Ge escaped here was that the black monkey had forced them into a corner. What was the point of this round of criticism now? He could only say helplessly, "Then what do you think we should do?"
"Can you come out from the Saint's Tomb?" the pig-faced monster repeated its question.

As he disappeared, the pig-faced monsters surrounding the light pillar began to curse again. Even the leading cyan pig-faced monster was cursing, asking Luo Zheng to come out and face his death.

In the Taishi Palace.

Golden flames were constantly flowing in it, releasing terrifying energy.

"Concubine Yan, we've tried three times, but we've failed. His Highness Feng Ge can't leave the other side. We can't pull him out by force, unless he happens to go to a place where the power is weak …" one of the old men said.
There were some methods in the Mother World that could forcibly pull people out of the other side. For example, methods like Soul-entangling Silk could trap people on the other side, but they could still be forcibly pulled out by external forces.
However, the power of the temple and the dark realm was too strong. Even though Concubine Yan had set up a magic array, she still couldn't wake Feng Ge up.

Although so much time had passed and Feng Ge had been lucky to survive, her reaction was definitely not a good sign.
As soon as Concubine Yan's voice fell, Feng Ge, who was sitting in the center of the magic array, trembled slightly. Fear appeared on his cold and beautiful face. His body kept trembling, and he even made a faint whimpering sound.
That was the reaction of the Yang Soul's fear transmitted to the physical body. God knew what her daughter was going through. Concubine Yan's heart ached when she saw this.
"It's not a big problem if we contribute our soul power, but the soul power is too strong. I'm afraid Prince Feng Ge's soul can't bear it," another old man said, shaking his head.
If they continued to strengthen their soul power, Feng Ge's soul would be torn apart.
"How do you know if you don't try? Look at her now. I'm afraid she will die on the other side at any time … "Concubine Yan said, gritting her teeth.
After the only two disciples of the Heavenly Palace returned, they immediately reported to Concubine Yan about the dark realm. Concubine Yan knew how dangerous the dark realm was and didn't want her daughter to stay there for a moment.
Under Concubine Yan's strong request, the three old men could only obey. The huge magic array began to operate again. Concubine Yan also took out the Golden Crow Divine Fire to control the magic array.

Chapter 2954
Imperial Concubine Yan's Promise

Luo Zheng's two Nirvana Realm tokens greedily absorbed the energy of the divine crystals. He had absorbed more than 100,000 divine crystals in a row. The energy of these divine crystals was absorbed by the tokens, and was continuously converted into Nirvana force.
After recovering more than half of his Paramita Force, Luo Zheng's movements slowed down. At the same time, he stared at Ling Shuang and said with a smile, "It seems that you have successfully left the Temple of Heaven!"
Luo Zheng was in the Dark Domain and naturally did not know the situation outside. He was also worried that Ling Shuang would be in danger when she left.

During the time when Luo Zheng was in the Nirvana Realm, Xiaoyin reported everything in the courtyard to Ning Yudie.
Now that she suddenly saw that Luo Zheng was awake, Xiaoyin was naturally shocked.

Before Xiaoyin answered, two figures, one in blue and one in green, appeared on the second floor of the courtyard. The woman in the blue robe was Feng Ge, and the one in green was Concubine Yan.
Seeing that Concubine Yan had come in person, Luo Zheng understood something.
Since his identity had been seen through by Feng Ge, there was no way he could hide it from Concubine Yan.
Luo Zheng got up and bowed to Concubine Yan, saying, "Luo Zheng didn't know that Concubine Yan was coming. Please forgive me for not coming out to meet you."
Concubine Yan's eyes were smiling, and a trace of charm appeared on her face. She said with a smile, "If you want my forgiveness, it's very simple. You have to protect my daughter and leave the Dark Domain unharmed!"
"Mother!" Feng Ge stood behind Concubine Yan and complained.

"If I can leave that damned place safely, I will ensure the safety of Prince Feng Ge," Luo Zheng replied respectfully.

She took a step forward and stared at Luo Zheng. She said with a serious face, "I only have one daughter, Feng Ge. I won't allow anything to happen to her. What I want is a 100% promise!"
Luo Zheng frowned slightly. Even the faces of Ning Yudie and the other girls darkened slightly.
Concubine Yan could naturally sense these emotions. However, she changed her usual gentle image and looked more and more serious. "As long as Feng Ge can leave the Dark Domain and the Nirvana Realm safely, I will give you an unimaginable reward, a peerless gift!"
If someone else spoke to Luo Zheng like this, Luo Zheng would have chosen to send them away.
However, when Luo Zheng first broke into the Taishi Palace, Concubine Yan had helped him lead the way. On Taihao Mountain, Concubine Yan had also suppressed Emperor Shou.
Now, Concubine Yan's attitude was completely out of her eagerness to protect her daughter. Luo Zheng could understand her.
Luo Zheng cupped his hands again and said, "Concubine Yan, don't be anxious. The Dark Domain is not as dangerous as you think. We are not facing a desperate situation."
In the cave of light, it was safe.
Moreover, those pig-faced monsters were not very intelligent. There was still a chance to escape.
Luo Zheng's sincere expression calmed Concubine Yan down. What she was most afraid of was that Luo Zheng would leave her daughter behind. She had been judging how to communicate with Luo Zheng all the way from the Taishi Palace.

Pure Witch

Concubine Yan personally sent Feng Ge to the top of Greater One Mountain.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were well-prepared. They sat cross-legged again with their eyes closed.

Luo Zheng kept struggling with his feet. However, half of his body was bound by the bronze plate. The space for him to move was limited. He could not get rid of those things.
The more he struggled, the more things were attracted to him. In a short while, Luo Zheng felt that his feet, legs, and even his back were covered with those things!
"What the hell is this …"
Luo Zheng could not see anything. He could only grit his teeth and bear with it. He finally understood what the pig-faced monsters meant.
Being thrown into the black pool and being sucked by those unknown things was a fate worse than death.

Reborn Body

The octopuses left the cave one by one and tangled with each other again, forming a rope that was thousands of feet long. It carried Luo Zheng over the mountain range and threw Luo Zheng into the Black Pool.

Giant Beast

This time, it was Luo Zheng's turn to be dumbfounded.

Chapter 2965
