No wonder …
When Luo Zheng saw the giant bird under the Green Jade World, he was extremely shocked.
This giant bird was the king of a civilization!
In Luo Zheng's era, even the strongest powerhouses of Nuwa, Shennong, Dihong, and the Liyuan Clan could not be compared to the Wing King.
The Wing King grew rapidly before Luo Zheng's eyes. It grew from the size of a fist to a giant bird. The entire Green Jade World grew bigger and more prosperous.
Just as the Green Jade Spirit said, the Tool of Civilization recorded the birth, beginning, prosperity, and destruction of the Green Jade Civilization.
However, during the most prosperous period of the Green Jade Civilization, rebels finally appeared.
The entire Green Jade World was split into two factions, falling into the chaos of years of war.
The Green Jade Spirit explained to Luo Zheng, "The rebels were controlled by the Reclaimers in the Nirvana Realm. The Green Jade Civilization was almost destroyed."
Seeing this, Luo Zheng asked, "If that's the case, the Wing King also chose the Path of the End and failed in the end?"
"He did fail," the Green Jade Spirit said with some regret, "The Wing King found the clues left behind by the No.1 Ruler Civilization and found a way to integrate the Path of the Great Unity. However, he failed in the end."

Chapter 2970
Only for an instant
The Grand Unification of Dao was the Origin Source Energy of the entire Chaotic Origin.
Everything in the Primal Chaos was formed from the Dao Fusion Energy.
When the Primal Chaos first opened, the Dao Fusion Energy was split into 3,000 different energies, known as the 3,000 Deity Daos.
There were many experts who cultivated the 3,000 Deity Daos by themselves. But until now, no one had been able to fuse them together and complete the Grand Unification of Dao Fusion.
Even so, Luo Zheng did not give up on fusing the Dao Fusion Energy. But in the recent few years, he had been immersed in the Paramita and did not spend much time on fusing the Deity Daos.
'Heh, this fellow is talking nonsense again. The 3,000 Deity Daos have never been fused together perfectly. Not in the past, and not in the future.' 9527 refuted in Luo Zheng's mind.
Hearing 9527's affirmation, Luo Zheng took the opportunity to raise his doubts to the Spirit of Green Jade, 'According to what I know, there are many people who cultivated the 3,000 Deity Daos in the Mother World. But no one has succeeded in fusing them together. Even the Ancient God Hundu who once witnessed the Grand Unification of Dao Fusion and grasped the Truth could not do it …'
'The Primal Chaos Ancient God who grasped the Truth? You should be referring to the Children of Truth. Hehe … 'The Spirit of Green Jade faintly laughed before saying,' The Children of Truth are just some housekeepers after the Primal Chaos was created. The Truth they grasped originally has a flaw. '
'The Truth has a flaw? That's impossible right? 'Luo Zheng revealed a look of astonishment.
'Impossible! What a load of nonsense! The Truth is the strongest energy in the Primal Chaos. How could it have a flaw? Nonsense! '9527 inexplicably became agitated.
The reason why the Ancient God Hundu was supreme in the Primal Chaos was because they each grasped a Truth that could not be surpassed. That was an existence above all other energy. This Spirit of Green Jade actually said that the Truth had a flaw? 9527 found it hard to accept.
Who knew that the Spirit of Green Jade sneered, 'I was once a Child of Truth. I grasped a Truth before. Why would I speak nonsense?'
"You once controlled the truth?" Luo Zheng's eyes were full of shock.
"That's right. In the era when the 750th ruler's civilization was ruled by the Mi Tian clan, I was one of the Children of Truth, the Primal Chaos Ancient God you speak of," the jade spirit said calmly, as if this was a very ordinary matter.
Once the Dao Fusion Grand Unification energy gave birth to chaos, it would completely disappear. Therefore, living beings born after the chaos was created would never be able to witness the Dao Fusion Grand Unification. Naturally, they would not be able to become primal chaos ancient gods.
The original owner of 9527 was also a creature from the previous round of chaos.
All the connate dao treasures had been passed down from the previous cycle of chaos.
The jade spirit was born in the 749th chaos. After the 749th chaos was destroyed, all the energy returned and the Dao Fusion Grand Unification was formed again. Only then did it have the opportunity to observe the Dao Fusion Grand Unification. After comprehending the Truth from the Dao Fusion Grand Unification, it became a primal chaos ancient god in the next round of chaos.
The Mi Tian clan was also a ruler's civilization. Luo Zheng had heard the Ear Mice mention it before. However, he did not expect the jade spirit to be the primal chaos ancient god of this civilization, which was also the Child of Truth …
And from the jade spirit's tone, the Child of Truth was just the steward of the next round of chaos. It made it sound so easy!
"But why is there a flaw in the Truth?" Luo Zheng murmured. Although the jade spirit's words were marvelous, he still found it hard to believe.
"Because the Dao-Fusion Grand Unification energy they observed was flawed, the truth naturally became flawed as well." The Spirit of the Green Jade directly replied, "In other words, other than the Dao-Fusion Grand Unification observed by the No.1 Sovereign Civilization, all subsequent Dao-Fusion Grand Unification energies have flaws!"
This reason was very accurate and there was basically no way to refute it.
"Then what kind of civilization is this No.1 ruler's civilization?" Luo Zheng asked.
The chaos had always been alternating between destruction and birth. However, the beginning of everything was the No.1 ruler's civilization. Naturally, Luo Zheng was very curious about this civilization.
"The No.1 ruler's civilization is called the Instant Civilization," the jade spirit said.
"The Instant Civilization?"
"Yes, this civilization only existed for a moment. After that, the entire chaos was destroyed," the jade spirit continued.
A civilization had to go through hundreds of millions of years from birth to prosperity. However, the Instant Civilization only existed for a moment. Luo Zheng felt that his brain was not enough to comprehend it.
The jade spirit seemed to understand Luo Zheng's confusion and said, "To you and me, a moment is like a blink of an eye. However, to the Instant Civilization, it is an eternity. Their concept of time is different from yours and mine! Nearly half of the Sanskrit was created by the Instant Civilization. You should have seen Sanskrit when you entered the Nirvana Realm through cultivation. "
"Sanskrit was created by the Instant Civilization …"
Luo Zheng had long known that Sanskrit was spread from the Nirvana Realm. However, he had no way of knowing who created Sanskrit.
He did not expect that these Sanskrit could be traced back to the beginning of the chaos.
While Luo Zheng was conversing with the Green Jade Spirit, scenes were constantly evolving in the chaotic illusory realm. The Green Jade Civilization was divided into two large groups and launched an extremely intense war.
In the war, the Wing King appeared again. The wings all over his body were blooming with a colorful luster.
Luo Zheng was very familiar with this luster. It was the Fusion Energy. However, this Fusion Energy was more abundant and powerful. It seemed that there was a trace of indescribable energy contained within it. Although this trace of energy was small, it played the role of the finishing touch.
Although it was just an illusion, Luo Zheng could still feel the vast power!
"This is the perfect Fusion Energy? This is the unification of the Fusion Energy? "Luo Zheng asked.
Luo Zheng thought that he could get a definite answer, but the Green Jade Spirit said, "We once thought that the Winged Monarch had mastered the perfect Dao fusion energy. At the last moment of the destruction of the Green Jade Civilization, we also understood that it wasn't perfect …"
If it was the perfect Fusion Energy, then the chaos would have stopped in the era of the jade civilization. The chaos would not have been pierced by that needle, and the Path of the End would have been successful.
"It's still not perfect?" Luo Zheng was shocked.
"Yes, the Wing King made up for one flaw in the Fusion Energy. In fact, there are still two flaws in the Fusion Energy that have not been made up," the jade spirit said. "So we have been waiting, waiting for a suitable person to make up for the final two flaws."
"Then this person …" Luo Zheng hesitated.
"The person who got the weapon of our jade civilization," the jade spirit said.
Luo Zheng looked at Feng Ge. Her big eyes were bright and full of expression. She looked at the scenes that were constantly evolving in the chaotic illusion. Her face was full of curiosity. This scene was overflowing with brilliance and was indeed very eye-catching.
"You mean Feng Ge?" Luo Zheng asked.

Chapter 2971
Recognition
The Green Jade Spirit was also observing the two humans.
Even though there was a huge difference between the Green Jade Race and the Human Race, Feng Ge wasn't suitable to wield the Tool of Civilization.
"We have waited for too long. This isn't the first time you two have visited us." The Green Jade Spirit sighed before saying, "The most suitable person to wield the Tool of Civilization isn't the Pure Ones. Instead, it's someone who cultivates in fusing Dao energy. You two aren't suitable for us. Hence, please return …"
The Paramita Realm experts in the 13 Heavens of the Color Realm could not reach the Chaotic Origin Realm. Hence, it was impossible for them to spend a large amount of time cultivating in fusing Dao energy.
Even in the Mother World, only the apex experts from the superpowers could comprehend 3,000 Deity Daos like Fuxi, Chi You, and others.
The Green Jade Civilization hid in the Color Realm. It was basically impossible to find a suitable person.
But the Green Jade Civilization was sealed in the Green Jade City. It was an eternal existence.
Regardless of whether the Primal Chaos was destroyed or born, the Paramita Realm would not be affected.
They had endless time to wait. They could slowly identify the person they needed.
"Weng …"
The moment the Green Jade Spirit finished speaking, the Dao Accumulations in Luo Zheng's inner world had already gathered. Many Deity Daos began fusing together, forming a blazing white light that appeared before the Green Jade Spirit.
"The Dao energy you mentioned … is this?" Luo Zheng chuckled.
The surface of the Green Jade Spirit's body emitted a wave of astonishment. It took a large look, and already had an accurate judgment. "Two thousand nine hundred and ninety-four divine daos … Did you fuse them yourself?"
"Yes, and no." Luo Zheng answered.
In the Deity Realm, Luo Zheng had been fusing Deity Daos. It's just that the number of Deity Daos he fused increased, and the difficulty increased. In addition, he had been moving around everywhere, causing his progress to stagnate.
But with the appearance of the Evil Deities, Luo Zheng took a huge step forward. He cultivated according to the Deity Daos he had fused with the Evil Deities. Hence, it wasn't difficult for him to cultivate again. He rapidly reached the level of 2,994 Deity Daos.
"Interesting …" The Green Jade Spirit examined Luo Zheng from head to toe. At the same time, it said, "You aren't a Pure One. You are relying on the Fruit of Darkness to stay in the Paramita Realm, right?"
Luo Zheng nodded.
"But the Aragami is still a little weak. No … You are not qualified," the Spirit of the Green Jade muttered.
In the eyes of the Spirit of the Green Jade, the most suitable candidate was not only a Pure Witch, but also a living being who majored in the Path of Fusion. Although Luo Zheng cultivated the Path of Fusion, his strength was not enough. Compared to a Pure Witch, the Aragami was at least two levels weaker.
Although the Spirit of Green Jade had already denied Luo Zheng, it still released a beam of green light in an attempt to probe the strength of Luo Zheng's body.
"Buzz …"
As a green light swept over Luo Zheng's body, the Green Jade Spirit suddenly found something strange. "Eh?"
"What's wrong?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Your body doesn't contain the True Meaning of Strength Crusher Sage Physique?" The Spirit of the Green Jade was surprised again. "What is going on here? Although you are an Aragami, you do not practice the True Meaning of Holy Physique. How did you get your body to the Paramita Realm?"
There were many accidents in this world, and Luo Zheng was one of them.
Initially, the Spirit of Green Jade thought that it had seen through everything. However, Luo Zheng was obviously a weirdo that it could not understand.
"This …" Luo Zheng did not know how to explain it.
He could feel that his body contained the True Meaning of Dao. However, he could not tell what the True Meaning of Dao was.
"Your body is far stronger than an Aragami, but not as strong as a Pure Witch …" The Spirit of Green Jade was a little shaken.
All of the people in the Green Jade Civilization had sealed themselves and fell into a deep sleep. No one could give it any advice. It could only make its own judgment.
Once the Weapon of Civilization was given to him, it meant that the core of the entire Green Jade Civilization was in his hands. It had to be careful with this decision.
Luo Zheng stood still and did not say a word. He was also waiting patiently.
If it was something else, Luo Zheng would not be too interested.
Luo Zheng would definitely not let go of the mystery of the Fusion of Dao energy. Especially when the Spirit of Green Jade mentioned that there were three flaws in the Fusion of Dao energy!
"However, I can give you a chance. You can fuse with the Weapon of Civilization."
After the Spirit of Green Jade finished speaking, the chaotic void that was originally shrouding Luo Zheng and Feng Ge disappeared. They returned to the high platform once again. The rhombus-shaped spiky ball, which was also the Weapon of Civilization, was emitting a green luster as it floated in front of Luo Zheng.
The meaning was clear. It wanted Luo Zheng to fuse with his body directly.
However, Luo Zheng's body could not fuse with the Paramita Realm Token. Or rather, he did not know how to fuse with his body. He then said to the Weapon of Civilization, "I'm afraid I can't fuse with it here. Can I bring this thing out of the dark zone?"
As long as he left the dark zone, Luo Zheng could return to the Paramita Realm with his soul.
Among the three True Meanings of Dao that he cultivated, the True Meaning of Dao Technique Nature was still missing.
In fact, Luo Zheng's purpose on this trip to the Paramita Realm was to fuse a Paramita Realm Token for the True Meaning of Dao Technique Nature. However, after running to the dark zone, he encountered a series of troubles and almost lost his life here.
"Yes." The Spirit of Green Jade's voice came from the Weapon of Civilization.
After Luo Zheng asked Feng Ge to open the Sumeru Ring, he was about to put the Weapon of Civilization inside.
However, at this moment, several shadows rushed out from the darkness!
Whoosh! Whoosh!
The newcomers were the black monkeys lurking in the darkness.
The three black monkeys were also extremely shocked when they saw the Green Jade City.
The records of the Green Jade Civilization were extremely limited. However, based on their instincts, they also knew that this discovery was no small matter.
When the Spirit of Green Jade removed the illusion, they had already set foot on the Green Jade City and went straight to the high tower.
Swoosh! Swoosh!
Swoosh! Swoosh! Golden Sanskrit shone on the yellow paper on the three black monkeys' bodies.
Under the support of this light, each of the black monkeys exploded with astonishing strength and speed!
The black monkey in the front flew and grabbed the Weapon of Civilization in Luo Zheng's hand. Feng Ge, who was next to him, frowned.
Subconsciously, she didn't just hate those pig-faced monsters. These black monkeys had previously chased Luo Zheng and Feng Ge all the way. They also deserved to die.
"You're all courting death!"
A murderous intent flashed in the depths of Feng Ge's clear eyes.
He raised his hand, and a dazzling beam of light shot toward the black monkey.
Caw …
One of the black monkeys was hit by the light. The yellow paper on the surface of its body began to burn piece by piece.
However, it was these yellow paper pieces that blocked the light released by Feng Ge for a moment. The black monkey crashed into Luo Zheng with flames all over its body.
Bang! Bang!
The Weapon of Civilization was knocked out of his hand and fell out of the high tower.
"It fell down!"
"I must get my hands on it!"

Chapter 2972
Who gave you the courage?
The Weapon of Civilization drew an arc in the darkness and landed in a large, furry hand.
The owner of this large hand was the One-Eyed Monsters. They were another powerful force in the Dark Realm.
"Hey, hey …"
The One-Eyed Monsters and the Black Monkeys were lurking in the dark, observing every move in Green Jade City. Seeing the Black Monkeys take the lead, they also became anxious.
Unexpectedly, just as they hurriedly rushed into Green Jade City, they saw a glimmering green object fall down. Coincidentally, it was caught in the hands of one of the One-eyed Monsters.
The One-Eyed Monster was delighted. Before he could examine it carefully, he said, "We got it. Let's go!"
The other One-eyed Monsters followed closely behind, sprinting out of Green Jade City.
"One-eyed old thief! How dare you! "
When the remaining two Black Monkeys saw this scene, their faces were filled with rage.
These three Black Monkeys were elites stationed in the 13th Heaven. The three brothers were very close. Seeing that their third brother had sacrificed his life to help the One-Eyed Monsters, how could they be willing?
"Whoosh!"
The yellow paper on the two Black Monkeys flashed with golden light. They jumped down from the tower and shot toward the One-Eyed Monsters like two arrows leaving the bow.
Luo Zheng had spent so much effort to obtain the Weapon of Civilization, but it was obviously snatched away by other creatures. He was even more unwilling.
"Feng Ge! Let's chase! "
He dragged Feng Ge and jumped down from the tower.
Feng Ge was not very interested in the Weapon of Civilization. She did not know what it meant, but in her mind, what Luo Zheng needed was what she needed.
The two Black Monkeys' strength was about the same as the One-Eyed Monsters, but the Sanskrit on the yellow paper flashed, greatly strengthening the Black Monkeys' explosive power.
In just two or three breaths, the two Black Monkeys caught up to the One-Eyed Monsters.
"Ha Za! Ku Wo! "
Seeing the Black Monkeys coming menacingly, the One-Eyed Monsters ordered their tribesmen to stay and snipe.
The two One-eyed Monsters suddenly stopped in their tracks and turned around. A strange symbol abruptly appeared in their large eyes.
"Eye of Light!"
The innate ability of the Cyclops race was all in this eye. When the symbol appeared in their field of vision, the space within their field of vision was sliced apart, turning into a spatial cage that enveloped the two black monkeys.
"It's just a mere spatial lock!"
One of the black monkeys rammed forward, and the void in front of it suddenly bloomed with spiderweb-like cracks. At the same time, there was a crisp "clang" sound, and the sealed space was directly shattered by the black monkey!
The other black monkey stepped on the shoulder of the black monkey in front of it. With an extremely agile posture, it drew an arc and smashed into the body of the one-eyed monster at the front.
"Hand that thing over, or I'll eat you!" The black monkey threatened.
These black monkeys were extremely savage in the Dark Domain. They ate almost anything, including the One-eyed Monsters.
"In your dreams!"
An illusory halo of light flashed in the eye of the one-eyed monster. As the halo flashed, the black monkey was stunned. It was actually confused by the halo of light.
While the black monkey was in a daze, the One-Eyed Monster threw the Weapon of Civilization in its hand at the other One-Eyed Monster.
At this time, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge also caught up.
When Feng Ge saw these creatures of the Dark Domain, she instinctively felt a sense of disgust. Now that she was angry, she no longer held back.
"Brahma Immeasurable Sky …"
A ray of light circled around Feng Ge's jade finger.
Luo Zheng had seen the power of the Brahma Immeasurable Sky Light. This thing had no effect on creatures from outside the Dark Domain like Luo Zheng, but it had a terrifying destructive power on everything in the Dark Domain.
Any substance would be annihilated in this light!
Just as Feng Ge was about to unleash the Brahma Immeasurable Sky Light, the Weapon of Civilization that was thrown into the air suddenly stopped.
"Stop!" The Green Jade Spirit's voice came again. It didn't tell the black monkey and the One-Eyed Monster to stop, but it told Feng Ge to stop.
Other than Luo Zheng, why would Feng Ge listen to anyone else?
Fortunately, Luo Zheng reached out and grabbed Feng Ge's hand, saying, "Wait!"
The Green Jade Spirit also understood the terrifying strength of the Pure Witches in the dark domain. Just the ordinary light could make the Green Jade City mottled and full of potholes. However, the Green Jade City originally had the ability to self-repair, so it was not a problem.
However, when the Brahma Immeasurable Sky Light shone, it was enough to melt part of Green Jade City.
Although Green Jade City could still be repaired, it would still cost a lot, so the Green Jade Spirit was quite helpless.
The black monkey and the One-Eyed Monster didn't expect the Weapon of Civilization to have self-awareness. For a moment, they were stunned and didn't dare to fight for it.
"Who gave you the courage to steal from Green Jade City?"
The Green Jade Spirit's dignified voice came again.
The black monkey and the One-Eyed Monster also knew that Green Jade City was no small matter. They also had the mentality of going all out, so they ignored their own situation.
As soon as the Green Jade Spirit's voice fell, the entire Green Jade City seemed to come alive at this moment.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Green Jade Poisonous Bugs came out of Green Jade City one by one. In addition, there were also Green Jade Snakes and Green Jade Behemoths …
These Green Jade creatures were not to be underestimated. One Green Jade Giant Beast had almost wiped out the entire clan of the pig-faced monsters. Even the octopuses, which had no natural enemies in the Thirteenth Heaven, had been devoured by it one by one.
Moreover, they were facing hundreds and thousands of them …
There was almost no room for resistance. The two black monkeys and the One-Eyed Monster were directly drowned by the Green Jade army.
Even Luo Zheng felt a little scared when he saw the Green Jade army. Only Feng Ge opened his eyes wide, without the slightest trace of fear.
In her eyes, no matter how many Green Jade armies there were, they were just a ray of light.
After the Green Jade army killed the Hidden Realm creatures, they only glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently. Then, they all merged into Green Jade City. They constantly wandered around the city and quickly returned to their original positions.
Luo Zheng walked forward and took the Weapon of Civilization. Then, he asked, "Didn't you say that the clansmen have all fallen into a deep sleep?"
"Ha … clansmen. These Green Jade creatures can't be considered clansmen," the Green Jade Spirit said indifferently.
This Green Jade Spirit was once a Child of Truth, a Chaos Ancient God! To be recognized as clansmen by it, they must have been peerless powerhouses in the Chaos.
Luo Zheng once again put the Weapon of Civilization into Feng Ge's Sumeru Ring. The two of them walked along the road outside Green Jade City. The ground under their feet began to tremble.
"Rumble …"

Chapter 2973

On the Watch
The creatures of the Dark Realm lurking in the dark broke out in a cold sweat when they saw what happened to the black monkeys and the One-Eyed Monster.
They had wanted to risk everything and rush into Qingyu City.
However, these creatures of the Dark Realm were not as strong as the black monkeys or the One-Eyed Monster, so their rationality prevailed. Now, they were all glad that they had escaped with their lives. They could only watch as Luo Zheng and Feng Ge left.
A pure witch alone was enough to make them suffer, so they did not dare to follow them.
The Dark Realm occupied more than half of the Thirteenth Layer of Heaven, and the space inside was extremely vast.
Luo Zheng's original plan was to turn back along the mountain stream.
However, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were chased by the black monkeys all the way and were in a panic. After a part of the mountain stream was destroyed by Feng Ge, the two could not find their way back.
However, with Feng Ge's identity as a pure witch, their safety was still guaranteed when they walked through the Dark Realm. As long as they walked in one direction, they would eventually be able to walk out of the Dark Realm.
After climbing over a small hill, Luo Zheng felt that the surrounding darkness was a little deeper. The effect of the Fruit of Darkness was about to disappear.
"Feng Ge, give me a Fruit of Darkness," Luo Zheng said.
Feng Ge did not do as Luo Zheng said. With a wave of his hand, a light shrouded Luo Zheng's head. He smiled sweetly and said, "In this way, we don't need any Fruit of Darkness."
Wherever the light reached, it was equivalent to outside the Dark Realm. There was indeed no need to swallow the Fruit of Darkness.
"Isn't this a little inconvenient?" Luo Zheng said.
In any case, it was safer to swallow the Fruit of Darkness.
Who knew that Feng Ge snorted and said, "If you don't want to, go on your own!"
Hearing her childish answer, Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of helplessness.
However, although the Feng Ge in front of her was a little crazy, he was still much cuter than the cold and expressionless Feng Ge.
The two beams of light moved bit by bit in the dark domain. Wherever the two of them passed, the light that enveloped their bodies was like a sharp blade, forcibly digging out two trenches on the ground.
Under the illumination of the light, the Dark Domain plants along the way also quickly disappeared.
Along the way, the creatures of the dark realm who saw this scene were all deeply shocked. They didn't even dare to breathe loudly, not to mention having any thoughts about the two of them. They hid themselves well.
If he angered these two people, a beam of light would shine over him, and his life would be lost for no reason.
Luo Zheng didn't know what path he had chosen. After walking for more than four hours, he was surrounded by uneven hills and mountains.
If they encountered some relatively tall mountains, Feng Ge was too lazy to climb over them. He simply shot out a beam of light, directly melting a hole at the bottom of the mountain and passing through it. This was really like cutting through a mountain when you meet a mountain, cutting through a river when you meet a river. Their journey was unimpeded.
As the two of them walked more and more desolate, they finally arrived in front of a large mountain. With this mountain as the boundary, the other side was the other side of the Dark Domain.
Perhaps because they had stayed in the dark domain for too long, even with Feng Ge's light enveloping them, when Luo Zheng climbed to the top of the mountain, he still felt that the light outside the dark domain was dazzling.
"Huh? Is that … the Temple of Heaven? "Luo Zheng stood at the top of the mountain and unexpectedly saw the Temple of Heaven.
What he saw was the front of the Temple of Heaven!
This meant that they had taken a big detour and came out from the other side of the dark domain. It seemed that they had taken a lot of unnecessary detours, but at least they had come out.
Before preparing to leave the dark domain, Luo Zheng suddenly had a trace of doubt in his heart.
Everything in the dark domain was afraid of light, or they were completely conflicted with the matter in the light.
Even the solid green jade melted quickly under the illumination of the light like snow in summer. However, compared to other substances, green jade was much stronger and naturally melted slower. Feng Ge had left a lot of traces in Green Jade City.
Although the Instrument of Civilization could withstand the light released by Feng Ge, in fact, it would also be destroyed by the light. If he took the Instrument of Civilization out of the dark domain, wouldn't it be destroyed?
After Luo Zheng stopped, he took out the Instrument of Civilization again and told the Green Jade Spirit his doubts.
The Green Jade Spirit only gave him one answer: the candle.
The reason why the creatures in the dark domain wanted to get the candle was that, in their nature, they still desired the light. On the other hand, the candle was a bridge between the dark domain and the light domain.
If Luo Zheng wanted to fuse with the Instrument of Civilization, he would have to light the candle and enter the dark domain. This was probably the only way to fuse with the Nirvana token in the dark domain!
"But … if I fuse with the Nirvana token of the dark domain, after this thing is placed in my inner world, the light in my inner world will also eliminate it, right?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
The Green Jade Spirit just smiled faintly. "Light is originally a kind of energy. Let alone the light in your inner world, even the level of energy contained in the light of your mother world is not enough to eliminate the object in the dark domain."
"So that's how it is …" Luo Zheng nodded, slightly understanding in his heart.
This was not the first time he had heard the definition of energy level. In the divine domain, Luo Zheng had used the energy released by the Truth Crystal to break Dongfang Chunjun's killing move. The reason was that the energy level of the Truth Crystal was too high.
…
…
In front of the main entrance of the Temple of Heaven, a large number of Sun Souls had gathered.
During the last exploration of the Temple of Heaven, the Ear Rats had suffered too great a loss. These days, the Ear Rats were too lazy to even take care of the auction.
This time, it was the You Xiong Clan who planned to open the Temple of Heaven.
Last time, the You Xiong Clan had a conflict with Luo Zheng and lost several elites. Because of this, they had lost the qualification to enter the temple and escaped a disaster. Even the Jinwu Clan, the Shennong Clan, and other clans had escaped with them.
Now, they were making a comeback.
"Except for the Sun Souls of the You Xiong Clan, the Shennong Clan, and the Jinwu Clan, all unrelated people should leave quickly," a member of the You Xiong Clan shouted loudly. Not far away, dozens of people were spreading Soul-entangling Silk on the ground.
Strands of Soul-entangling Silk covered the ground, as if it had grown a shiny blue turf.
Not many superpowers had participated in the opening of the Divine Temple of Heaven. Whether it was the Liyuan Clan or the other superpowers, they had all suffered great losses. The Divine Temple of Heaven was simply too dangerous. Although it looked like a delicious cake on the surface, it wasn't something that just anyone could take.
Most of the people gathered at the entrance of the Temple of Heaven were Sun Souls of some small clans.
Ling Shuang sat on the hillside opposite the Temple of Heaven and stared blankly at the gate of the Temple of Heaven.
This kind of guarding had been going on for a long time.
She had countless illusions in her eyes. She saw Luo Zheng pushing open the gate of the Temple of Heaven and walking out of it, but the gate was still motionless.

Chapter 2974
Seeing through her identity
Although Ling Shuang knew that her wish was unrealistic, she still waited here.
You Xiong Family people ceaselessly spread the soul-entangling silk, and soon, they reached the hillside.
A You Xiong Family man glanced at Ling Shuang and said, "Unrelated people, leave now. If you get stuck by the soul-entangling silk, don't ask us to help you!"
Hearing him, Ling Shuang responded slowly, "It's just opening the gate of the Mi Tian Divine Temple. Who wants to get in? Even Ear Mouse Family didn't do this. "
When Ear Mouse Family opened the gate of the Mi Tian Divine Temple, they only spread a layer of soul-entangling silk in front of the temple and set up a magic formation. They didn't do it as openly as You Xiong Family.
"Hm! Cut the crap! If you don't leave, don't blame us for being merciless! "said that You Xiong Family man coldly.
When the soul-entangling silk arrived, it wouldn't be so easy to leave the Nirvana Realm.
Ling Shuang was angry, but she was alone after all. She stood up reluctantly and prepared to leave this hillside.
No one in the Nirvana Realm could see each other clearly, and neither side could identify each other. Therefore, Ling Shuang chose to give in, and everything should be fine.
However, a You Xiong Family man silently looked at Ling Shuang, and a beam of light flashed across his eyes.
On the day when Ear Mouse opened the Mi Tian Divine Temple, this man was also there. Ling Shuang fought against Ji Yan, and then she stayed with Luo Zheng.
This You Xiong Family man couldn't see Ling Shuang's face, but her appearance, soul power, and voice were all the same!
After the battle in front of the Mi Tian Divine Temple, You Xiong Family and the Great Yi Heaven Palace became enemies. In the Nirvana Realm, these two large families had fought against each other for quite a few times.
Not only that, after You Xiong Family realized how powerful Luo Zheng's body was, they started to work out a plan, hoping that they could take Luo Zheng out.
Although Luo Zheng's body had the absolute upper hand against the sun soul, and was even invincible, it wasn't impossible to deal with it.
However, Luo Zheng's body had never returned since he entered the Dark Realm. You Xiong Family had been waiting for an opportunity.
It would be best if Luo Zheng died in the dark realm. But, if Luo Zheng truly returned from the Sky Divine Temple, You Xiong Family, who was well-prepared, would certainly wipe him out with thunderous methods.
The woman in front of him had always been by his side …
When the You Xiong Tribesman saw Ling Shuang turn to leave, he suddenly said, "Wait."
Ling Shuang turned her head and asked curiously, "What is it?"
During the time when Luo Zheng was trapped in the Dark Realm, Ling Shuang had been numb. She had indeed let down her guard. Moreover, it was very difficult to confirm one's identity in the Nirvana Realm.
Swish …
Just as Ling Shuang's voice fell, a handful of azure Soul Entangling Silk scattered towards Ling Shuang, completely enveloping her body.
Looking at the Soul Entangling Silk on her body, Ling Shuang instantly showed an angry look. "What do you mean by this?"
In the Nirvana Realm, throwing Soul-breaking Sand, Soul-repelling Water, Soul-entangling Silk to the enemy meant declaring war. Ling Shuang didn't know when she had offended You Xiong Family people. Naturally, she asked angrily.
The other You Xiong Family people were confused as well. It was a critical moment to open the Sky Divine Temple. Why did this happen?
The You Xiong Family man shouted, "This woman is from the Heaven Palace! She was none other than the woman beside the physical body! Keep her! "
You Xiong Family people had been planning to deal with that body. Hearing this You Xiong Family man, the others immediately understood what was going on.
It was hard to confirm one's identity in the Nirvana Realm, but they would rather believe it than not. Therefore, they wouldn't let Ling Shuang go so easily.
Ling Shuang was also shocked when her identity was exposed by that unremarkable Sun Soul with a single sentence.
In a panic, she stepped back as fast as she could. "I … I'm not from the Heaven Palace …"
Her answer was hesitant, and her intention to escape aroused the suspicion of the You Xiong Family people. The other You Xiong Family people immediately surrounded Ling Shuang.
"If you're not from the Heaven Palace, just tell us the truth. Why did you run away?" asked the You Xiong Family hostage.
Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …
Light flashed in the hands of these You Xiong Family people. They joined hands and formed a soul net, which covered Ling Shuang.
If she was caught by these people, the situation would be even more troublesome.
Ling Shuang kept stepping back. Seeing that she couldn't avoid the soul net, she gritted her teeth, and the soul necklace hidden in front of her chest emerged. Buzz!
That incomparably sharp Spiritual Sword struck towards the Spiritual Net. With a "swish", it cut through the Spiritual Net. At the same time, Ling Shuang's Yang Soul twisted slightly and burrowed out from the opening.
"That's the soul sword!"

She was waiting for Luo Zheng here, and for no reason, she had provoked the You Xiong Family. Her identity had been exposed. How unlucky she was!

This soul sword contained an astonishingly sharp aura. Those You Xiong Family Sun Souls knew how powerful it was, but they couldn't get too close to her.
Ling Shuang ran westward from the hillside. More and more You Xiong Family people were chasing her. Not only the You Xiong Family, but also the Golden Crows, the Divine Farmer Clan, and the Minyue Clan.
These Sun Souls had all stepped into the 13th Level. Their soul cultivation was not much weaker than Ling Shuang's. They chased her all the way without any intention of giving up.
If this went on, Ling Shuang would fall into their hands sooner or later …
"No matter who you are, I advise you not to waste time!"
"What we're looking for is that physical body. As long as we catch that guy, we won't make things too difficult for you!"

Hearing the words behind her, Ling Shuang snorted coldly. "You want to catch me? Let's see what you're capable of!"
Whoosh! Whoosh!
Whoosh! Whoosh! Three soul arrows were shot out from her soul necklace at the same time. They drew a messy trajectory in the air and swept toward those Sun Souls at an incredible speed.
Two You Xiong Family people were still trying to persuade Ling Shuang. Facing the sudden soul arrows, they didn't manage to dodge. Before they could scream, they were pierced by the soul arrows. Their Sun Souls were severely injured.
"This woman is so powerful!"
"Everyone, be careful!"
"….."

Ling Shuang's Sun Soul had surpassed her peers. Relying on the soul necklace, she was able to compete with the top Sun Souls of the 13th Level.

The soul of the pig-faced monster, Huyun, was much stronger than Feng Ge's. He had been wondering what happened in the middle and why Huyun failed to possess Lingshuang.
When Feng Ge fell under the tomb, Luo Zheng thought she was completely finished. In the end, Feng Ge became a Pure Witch!
"I want to know what happened next?" Imperial Concubine Yan looked at Feng Ge blankly and asked again.
"Feng Ge became a Pure Witch. She got a new Nirvana Realm physical body, and it was extraordinarily powerful," Luo Zheng replied.
Imperial Concubine Yan's eyes showed a trace of surprise. At that time, she also knew that such a big movement in the depths of Feng Ge's soul would make the situation very troublesome. However, she did not expect the situation to develop like this. No one had encountered such a thing before.
"How strong is she? How is she compared to you?" Imperial Concubine Yan asked.
"Her physical body is several times stronger than mine. No one in the Thirteen Layers of Heaven can contend against her. Moreover, she can instantly kill the creatures of the Dark Realm," Luo Zheng replied.
In terms of strength, Feng Ge's Nirvana Realm body was far stronger than Luo Zheng's. It was unknown if it could fight against the Three-eyed Boy and his giant ape. However, even if the Three-eyed Boy and the giant ape were stronger than Feng Ge, Feng Ge could still use Brahma's Infinite Sky. Under this light, the creatures of the dark realm could not fight back.
"Can she leave the 13th Heaven?" Concubine Yan continued to ask.
The Pure Witches were creatures of the Nirvana Realm. They were all fixed in a certain layer of heaven. The Pure Witches in the upper Nirvana Realm were also known as the Masters of Heaven.
However, no matter how powerful the Lord of Heaven was, he could only stay in a certain heaven. If Feng Ge could ascend in the Nirvana Realm, then her identity as a Pure One would have infinite significance!
Luo Zheng had not considered this question. He said, "After we escaped from the Dark Realm, we quickly left the Nirvana Realm. We haven't tried it yet."
"Hmm." Concubine Yan nodded. She reached out and gently stroked Feng Ge's face. "Now it seems that when I killed the Pure One, its Yang Soul remained in Feng Ge's body and integrated with Feng Ge's Fire of Memory …"
"Is there a way to restore it?" Luo Zheng asked.
"The Fire of Memory is fragile and complicated, but it's not impossible. It's just that the process will be very troublesome," Concubine Yan said faintly.
Luo Zheng had seen a lot of Fire of Memory. Those Fire of Memory in the Divine Domain could form an ocean. For example, the Fire of Memory with tassels was taken out and stuffed back in …
However, these Fire of Memory were all intact, unlike Feng Ge's that had been broken.
However, there were many capable people in the Heavenly Palace, so there was always a solution.
"Come back to the Taidi Palace with me first. I've been so worried these days," Concubine Yan said with pity.
Unexpectedly, Feng Ge pushed Concubine Yan's hand away and snorted. "Who's going back with you? I want to stay by my husband's side."
"Husband?" Concubine Yan's face darkened.
Although Concubine Yan admired Luo Zheng very much, her daughter was the most favored princess in the Taidi Palace. How could she call him husband so casually? She even suspected that Luo Zheng had done something to Feng Ge …
Ling Shuang, Ning Yudie, and the others suddenly smiled. Luo Zheng had explained before that since Feng Ge crawled out of the grave, he had been particularly dependent on him.
Luo Zheng was embarrassed, but he could only brace himself to explain to Concubine Yan.
Feng Ge had lost his memory. His intelligence was that of a six or seven-year-old child. He should have chosen to rely on him out of conditioned reflex.
If Concubine Yan wanted to cure Feng Ge, she would definitely not agree to leave Feng Ge here.
When Feng Ge refused to cooperate, she stretched out a finger and gently brushed Feng Ge's forehead, causing Feng Ge to fall into a state of coma.
Feng Ge, who was invincible in the Nirvana Realm, naturally had no power to fight back in front of Concubine Yan. He was taken away by Concubine Yan just like that.
Ling Shuang understood what had happened in the dark realm and then chose to leave. But before she left, her eyes were slightly bitter.
Luo Zheng's return made this independent courtyard lively again.
These days, not only were Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin exhausted, but even Xiaoyin and Xiaoxin were lamenting. Everyone's mood was gloomy and obscure. Now, the clouds were dispelled and the sun could be seen.
Luo Zheng was also sad to let them suffer with him, so he accompanied them all these days.
Seven days later, Luo Zheng chose to go into seclusion.
The Instrument of Civilization was too important to Luo Zheng. The sooner he carried it, the sooner he could feel at ease.
The Instrument of Civilization was not only a powerful Nirvana token, but it also contained a flaw in the Great Unification of Dao.
Compared to this Nirvana token, Luo Zheng was more interested in this flaw.
"Whoosh …"
The True Meaning of Dao slowly spread in Luo Zheng's Inner World.
When this True Meaning spread, the entire Inner World became more harmonious and gentle.
Although thousands of creatures could not comprehend the True Meaning of Dao, just the aura emitted by the True Meaning was enough to benefit many talented people.
Some talented martial artists and True Gods even went into seclusion on the spot, intending to break through under the cover of this aura.
When he first introduced the Mighty God Totem, the terrifying power of the shore gave Luo Zheng's Inner World a strong pressure.
But when he put down the Instrument of Civilization, it was much quieter.
It looked like a diamond-shaped, thorny handicraft carved out of green jade. It did not look amazing.
But when it appeared, the entire Thousand Fan Territory shook.
"Buzz …"
Lines of Sanskrit began to flicker with golden light.
When he used the True Meaning of Good and Evil to carry the Mind's Eye, 104 Sanskrit lit up in the Thousand Fan Territory.
When he used the True Meaning of Eternal Sword to carry the Mighty God Totem, 140 Sanskrit lit up.
This time, when he used the True Meaning of Dao to carry the Instrument of Civilization, 366 Sanskrit lit up!
"Swoosh!"
Every time the Sanskrit characters shone, a chain would extend from them.
Three hundred and sixty-six chains shot out and wrapped around the weapon of civilization.
"This Instrument of Civilization should not be considered a Nirvana token in the Color Realm," Luo Zheng's incarnation murmured as he looked at the Instrument of Civilization not far away.
Generally speaking, no matter how strong the Nirvana tokens in the Color Realm were, they were mostly suitable for the Hundred Fan Territory. For example, the Mighty God Totem, as a Nirvana token of the 13th Heaven, only needed 140 Sanskrit to carry it.
"Heh …"

Flowers, trees, birds, beasts, heaven, earth, sun, moon, yin and yang, the five elements, everything was included.
According to the Jade Spirit, there was an additional Divine Path out of thin air?

The Blood Elemental Divine Path was also one of the Divine Paths. Its power might not be strong enough, and like other Divine Paths, it was far inferior to the Nirvana Realm Power.

Although I don't know what the other two flaws are, they definitely aren't elemental energy. It can't be the Divine Dao you speak of."
Hearing what the Jade Spirit said, Luo Zheng pouted. He was indeed taking it for granted.
The Jade Civilization had used the power of the entire civilization and still could not find the remaining flaws. How could the remaining two flaws be so easy to find?
After the merger, Luo Zheng began to consider another problem. He asked, "What kind of Nirvana Realm token does the Instrument of Civilization belong to? Descending type? Strengthening type? Or mystery type? "
Luo Zheng roughly explained the classification of these types to the Jade Spirit. The Jade Spirit replied, "The characteristics that you mentioned exist in the Instrument of Civilization. I will teach you the specific method of using it …"

When the Instrument of Civilization appeared in his Inner World, Luo Nian knew that his father must have brought in something strange.

Deciphering some Sanskrit in the mother world could earn a lot of money. If it was slightly more complicated, it would often cost a lot.

Every time Luo Zheng asked Luo Nian for help, it was always free …

"Any world, including the Mother World, once formed, the Primal Chaos Qi in the body will completely fade away. This is the most basic form of energy, and it is highly toxic to living beings. It is very difficult to nurture living beings in such an environment," the Green Jade Spirit said thoughtfully.
The aura of Chaos receded …
Only Sages and Nirvana Realm experts could travel in the Primal Chaos, and there were still a large number of low-level living beings in the Mother World. There was no aura of Chaos in the Mother World at all.
In Luo Zheng's Inner World, the Primal Chaos Seawater was still in an extremely primitive state, but the living beings in his Inner World were not afraid. This Primal Chaos Sea had even become a place of birth for living beings.
Although Luo Zheng understood what the Green Jade Spirit meant, he was even more confused. What was the use of such an Inner World? Until now, his Master's whereabouts were still unknown, and he had no way to ask for the reason.
Looking at Luo Zheng's confused face, the Green Jade Spirit immediately said, "In a situation like yours, I have only seen one possibility."
"What possibility?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Such a situation would occur in the early stages of the Land of Origin. I have observed it after I became the Son of Truth," the Green Jade Spirit's eyes revealed a deep light, which was naturally a very long memory.
The Green Jade Civilization was the 749th dominant civilization, and he was the Son of Truth of the 750th civilization. Naturally, he could observe the birth of the 750th civilization.
"What is the Land of Origin?" Luo Zheng continued to ask.
"The earliest form of your Mother World is the Land of Origin. If the entire Primal Chaos is regarded as an egg, then the Mother World is the egg yolk, and the Land of Origin is when the egg yolk first formed …" the Green Jade Spirit replied, "If your Inner World is really the Land of Origin, then it should not be in your body in name, but born somewhere in the Primal Chaos. Have you noticed it?"
Reminded by the Green Jade Spirit, Luo Zheng's body suddenly shook.
His Inner World was bounded by 1,080 Sanskrit characters, which defined the scope of his Inner World. However, when his Inner World was completed, Luo Zheng did faintly feel that his Inner World was located somewhere in the Primal Chaos!
At that time, Luo Zheng stayed in the Divine Realm and thought that all living beings from the Mother World were like this. Now it seemed that his idea was very wrong!
"It seems that I was right," the Green Jade Spirit said with a smile.
"Isn't there only one Land of Origin?" Luo Zheng asked.
According to the Green Jade Spirit, the Primal Chaos was the egg yolk, and the Mother World was the egg yolk. Only one Land of Origin could be born, right?
"Father, haven't you heard of a double-yolk egg?"
Luo Nian, who had been listening silently at the side, interjected.
Luo Zheng glared fiercely at Luo Nian. According to this analogy, it did make sense, but it was just an analogy. How could it be the same thing?
"When a Primal Chaos is born, the Land of Origin is indeed not an isolated existence. It is possible to give birth to several or even more than a dozen Lands of Origin, from which different civilizations emerge. The Green Jade Civilization had been hiding in the 13th Heaven for too long, and the Green Jade Spirit also felt that there was not enough time. Choosing Luo Zheng was purely a plan to make do, but as the Green Jade Spirit understood, it became increasingly aware that Luo Zheng was not simple.
Just as Luo Zheng and the Green Jade Spirit were discussing, on the other side of the Primal Chaos, the Divine Territory had been wandering in the boundless Primal Chaos for a long time.
The Divine Territory was like a bright gem, shuttling through huge Primal Chaos clouds one after another. These Primal Chaos clouds were really too big. With the speed of the Divine Territory, it often took several months to pass through a Primal Chaos cloud.
Before Luo Zheng left, he handed the Green Parasol to Luo Xiao, but the one who really controlled the direction of the Divine Territory was Elder Jin.
He stood inside the sun, holding the four rings in his hand, and his consciousness drifted outside the Divine Territory, watching every movement around the Divine Territory.
Controlling the direction of the ship of the Divine Territory was a boring thing. There was no scenery to speak of in the vast outer region, but Elder Jin was extremely responsible. For several months, he almost didn't even blink his eyes, maintaining a constant movement.
"What is that?" Elder Jin's eyes suddenly widened.

Chapter 2987
The Graveyard
The Divine Realm was an extremely large world. Chiyou had spent a great deal of effort to open it up.
This tombstone was about the same size as the entire Divine Realm. Even Elder Gold was shocked!
Amidst his shock, Elder Gold thought of a rumor.
Although the chaos was vast and empty, it hid many secrets and danger zones. There was no turning back once one entered these danger zones. The Sinkhole Graveyard was one of them.
Rumor had it that as long as one saw a huge tombstone, it meant that one had stepped into the Sinkhole Graveyard. There was no turning back!
Elder Gold had always been dismissive of this rumor. An existence like him only believed in what he saw.
But now, this huge tombstone was right in front of him. Elder Gold was on high alert.
With a twist of his hands, he turned 180 degrees to the side.
The Divine Realm, which had been moving at high speed, turned around and flew in the other direction.
This time, Elder Gold didn't enter the chaos clouds. His vision was too poor. He had to ensure that he kept away from the tombstone.
However, not long after he followed the chaotic clouds, his face turned ghastly pale. The tombstone had appeared in another direction!
"Damn it!"
An ominous premonition rose in Elder Gold's heart.
After he took over the position of captain, the entire Divine Realm was under his control.
If he really fell into the Sinkhole Graveyard, it would be troublesome. How could he face Chieftain Chiyou if he buried the entire Divine Realm?
Elder Gold suddenly waved his hands. The bow of the ship rose at an almost vertical angle into the chaos. But not long after, the same scene appeared. The huge tombstone was waiting for him.
"I can't get out …"
Realizing this, Elder Jin clenched his teeth. This time, he flew towards the tombstone in the opposite direction.
After he passed by the tombstone, another huge tombstone appeared not far away.
These tombstones, which were the size of the Divine Realm, could be seen everywhere. However, these tombstones looked very dilapidated, as if they had been here for trillions of years and had turned into ruins.
"The Sinkhole Graveyard is indeed similar to the rumors …"
After traveling a distance, Elder Gold realized that he couldn't go any further. He needed to discuss with them.
He withdrew from the rings, picked up the Azure Parasol, and left this place. After drilling out of the sun, he rushed to the Sea of Time with a greater teleportation.
In the center of the Sea of Time, there was an extremely beautiful scenery on the largest floating island. In the palace beside the scenery, the atmosphere was stiff and solemn.
Many Saints were gathered in one place, while Luo Shui was high above with Ling Shu by his side.

Elder Jin nodded. "Such a perilous place is widely known in the Mother World. Almost everyone who goes out to explore the Chaos has heard of it. It shouldn't be a baseless rumor."
"Then wouldn't the Deity Realm be finished?"
"This is our only resting place!"
"I wonder what kind of danger lies in this land of peril for it to have such a vicious reputation?"
The Sages discussed fervently.
These Sages' family properties were all here, and they were all in the same boat. If one of them suffered, all of them would suffer. Now, all of them were especially anxious.
"Elder Jin, since you said that you can't return once you see the tombstone, then who named the Sinkhole Cemetery? The person who named it must have been here before. How did he return to the Mother World? "Ling Shu stood beside her sister and smiled.
Elder Jin cupped his hands at Ling Shu. "That's why I didn't really believe this rumor. Perhaps we can find a way back, but in this situation, I'll have to ask the two of you to make a decision."
In terms of foresight and judgment, Elder Jin was the leader in the entire Deity Realm.
However, when Elder Jin was uncertain, he could only let Luo Shui and Ling Shu make the decision. The two sisters were the most authentic descendants of the Chiyou clan.
Li Luo Shui and Luo Xiao looked at each other, and Luo Xiao suggested, "Why don't we send people out to investigate the Deity Realm?"
Although the aura of the Chaos was terrifying, there were still many people who could travel through the Chaos. These Saints and even Nirvana Realm experts could do it.
"That's feasible." Fu Er nodded at the side of the round table.
Elder Jin agreed. "Let's stop the Deity Realm for now and let us explore it."
"Elder Jin should stay in the Deity Realm. Leave the exploration to us," Luo Xiao said.
Compared to the powerful existences in the Chaos, the Deity Realm was still too weak. Luo Xiao's suggestion made sense. They should try not to risk Elder Jin.
"But husband, you …" Luo Shui was a little hesitant. She did not want Luo Xiao to take the risk.
Unexpectedly, Luo Xiao chuckled and said, "We Saints have built a place of refuge. It's not a big deal if we die outside. I'll lead the team out to explore!"
The place of refuge was not a foolproof guarantee. Luo Shui was very clear about this, but in front of so many people, she could not say much. In the end, she could only agree.
Not long after, Luo Xiao, Elder Jiu, Shi Qiaoqiao, Chen Huang Yijian, and the other Saints escaped from the Deity Realm. Fu Er was the only Chiyou Guard who went out with them.
Standing on the outer wall of the Deity Realm, when they saw the incomparably huge tombstone, everyone's eyes widened.
"Even a tombstone is so big. Who was buried here?" A Saint sighed.
"There's only a tombstone but no grave. This area is very strange," Chen Huang Yijian said.
Fu Er stared at the tombstone and said cautiously, "There are many inconceivable things in the Chaos Realm. Everyone should be extra careful."
"Everyone, follow me." As Luo Xiao spoke, he leapt up and began to travel through the Chaos Realm.
The others followed closely behind him, constantly traveling through the Chaos Realm.
The Deity Realm was not too far away from one of the tombstones, but in terms of the Chaos Realm, even the naked eye could see a very long distance. Even a Saint would have to spend a lot of time traveling.
A day and a half later, the Saints finally arrived in front of the tombstone.
The pitch-black tombstone floated alone in the air. This lonely, lifeless object gave off a strange aura, and its enormous appearance gave the Saints a great deal of pressure.
One side of the tombstone was covered with mottled cracks. The cracks on the tombstone were like an abyss.
After a Saint looked around, he approached one of the cracks.
Just as the Saint approached one of the cracks, a giant tongue stretched out from within the crack. This tongue contained countless barbs, each of which shone with a cold light.
Puff puff puff …
The giant tongue wrapped around the Saint, and the barbs easily pierced into the Saint's body, pulling him into the cracks on the tombstone.

Chapter 2989
Old Friends
Under the escort of those toads, Luo Xiao and the group of Sages entered a crack on the tombstone.
There was another world within the crevice, and it was wet everywhere.
On that black stone wall, there were enormous black toads, huge beetles that were hundreds of meters wide, and even centipedes that were thousands of meters long.
Facing such an environment, the group of Sages frowned. What exactly was this damned place inside the tombstone?
Those people wearing insect clothes did not speak. They simply led the way.
After the toads escorting the Sages entered the crack, they dispersed and returned to their lairs. They were not afraid that these Sages would escape.
"Brother Luo Xiao, what is this damned place?"
"I'm afraid we won't be able to return this time …"
The Sages were also afraid of death …
After Luo Zheng ruled the Divine Realm, the Sages had either died or reincarnated. The entire Divine Realm had entered a new era, and the rules were much fairer than before.
Those latecomers from the great clans had risen up. The current Divine Realm was a brand new era, and they were not willing to fall just like that.
"If you want to kill us, you can just do it outside the tombstone. Why bring us so deep into this place?" Luo Xiao calmly said.
The Sages understood this logic too. But they still felt uneasy in this dark environment.
After flying for more than a hundred kilometers in this region, they entered a vast primitive forest. Seeing those trees that were thousands of meters tall, some of the Sages even thought that they had arrived at the Waterfall Spiral Forest. The scenery here was indeed similar, just that it was darker and more obscure.
On top of an enormous tree deep within this primitive forest, Luo Xiao and the others finally saw some decent buildings.
After entering this building, those people wearing insect clothes ordered them to wait there.
About an incense's time later, a person stepped into the building.
At the moment this person stepped into the building, the group of Sages felt a huge pressure. Even the Paramita Realm Fu Er felt the same.
'This person is so strong. He should have already surpassed the Chaotic Origin Realm …' Fu Er thought.
Facing such an expert, Fu Er could not be the other party's opponent even if he used all his strength, much less the Sages present.
After this person entered the great hall of the building, he quickly walked over. He grabbed the arms of Luo Xiao and Sage Jiu and said excitedly, "Brother Luo Xiao, Brother Jiu, I trust you have been well since we last met!"
Luo Xiao and Saint Jiu were dumbfounded.
This was the first time they had come to this Sinkhole Cemetery. Everyone was already feeling very depressed and nervous. Now that someone had suddenly popped out to address them as brothers, it was obvious how they felt.
This person was also wearing a mask made of insect heads, so they couldn't see his face clearly. They were all a little stunned.
"After so many years, my two brothers can't even recognize my voice." The man laughed as he took off the mask on his head. Under the mask was the face of a handsome middle-aged man. His face was like jade, and he looked quite elegant.
"Kubei?"
"It's Northern Sage!"
"North Sage! It really is North Sage! "
Before Luo Xiao and Saint Jiu could speak, the other Saints present had already recognized him.
After all, Gu Bei was one of the top ranked aristocratic families. Even after disappearing for so many years, the Gu family's floating island still stood tall in front of them. It could be considered a special existence, and had left a deep impression on the other Saints back then.
Some of the Saints present had once followed Dongfang Chunjun, and they more or less had some grudges with Gu Bei back then.
However, these aristocratic Saints had already been punished by Luo Zheng, and were as docile as sheep in the divine realm. Now, they were all extremely attentive to Gu Bei, as if he was their long-lost brother.
Luo Xiao and Saint Jiu stared at Gu Bei for a long time.
After being separated for so long, Gu Bei's cultivation had also increased dramatically. His aura had changed completely, and for a moment, they didn't dare to recognize him.
However, after a while, the two of them hugged Gu Bei tightly.
"If not for your universe, we would have thought you were dead …" Luo Xiao said.
Through the Spiritual Lodging Grounds, one could determine the general location of the Spiritual Lodging Grounds. Although it was very vague, the direction was definitely not wrong.
Before Luo Zheng left the divine realm, Luo Xiao had asked him to try to find Gu Bei's whereabouts. However, through Gu Bei's universe, Luo Zheng could not find any clues.
Luo Shui and Elder Jin had also explained this matter. There were some special places that could render the Spiritual Lodging Grounds ineffective. For example, the Spiritual Lodging Grounds in the prison of the Primal Chaos Elder God was ineffective.
If Gu Bei was in such a place, it was true that he could not be found through the Spiritual Lodging Grounds.
The Ultimate Graveyard was such a dangerous place. The Saint of the Fang family had fallen because he was in the Ultimate Graveyard.
"I accidentally entered the Ultimate Graveyard, and I was constantly worried about Brother Luo Xiao's safety. However, I was unable to leave this place. As a brother, I am truly ashamed," Gu Bei said with his head lowered.
Back then, it was useless for Gu Bei to stay in the divine realm. He could not fight against Dongfang Chunjun. If Dongfang Chunjun deliberately targeted him, the Gu family would probably end up like the Luo family. His departure was the right choice.
"Brother Bei is not in the divine realm, but it is better than being there. The immortal mansion you left behind back then was enough for me to break out of my prison," Luo Xiao said with a smile.
Luo Xiao's arrangement in the universe had not only nurtured Luo Zheng, but had also turned the Universal War around. All of this could not be separated from the immortal mansion Gu Bei had left behind …
The Saints calmed down and listened to Luo Xiao, Saint Jiu, and Gu Bei reminisce.
The Saints also faintly heard about the process of Luo Zheng's growth, how he had won the Ultimate Universe in the Universal War, and how Luo Zheng had risen step by step in the divine realm.
These Saints had originally thought that Luo Zheng had been very lucky. They had not expected that there would be so many twists and turns in the process. Hearing about it, they clicked their tongues in wonder.
After listening to Luo Xiao's narration, a strange light flashed in Gu Bei's eyes. He then shook his head and said, "From what Brother Luo Xiao has said, the greatest credit does not come from me, but from Brother Luo Xiao's good son."
"He is also your good disciple, haha …" Luo Xiao laughed heartily.
They had been master and disciple for so many years, but they had never met. It was a strange thing.
After giving a rough account of what had happened in the divine realm, Luo Xiao asked again, "I wonder where this Ultimate Cemetery is? Could it be that once you enter this place, you cannot leave? "
Hearing Luo Xiao's question, Gu Bei's eyes were full of deep thought. "The Ultimate Cemetery is incomparably dangerous in the eyes of outsiders, but in reality, this is another place of origin of the chaos. It is not as frightening as you imagine. As for leaving, there is a way, but not now …"
"I would like to hear the details," Luo Xiao said with cupped hands.
The Saints had left the divine realm to explore the Ultimate Cemetery. To be able to meet Luo Xiao here was a great fortune.

Chapter 2990

Master's Return

After Luo Zheng left, the Divine Realm was in a frenzy to comprehend the True Meaning of Dao.
Many Perfect True Gods, Quasi-Saints, and Sages were bitterly cultivating the True Meaning of Dao. However, directly comprehending the True Meaning of Dao was too difficult. If one did not have an extremely high compatibility, they would often not even be able to touch the threshold. If they could obtain the support of the Ultimate Cemetery, it was something that the wealthy families could only wish for.
These wealthy families did not care at all about where the Divine Realm went. It was fine as long as the Divine Realm was not destroyed.
Gu Bei smiled faintly and said, "Of course there will be. I can guarantee that."
Now that Gu Bei was considered an important person in the Ultimate Graveyard, he could easily give him some cultivation resources.
After discussing the Divine Realm's current situation, Gu Bei stared at Luo Xiao and asked, "How is Shi Qiaoqiao now? Has she come out of the Immortal Abode? "
Shi Qiaoqiao had been locked in the Immortal Abode for a long time. Her personality was too strong.
Back then, Gu Bei had done it out of helplessness. Every year, Gu Bei would feel guilty. Now, he was naturally quite concerned.
Saint Jiu revealed a meaningful smile. "Back then, when I went to the Soul Wilderness to ask her to help, she made a request. She wanted us to find you. Hehe, now we've fulfilled our promise back then! Fortunately, we didn't disappoint you! "
Hearing Saint Jiu's words, Gu Bei's eyes revealed a hint of excitement. He then said, "I'll follow you all back to the Divine Realm!"
He led the way in front, and the other Sages followed closely behind.
After coming out of the huge crack, they headed straight for the Divine Realm.
Gu Bei was originally born in the Divine Realm. Naturally, he would not be obstructed by the Divine Realm's Heavenly Mirror Shield. The instant he entered, he used greater teleportation and headed straight for the floating island in the Sea of Time.
Now, the floating island in the Sea of Time had changed greatly. Only the Gu Family, the Luo Family, and a few other wealthy families were floating on the Sea of Time. Other than that, there was the huge floating island in the center.
This time, the Divine Realm had mistakenly entered the Ultimate Cemetery, alarming all the experts in the Divine Realm.
Yu Taibai, Jing Wuhuan, Yu Shenfeng, Shi Qiaoqiao, and the others were waiting on the floating island for news from the outside world.
The Sages had just left not long ago, and one of the Divine Realm's universes had already collapsed. This Ultimate Cemetery was too strange.

Chapter 2991

Chi Yi requested an audience.
When Gu Bei entered the divine territory, he had already taken off his insect clothing and changed into a set of green robes, returning to his usual appearance.
When he was young, he had been extremely handsome. Now that his cultivation had improved, he looked even more elegant with the support of his aura.
Even Luo Xiao and Saint Jiu found it hard to recognize him, let alone Jing Wuhuan and Yu Shenfeng.

"It's Master," Jing Wuhuan said.
Although he was very sure, he did not dare to go up and recognize him.
The strongest people they had seen in the divine territory were Elder Jin and Ming Wei.
However, Gu Bei's aura far surpassed theirs!
The limit of cultivation in the divine territory was the great perfection stage. The path to becoming a Saint was extremely difficult, and the path to the other shore was one in ten thousand.
So many Saints had not been able to break through easily. Gu Bei's step was like a leap in quality. He was like heaven and earth compared to the Saints in the divine territory!
Gu Bei landed on the floating island and looked at his two beloved disciples for a while. A gentle smile appeared on his face as he called out, "Wuhuan, Shenfeng."
At this time, Yu Shenfeng and Jing Wuhuan were finally sure that this person with unfathomable cultivation was their Master!
"Master!"

At that moment, Kubei met eyes with someone in the crowd. It was Shi Xiaoqiao.

Buzz!

"Silence!"
In the past, when Shi Xiaoqiao used the Silence Scepter's power, it was directed at the entire Divine Realm. But now, she was directing all the power toward Kubei alone!
Even though Kubei was very strong now, the power in his body was forcibly sealed by the Silence Scepter!
Boom!

Master's current cultivation was beyond their imagination. With Shi Xiaoqiao's strength, it would be difficult to shake him.
But Shi Xiaoqiao was indeed using all her strength. Although Jing Wuhuan said that, his eyes trembled when he heard the sounds of the attacks.
At this rate, even if Master didn't die, he would be crippled, right?
Luo Xiao and Jiu Sheng couldn't stand by and watch. Even if Kubei had wronged Shi Xiaoqiao, they couldn't let Shi Xiaoqiao kill him.

Shi Xiaoqiao ignored him. If she didn't kill Kubei, she wouldn't be able to get rid of the hatred in her heart.
Luo Xiao and Jiu Sheng were about to step forward and stop her when Kubei, who had been beaten until he was rolling around, gave them a look.
Both of their faces froze.
It seemed that Kubei's strength was above their expectations. If Luo Xiao and Jiu Sheng were in Shi Xiaoqiao's place, they would probably die if they didn't put up any defenses. But Kubei seemed to be completely fine. How could his physical body be so strong?
Kubei let Shi Xiaoqiao beat him for a long time. Towards the end, the Silence Scepter's power dissipated, but he still didn't fight back.
It was only when Shi Xiaoqiao was completely exhausted that Gu Bei pulled her into his arms.
Shi Xiaoqiao buried her face in his chest and began to cry.
Kubei couldn't comfort her in front of so many people, so he said to Luo Xiao, "Brother Xiao, I'll leave for now."
Then, he waved his hand lightly and teleported away with Shi Xiaoqiao …
"Master is truly Master! Master's wife beat him up for so long, but he didn't get hurt at all! "Yu Shenfeng said proudly. This was enough for him to brag about for a while.
Four hours later, Kubei returned to the floating island in the center.
Shi Xiaoqiao followed behind him. Her emotions had calmed down, and her every move carried a trace of sweetness.
Not long after the Saints returned, Elder Jin also hurried over and learned about the situation in the Gui Cemetery from Fu Er.
As for the future of the divine realm, they still needed to discuss it in detail. At the very least, they needed to find a way to integrate it.

After Luo Zheng successfully fused with the Paramita token, the Jade Spirit remained in his inner world.
After Luo Nian formally became his disciple, Luo Zheng even opened up a world for them. Only Luo Nian and the Jade Spirit could enter that world at will, free from outside disturbances.
After leaving his inner world, Qiu Yinhe had been waiting in the courtyard for a long time.
When he was trapped on the other side, Qiu Yinhe and the others were also quite worried.
"Disciple met with danger in the other side and made Senior Qiu worry. I'm really sorry," Luo Zheng said to Qiu Yinhe with cupped hands.
Entering the hidden realm was completely his and Ling Shuang's idea on the spur of the moment. They had never discussed it with Qiu Yinhe, and now it seemed that it was indeed a bit rash.
Qiu Yinhe had more or less understood the situation through Concubine Yan. He only heard him say, "There are dangers everywhere in the other side, and you can only rely on yourself in many situations. Kid, you went to the hidden realm and returned safely. You really are blessed by the heavens!"
"Senior Qiu, you flatter me," Luo Zheng said with a smile.

"Disciple will remember," Luo Zheng replied.
