"What is it?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Someone called Chi Yi is looking for you," Qiu Yinhe said.

Chapter 2992
Li Lu
Chi Yi returned to the mother world, on one hand to escort Luo Zheng, on the other hand to find the old Jiuli subordinates.
In fact, Elder Jin did not deliberately assign a task to Chi Yi.
The Jiuli people had left the mother world for too long, and after experiencing the tribulation at the bottom of the chaos, they just wanted to live peacefully.
If he was willing to follow Ming Wei to Li Mountain, he could also live a peaceful life in the Nuwa Clan.
But Chi Yi did not do that. He went to the Seven Plains to search for the clues left behind by the Jiuli people.
Hard work pays off, he really found a clue.
Following that clue, he really found the survivors of the Jiuli people!
When Luo Zheng heard Qiu Yinhe mention Chi Yi, his expression changed slightly, "May I ask Senior Qiu, where is this person?"
Qiu Yinhe looked at Luo Zheng's anxious expression and smiled, "Follow me."
Then Luo Zheng stepped onto Qiu Yinhe's giant sword, flew to the Li Sword Sect, went around the back of Greater One Mountain, and landed at the foot of the north side of Greater One Mountain.
There was a Splendid Palace built here, which was used by Greater One Mountain to receive guests.
Luo Zheng hurriedly entered the Splendid Palace and saw more than ten people sitting in the palace, and Chi Yi was among them!
"Brother Chi Yi!"
"Brother Luo Zheng!"
After rushing into the mother world with Ming Wei, and being separated from Luo Zheng, Chi Yi was disheartened. He was pleasantly surprised to see Luo Zheng again.
Chi Yi originally did not know Luo Zheng's whereabouts, but after Luo Yan led the people of Li Mountain to search, he learned that Luo Zheng was in Greater One Heavenly Palace.
Greater One Heavenly Palace was the orthodox Human Race, if Luo Zheng could stand firm there, it would be a good choice.
After Chi Yi returned to the remnants of the Jiuli tribe, he reported this matter to Li Lu, but there was no good opportunity, so he did not go.
Until the series of events at the Temple of Heaven, the remnants of the Jiuli tribe made the decision to take a risk and go to Greater One Heavenly Palace.
"Brother Luo Zheng's cultivation has probably reached the Thirteenth Heavenly Layer in such a short time. I'm really ashamed," Chi Yi looked at him with a surprised expression.
When Luo Zheng entered the Mother World, he had just entered the Sea of True Meaning. Whether it was strength or cultivation, he was far inferior to Chi Yi. Now, he had surpassed Chi Yi by a lot.
"It's fate," Luo Zheng said modestly.
"Come, come and greet Chief!" Chi Yi pulled Luo Zheng to the rear.
Behind them, a tall and sturdy old man stood on the spot.
The old man's aura was restrained and his face was pale. He seemed to be seriously ill and was no longer as brave as he was in the past. However, his large eyes were shining brightly. Anyone who met his gaze would feel pressured.
"Chief?" Luo Zheng looked confused.
According to Luo Zheng's understanding, there was only one chief in the Jiuli tribe, and that was Chi You.
Chi Yi said, "The Jiuli tribe is a union of nine tribes. Besides the Great Chief, there are eight other chiefs. They are all brothers of the Great Chief. You should call him Third Grandpa."
Chi You was the head of the nine chiefs. The second in command was Li Ju. Today, the one who came with Chi Yi was the third in command, Li Lu.
Since Luo Zheng came here, Li Lu's eyes had been fixed on Luo Zheng.
After Chi You was defeated, there was no chance for him to make a comeback. The God Domain was just a place for his big brother to continue the bloodline of the Jiuli tribe.
When the chaos was destroyed, the God Domain would also be destroyed. The Jiuli tribe would disappear forever in the river of time.
Li Lu didn't expect that someone from the Jiuli tribe would return to the Mother World in the God Domain.
"Third Grandpa," Luo Zheng called.
Qiu Yinhe already knew Luo Zheng's background, and Chi Yi was not an outsider. Luo Zheng didn't plan to hide his identity in front of these people.
"Luo Shui was so young when she left. In the blink of an eye, her son is already so big." Li Lu's face was full of relief.
Luo Zheng nodded and said, "Mother also misses you in the God Domain."
"Let me introduce you. This is my grandson, Huo Ze. This is my granddaughter, Lan Qing. I wonder … have you met them before?" A man and a woman walked out from behind Li Lu.
The man was wearing a beast robe made of golden silk. His eyes were as sharp as knives. The woman was wearing a silk robe. She looked petite and cute. When she smiled, two sweet dimples appeared. She looked very approachable.
Li Lu introduced Lan Qing and Huo Ze as soon as he came up. In fact, there was a hidden meaning in his words.
Because of Chi Yi, the old tribes of the Jiuli tribe already knew of Luo Zheng's existence. They even knew that he was in the Heavenly Palace, but they never came.
At that time, Luo Zheng's strength was too weak. If he had outstanding talent, it would have been better for him to stay in the Heavenly Palace. After all, the Jiuli tribe was already so dilapidated.
But Huo Ze and the others' trip to the Hidden Domain made Li Lu unable to sit still.
Huo Ze told Li Lu that when he was exploring the Temple of Sky and the Hidden Domain, he met a physical body with the bloodline of the Jiuli tribe. It was said that this person was inextricably linked to the Heavenly Palace.
This clue was mixed with the news brought by Chi Yi. The result was that it pointed to Luo Zheng.
Li Lu took a huge risk to leave once. His purpose was to verify this.
Luo Zheng glanced at Lan Qing. At the same time, he lowered his voice and said, "Of course we have met. Not only have we met, but we have also gone through hardships together, right?"
"It's Brother Tianxing!"
Lan Qing had been secretly sizing up Luo Zheng. On one hand, she had never seen Luo Zheng's real face. On the other hand, there was something wrong with his voice. That "Tianxing's" voice was deeper.
Now that she heard Luo Zheng's voice, she was a hundred percent sure.
"I asked you to come and find me. I didn't expect it to be so soon." Luo Zheng smiled and said, "Everyone here is not an outsider. You don't have to call me Tianxing. My name is Luo Zheng!"
Huo Ze also stepped forward and bowed to Luo Zheng. "When we parted at the Hidden Domain, I thought that Brother Luo Zheng would never return. Huo Ze is also very happy to see that you have safely left the other side."
They were also very interested in what Luo Zheng encountered later. But those things involved Feng Ge and the pure ones. This was considered a secret of the Heavenly Palace. Luo Zheng did not say much.
To the Jiuli tribe, this meeting confirmed their guess. Tianxing was Luo Zheng, and Luo Zheng came from the Divine Realm.
After returning to the mother world for so long, this was the first time Luo Zheng met his own tribe.
After some questioning, Luo Zheng also understood the current situation of the Jiuli tribe.
Among the nine tribe chiefs of the Jiuli tribe, only Li Lu and Li Wu were in charge. The other brothers were either dead or scattered.
The troublesome thing was that the You Xiong tribe had been secretly investigating the Jiuli tribe. As long as they found a trace of their bloodline, they would kill them on the spot. Therefore, every time the Jiuli tribe members went out, they had to take a huge risk.
After hearing what the You Xiong tribe did, Luo Zheng was also very angry. In comparison, he treated the You Xiong tribe too gently.

Chapter 2993
Promise
Luo Zheng was not surprised by Li Lu's request.
He came from the God Realm with a huge amount of God Crystals. These were resources that originally belonged to the Nine Li Tribe.
More importantly, Chi You had other instructions for Luo Zheng. Even though these instructions were given to 9527, the Nine Li Tribe had lost all hope.
The remnants of the Jiuli Tribe had almost lost all hope. The appearance of Luo Zheng brought them a new ray of hope.
Qiu Yinhe's eyes flashed slightly. His expression turned complicated.
In all fairness, the Great Yi Heaven Palace did not invest much in Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng had climbed up the mountain step by step in the Heaven Palace. He had relied on his own efforts.
His arrival did not consume too much of the Heaven Palace's resources. Instead, he had brought many benefits to the Heaven Palace.
If Luo Zheng chose to leave, Qiu Yinhe had no reason to stop him. But before Luo Zheng could speak, he cupped his fists towards Li Lu and said, "Old Li, can you listen to me?"
Li Lu indifferently sized up Qiu Yinhe before saying, "Please speak."
Li Lu was once an indomitable figure in the Mother World. His status was equivalent to the Mountain Lord of the Seven Mountains of the Heaven Palace. He was not someone Qiu Yinhe could compare with.
But as time passed, the current Nine Li Tribe was left with these remnants. Their influence was negligible. And Li Lu's soul and fleshly body were heavily injured. He had yet to recover and was no longer as glorious as he was in the past.
"After Luo Zheng entered my Heaven Palace, he improved by leaps and bounds. I've heard him talk about reviving the Nine Li Tribe many times. He is very eager. As a descendant of the Nine Li Tribe, I also feel fortunate for the Nine Li Tribe. In the future, he will definitely become a figure who can command a region." Qiu Yinhe changed the topic, "Old Li should be clearer than me about the current situation of the Nine Li Tribe. It's fine if Luo Zheng returns to the Nine Li Tribe. But how will the Nine Li Tribe nurture Luo Zheng? Let's not talk about the future. If the Chaotic Origin Great World opens, does the Nine Li Tribe have the ticket to safely bring him into the Chaotic Origin Great World? "
The Nine Li Clan used to be such a great force. Even though it had fallen apart, it still couldn't be underestimated. There should be more than one Nine Li Clan remnants like Li Lu. Therefore, You Xiong Family had been chasing after the remnants of the Nine Li Clan.
Because of this, the remnants of Jiuli could only hide in the world. They were unable to expand or accumulate resources. No matter whether they were in the Nirvana Realm or in the world, they had to be careful.
Just talking about the Chaotic Origin Great World, it was hard for the Nine Li Tribes to participate in it openly.
Lan Qing and Huo Ze looked at each other silently. They were indeed worried about the tickets.
Being the outstanding talents of the younger generation of the Nine Li, it was not a problem for them to cross the 13 Heavens of the Colored Realm. But if they wanted to step into the Chaotic Origin Realm, it was useless to improve their soul. The Chaotic Origin Great World was a threshold they had to cross!
Facing the series of questions thrown out by Qiu Yinhe, that old face of Li Lu revealed a dim look.
Qiu Yinhe could not bear to see that. After all, Li Lu was a senior worthy of his respect. Now, he was just a tiger that had fallen from grace. He then said: "It is indeed not the right time for Luo Zheng to return to the Nine Li Clan. If Luo Zheng could take on the responsibility of the entire Nine Li Clan in the future, I would naturally not have any objections. Of course, when Luo Zheng entered the Heaven Palace, I've told him before that the Heaven Palace would not stop him if he wanted to leave …"
"As you said, it is not realistic for Zheng 'er to return to the Nine Li Clan. Furthermore, I, Li Lu, can't represent the entire Nine Li Clan. Luo Zheng can stay in the Heaven Palace on one condition." Li Lu's originally dim face suddenly revealed a cunning smile.
Qiu Yinhe said with a straight face: "Elder Li, please speak."
"Could I ask the Heaven Palace to take care of the few clansmen I brought this time and bring them into the Chaotic Origin Great World?" Li Lu chuckled.
Luo Zheng and Qiu Yinhe were stunned for a moment.
The two of them did not expect that Li Lu would have such a plan.
Before Qiu Yinhe could speak, Li Lu sighed, "Ai, we have no other choice now. Even if we could get the entrance ticket, it would be hard for us to enter the Chaotic Origin Great World. If we could borrow the name of the Heaven Palace to enter, that would be our fortune …"
Luo Zheng immediately said to Qiu Yinhe: "Senior Qiu, I hope that Senior Qiu can report to the Heaven Palace to allow my Nine Li clansmen to enter. The entrance ticket to the Chaotic Origin Great World is extremely precious. I will bear the cost of it!"
If the Heaven Palace could not get the entrance ticket, Luo Zheng intended to buy it from the Ear Mouse Clan. He believed that the Ear Mice who were willing to sell anything would not reject his request.
Qiu Yinhe only regained his senses after a while. He sighed in his heart that the older the ginger, the spicier it was. Li Lu knew that the remnant of the Nine Li Tribe could not nurture Luo Zheng. Hence, he did not intend to bring him back. He personally came to the Heaven Palace for two reasons. On one hand, it was to meet Luo Zheng. On the other hand, it was likely that he wanted to squeeze his clansmen into the Heaven Palace. Otherwise, there was no need for him to bring these juniors of the Nine Li Tribe!
Under the gaze of the crowd, Qiu Yinhe revealed a bitter smile: "Even though it is hard to get the entrance ticket to the Chaotic Origin Great World, this Qiu can still get five or six extra slots. There is no need for Zheng 'er to worry about it."
Hearing Qiu Yinhe's reply, Lan Qing, Huo Ze, and the other Nine Li clansmen revealed smiles. They did not expect that the Heaven Palace would agree to it so easily.
Following that, Li Lu explained the situation of the remnant of the Nine Li Tribe so that Luo Zheng could have a rough understanding of it.
The situation of the remnant of the Nine Li Tribe was worse than what Luo Zheng had imagined. The important inheritances of the tribe had almost been destroyed. After the defeat with the You Xiong Clan, some clansmen had even used some forbidden secret techniques to cleanse their bloodline in order to break free from the Nine Li Tribe. Those alien clans that had once submitted to the Nine Li Tribe had even turned against them.
"Actually, our Nine Li Tribe had already lost all hope. When Chi Yi tried his best to find us, I had once reprimanded him. The Divine Realm was set free by Big Brother with great difficulty. Why should he go through so much trouble to return? He shouldn't have sent you back to the Mother World, "Li Lu said in shame." But I didn't expect Luo Shui to have such an outstanding son. It's a pity that Third Grandfather has nothing and can't help you. "
Luo Zheng's performance in the Paramita Realm had been transmitted to Li Lu through Lan Qing and Huo Ze, igniting a strand of hope in Li Lu's heart. Now that he had confirmed Luo Zheng's identity, this flame of hope burned even more fiercely.
After Li Lu finished speaking, he cupped his hands and bade farewell to Qiu Yinhe. After he stood up, Chi Yi and the others also left with him.
Luo Zheng stood in the same place and clenched his fists tightly. In the Divine Realm, he had seen the statues of the Nine Tribal Chiefs. Every one of them was powerful and imposing. Looking at Li Lu's old figure, it was poles apart from the statues in the Divine Realm. They did not look like the same person at all.
The real Nine Li Tribe had actually fallen to such a state. He felt sad and suddenly shouted:
"Third Grandpa."
Li Lu and the others turned their heads and smiled at Luo Zheng.
"In your lifetime, I will make the Nine Li Tribe rise again," Luo Zheng said seriously.
Li Lu laughed and said: "Don't let the old man wait too long. I hope to see it in my lifetime!"
